# OddsIntel — Master Priority Queue

> **✅ SQL-PERCENT-PIPELINE-BREAK + SHADOW-SILENT-FAILURE Done 2026-09-03** — **A comment I added broke the betting pipeline for ~16 hours, and only half the system said so.** The `STALE-BEST-ODDS` rationale block I put inside the `odds_raw` SQL literal in `_load_today_from_db` quoted measurements containing per-cent signs. psycopg2 reads a bare one as a placeholder, so the query raised `IndexError: tuple index out of range` from **2026-09-02 14:57 UTC to 2026-09-03 07:20**. This is ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #6, documented for months, which I had cited earlier in the same session — and my first fix reintroduced it **inside the sentence warning about it**. Comment now contains no per-cent character; verified by running `_load_today_from_db` for real (99 matches, 42 AF-only, 119 predictions), not by inspection. Smoke `SQL-PERCENT-GUARD` scans every SQL literal under `workers/` for a bare per-cent sign in a comment line, scoped to literals that contain a DML keyword AND reference a table so it does not fire on docstrings citing percentages. **The more important half — SHADOW-SILENT-FAILURE:** `betting_pipeline` re-raises, so it logged four loud failures and was fixed within the hour of being reported. `_shadow_run` caught everything and returned normally, so `_run_job` recorded **status='completed'** for every shadow cohort across the same 16 hours while writing nothing — `shadow_bets` fell from **3,269 rows on 09-02 to 7 on 09-03** and no surface said a word. Identical to EPICBET-403 (277 green runs, zero rows). `_shadow_run` now raises; `_run_job` already isolates jobs from one another so this costs no isolation. Smoke `SHADOW-SILENT-FAILURE` pins it, verified to fail when the swallow is restored. **Recovery confirmed:** the 07:35 betting_pipeline completed in 147s (previously failing in 0s) and the 07:40 shadow cohort took shadow_bets from 7 to 46. **Standing lesson: the job that told the truth got fixed in an hour; the one that lied cost 16.**


> **⬜ DB-RETENTION-POLICY Filed 2026-09-03 (P2 — 39.6 GB and growing)** — 3-4h — **ROOT CAUSE FOUND 2026-09-03, and for `match_signals` it is duplication rather than retention.** **Exact counts:** 49,344,722 rows, 100 signal names, 63,401 matches, and only **1,559,496 distinct (match, signal) pairs — i.e. 31.6 copies of every pair**, 130 on the busiest match. (An earlier estimate in this entry said ~8x; that divided rows/match by all 100 signal names, but a match carries only ~24.6 of them on average, so the real multiple is four times worse.) Sampled directly: one fixture carried 8,339 signal rows, with `elo_away`, `away_team_turf_games_ytd`, `bookmaker_count_active` and `ah_bookmaker_disagreement` each written **130 times** between 2026-08-31 04:03 and 2026-09-01 19:40. That is every pipeline run (30-min refresh plus the shadow cohorts) rewriting the **entire** signal set, including features that cannot change — a team's turf games YTD and its ELO on the day do not need 130 rows. **Deduping to one row per (match, signal) takes 49.3M rows to 1.56M — 3% of current, roughly 13 GB down to ~0.4 GB — without deleting a single distinct fact.** That is the single largest storage win available anywhere in the system. The right split is: write static/prematch-fixed signals once, and keep a time series only for the genuinely market-varying ones (`bookmaker_disagreement`, `bookmaker_count_active`, price-derived signals). Do that before writing any age-based retention for this table, because deletion would throw away history that deduplication keeps for free. `odds_snapshots` is different and genuinely a retention question: 67,125,099 rows over the 48,908 matches that have odds = **1,372 rows per match**, across **41 bookmakers and 74 markets**, and 5,197,765 distinct (match, book, market, selection) lines each holding **~13 observations** over time. Every row is a distinct price observation, so dedup does nothing here and only age-based retention helps. Worth a separate look: 41 bookmakers is far more than the 7 in `ACCESSIBLE_BOOKMAKERS`, so a large share of this table is prices at books we will never bet. — Sizing done while auditing in-play. **The database is 39.55 GB**, up from 18 MB right after SUPABASE-TO-VPS in July — it is `odds_snapshots` (20 GB / 55% / 67.0M rows) and `match_signals` (13 GB / 35% / 49.3M rows at **778 rows per match**) and essentially nothing else; the top 18 tables are 99.7%. **Retention is the only lever that matters**: both tables are ~47% under 30 days old, so a 30-day policy frees **~17 GB (DB → ~22 GB)** and a 60-day one ~10 GB (→ ~30 GB). `scripts/prune_odds_snapshots.py` only prunes `status='finished'` matches older than 30 days, which is why scheduled fixtures keep their full history and why the table is this size. `match_signals` appears to have no pruning at all — establish whether 778 rows/match is repeated capture of the same signals (in which case dedupe beats deletion) before writing a policy. **Also ~0.6 GB of pure bloat** in four zero-row tables reclaimable by `VACUUM FULL`: `match_events` 453 MB, `scratch_pit_odds_3h` 80 MB, `odds_snapshots_quarantined` 54 MB, `matches_dupe_quarantined` 14 MB. Note the constraint before deleting anything: model training and the backtests read this history, so pick the window from what training actually needs, not from what fits a hosting tier.


> **✅ SHADOW-VIEW-DUPLICATE Done 2026-09-02** — Self-inflicted, found and reverted the same day. Migration 290 created `shadow_bets_deduped` to fix the shadow-bots page truncation. The truncation fix was correct and stands; the **new view was redundant** — `shadow_bets_unique` already existed (migrations 282/283) with identical semantics, and ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS **#5 says plainly "use the shadow_bets_unique view" and ends with "one definition beats two"**. Verified the two were equivalent before reverting: **0 rows differed** out of 13,955. Migration 293 drops the duplicate; both shadow-bot pages and the smoke test now read `shadow_bets_unique`. Gotcha #5 updated to record the episode so the next person checks before adding a third. **Lesson worth keeping: I read gotcha #18's title without its body and #5 not at all, and produced one false P1 plus one redundant view in the same hour — the gotchas file only works if it is read before, not after.**


> **⛔ BTTS-DUAL-LEDGER-VIOLATION — WITHDRAWN 2026-09-02, same day it was filed. Not a bug.** I filed this claiming `bot_btts_all` writing to both `simulated_bets` (214 rows) and `shadow_bets` (1,996) contradicted ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #18. It does not. #18's **body** says explicitly: *"Pipeline bots ALSO re-record into `shadow_bets` as part of the timing-cohort experiment, so a union double-counts"* — the dual write is designed behaviour and was already documented. Verified at scale: **27 of 41 bots write to both**, concurrently. The fault was #18's **title** ("never both"), which contradicted its own body; I acted on the title without reading the body. **Title corrected** to "Pick ONE ledger per bot — do not union them" so the next reader cannot repeat it. Nothing to fix in the data.

> **🔄 BTTS-PLATT-NOT-APPLIED In Progress (claimed 2026-09-02) · filed 2026-09-02 (P1 — model calibration silently skipped)** — 2-3h — Found in the queue sweep while verifying BTTS-CHINA-CALIBRATION. **`calibrated_prob` is identical to `model_probability` on all 55 deduped picks of `bot_sweep_btts_yes_v1`** — the BTTS Platt calibration is not being applied to that bot at all. The contrast is the proof it should be: on `simulated_bets`, BTTS rows show a **−8.1pp average Platt shift on 44 of 102 rows** since 2026-07-19. So the shadow BTTS bot is betting raw model probabilities while the production path shrinks them by 8pp — which is exactly the direction that would explain its over-confident China picks (0.736 model vs 0.500 market). This is the mechanism BTTS-CHINA-CALIBRATION was filed to explain; fix this first and re-measure that ticket after.

> **⬜ PINNACLE-BTTS-ZERO-COVERAGE Filed 2026-09-02 (P2 — a whole market has no sharp anchor)** — 1h to decide — **Zero Pinnacle BTTS rows exist: 0 of 3,076,350 BTTS snapshots since 2026-08-01.** API-Football's Pinnacle feed carries only 8 bet types and Both Teams To Score is not one of them. Consequences: `clv_pinnacle` is permanently NULL for every BTTS bet, so the CLV-anchored promotion gate (`CLV_MIN_N=100` on de-vigged Pinnacle CLV) can never fire for a BTTS bot, and BOT-GATE-OU-BTTS cannot adjudicate the BTTS half on the evidence it wants. **Decide** whether BTTS bots are judged on a different anchor (soft-book consensus median, as ODDS-OUTLIER-FILTER already does for 1x2/BTTS/DC) or excluded from CLV-gated promotion entirely and held to ROI with a much larger n. Do not leave it implicit — today a BTTS bot silently sits outside the promotion machinery.


> **⬜ ALL-BETS-CEILING-DEAD Filed 2026-09-02 (P2 — silent-truncation trap)** — 1h — Found during the queue sweep. `odds-intel-web/src/lib/engine-data.ts` `getAllBets()` fetches with `.range(0, 19999)` and warns above `ALL_BETS_CEILING = 20000`. **That warning can never fire**, because PostgREST's `db-max-rows` is **10,000** — the response is capped at 10k and the guard sits at 20k, so the page will silently aggregate half the ledger the moment `simulated_bets` passes 10,000 rows. It is at ~4.7k today, so this is not yet live; it is filed now because the failure is invisible when it arrives (a capped response is indistinguishable from a complete one — the exact shape of SHADOW-BOTS-DETAIL-TRUNCATION and COMP-FALLBACK-DRIFT). Fix: paginate with `.range()` as the shadow-bots card now does, or lower the ceiling below `db-max-rows` so the guard actually trips. Related: PERF-PAGE-COLD-RENDER (same function).


> **⬜ PINNACLE-BEST-NO-EDGE Filed 2026-09-02 (P1 — real money, but underpowered on its own)** — 2-3h — Found while closing BET365-EXECUTION-AUDIT. Splitting production picks by which book held the best price, and pricing everything at `odds_at_pick_live` (so the STALE-BEST-ODDS inflation is out of the way): **when Pinnacle is top-of-market, live ROI is −14.82% (n=71, hit 29.6%); when a soft book is, +13.00% (n=485, hit 44.3%)** — a 27.8pp split. Gating out the Pinnacle-best cohort moves the whole book from **+9.45% to +13.00% (+3.55pp)** while dropping 13% of volume, and lifts the portfolio t-stat from **+1.64 to +2.11**. **Mechanism is sound a priori**, which is why this is worth pursuing rather than dismissing as noise: `recommended_bookmaker='Pinnacle'` means the *sharpest* book had the best price, i.e. no soft book was offering value at all. Our "edge" is then measured against a fair price, so there is nothing to take — we are betting into the sharp line. **Do NOT ship the gate on this alone**: the Pinnacle-only cohort is t=−0.93, so we cannot reject break-even on it, and BOT-GATE-OU-BTTS is already filed with an explicit *"DO NOT blanket-gate; evidence conflicts"* warning for exactly this failure mode. **Next:** (a) re-measure at n≥150 in the Pinnacle-best cohort, or (b) validate as a shadow gate first (raise picks, flag rather than suppress, compare after 4-6 weeks). Also check whether the effect survives conditioning on market and tier — it may be a proxy for something else, e.g. low-liquidity fixtures where only Pinnacle prices at all.


> **✅ SHADOW-BOTS-DETAIL-TRUNCATION Done 2026-09-02** (migration 290 + odds-intel-web + smoke) — Operator reported the shadow-bots dashboard card and the per-bot detail page disagreeing for the same bot: `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` read **+12.7% ROI on the card and −11.8% on its detail page**, and "none of the bots detail views match the dashboard". Both pages deduplicated in JS **after** fetching, so the row cap bit on RAW rows — and shadow bots write one row per (cohort × match × market × selection) with cohorts firing every 30 min, ~10 rows per pick. **Detail page** used `.limit(500)`: **24 of 41 bots truncated**, `bot_dc_value` showing **48 of its 3,081 picks (1.6%)**, `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` 61 of 304. **Card** had *no* `.limit()`, which is not the same as no limit — PostgREST caps at `db-max-rows`, **measured at 10,000 against 143,263 rows**, so the card aggregated **7%** of the ledger. A capped response is indistinguishable from a complete one, which is why this ran unnoticed; same silent-failure shape as EPICBET-403 and the COMP_FALLBACK rot. **Fix:** migration 290 adds `shadow_bets_deduped` (`DISTINCT ON (bot_id, match_id, market, selection)`, earliest `pick_time` — the same rule both pages had in JS), and both pages now read it, so they aggregate the same population by construction. The card paginates via `.range()` because the deduped set (13,955) still exceeds the cap. PostgREST embed of `matches`/`leagues`/`teams` verified working against the view **before** the pages were switched over. **Result for the reported bot: −11.8% → +13.7%, hit rate 40% → 47%, now matching the card's 47% exactly** (304 picks vs the card's 280 at screenshot time). Smoke `SHADOW-BOTS-DETAIL-TRUNCATION` pins the view's 4-column key, the earliest-pick_time rule, both pages reading the view and never the raw table, and the card's pagination; each verified to fail independently. **Note:** these are the numbers used to judge whether a shadow bot is worth following with real money ([[project_realmoney_shadow_signals]]) — `bot_dc_value` was being judged on 1.6% of its record.


> **✅ STALE-ODDS-HISTORY-RESTATE Done 2026-09-02** — 2-4h — `STALE-BEST-ODDS` is fixed going forward, but it is **not retroactive**, and `simulated_bets.pnl` is settled from `odds_at_pick`. So every historical bet priced by the affected pipelines carries an inflated price, and our published ROI is overstated by the same mechanism we just called out on Forebet (ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #27/#29/#30). **Measured:** on 488 settled bets over 90 days (1X2 + OU 2.5, non-inplay), ROI from stored `odds_at_pick` is **+9.47%** against **+4.43%** at the best price actually live at pick time — 47.5% of those bets record a price above anything live when they were raised. The landing publishes **+13.82%** for a different cohort, which has not yet been re-measured this way. **Decide:** (a) recompute a `odds_at_pick_live` column from `odds_snapshots` (latest-per-book at `pick_time`) and restate ROI on it, (b) restate only from the fix date forward and label the earlier window, or (c) publish both. Do NOT quietly leave the old number up — that is precisely the Forebet posture. Note the honest complication: the fix changes future ROI too, so a like-for-like before/after needs the same pricing rule on both sides. **Shipped:** migration 291 adds `odds_at_pick_live` to `simulated_bets` + `shadow_bets` — **additive; `odds_at_pick`, `pnl` and `bankroll_after` are never overwritten**, because the audit trail is the product, `bankroll_after` is a running total, and bot promotions were decided on those numbers. `scripts/backfill_odds_at_pick_live.py` fills it server-side in one pass (latest quote per book at or before `pick_time`, then max across `ACCESSIBLE_BOOKMAKERS` — imported, not copied, so it cannot drift). **Backfilled: simulated_bets 2,357/4,305 settled (54.8%), shadow_bets 115,855/139,612 (83.0%).** Six audit scripts each had their own copy of the cohort query, so the query moved to `scripts/_our_stats.py` — five of six would otherwise have kept publishing the inflated figure. **Published cohort restated: +15.99% → +10.65% (n=529, 76.3% coverage), −5.34pp**, with `roi_pct_recorded` retained alongside because repricing a record without showing the original claim is exactly what we fault Forebet for. Whole-table effect for reference: simulated_bets +0.83% → −4.69% (50.8% of settled bets record odds above anything live), shadow_bets −4.12% → −5.35%. Smoke `STALE-ODDS-HISTORY-RESTATE` pins the SQL selecting `odds_at_pick_live` (an earlier draft passed while the SELECT was gutted, because the docstring still said the words), that no audit re-defines a local `our_stats`, and that the published ledgers carry the restated shape with the recorded figure and coverage. **Standing: at +10.65% we are level with Betaminic (+10.60%) rather than ahead, and still clear of WinnerOdds (+7.67%), Forebet (−0.18%), SignalOdds (−6.44%), DeepBetting (−7.65%).**


> **⬜ BANKROLL-SHADOW-BLIND Filed 2026-09-02 (P2)** — 1-2h — `bots.current_bankroll` is only meaningful for bots that write `simulated_bets`. The shadow-only bots each carry `starting_bankroll=1` and therefore show `current_bankroll=1.00` with zero drift forever, while their actual settled P&L sits in `shadow_bets`: `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` **+€4,382** over 2,672 settled, `bot_sweep_btts_yes_v1` **−€1,823** over 1,017, `bot_sweep_ou25_v1` −€1,211, `bot_sweep_ou35_v1` −€1,337, `bot_coolbet_value_v1` −€165. So the bankroll column is silent for exactly the bots whose signals the operator follows with real money ([[project_realmoney_shadow_signals]]). `scripts/fix_bot_bankroll_drift.py` and the `BOT-BANKROLL-DRIFT` smoke test both sum `simulated_bets` only — correct today, but it means neither would ever notice a shadow bot's bankroll being wrong. Decide whether `current_bankroll` should track a bot's own ledger (per ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #18, whichever of the two tables it uses) or whether the column should be explicitly documented as simulated-only and the shadow P&L surfaced separately. Do not "fix" it by pointing the rebuild at both tables — #18 exists because a bot's ledger is one or the other, and summing both would double-count nothing but would silently mask a bot writing to the wrong one.


> **✅ FOREBET-REPRICE Done 2026-09-02** (engine + odds-intel-web + smoke) — The landing published Forebet at **+12.44% ROI** — a figure computed entirely from the odds we had just proven unobtainable — while footnoting that the odds were not real. Republishing a number derived from fabricated prices, with a caveat attached, still presents fiction as a measurement. New `scripts/_competitor_reprice.py` re-settles a competitor's own picks at prices that existed, wired into `audit_vs_forebet.py`. **Result on 1,136 of 1,909 picks (59.5% fixture-match, median 11 books at close): their claimed odds +8.97%, best closing price across books −0.18%, Bet365 close −6.54%, median close −7.40%.** Forebet's entire published edge is the odds inflation. `their_stats` now publishes the **best closing** figure (−0.18%) — the most favourable realistic assumption for them, so the claim is conservative — with their own figure kept verbatim as `their_stats_claimed_odds` and the full ladder in `their_reprice`. Landing row now reads the repriced ROI with "Forebet claims +12.44% on 1,909 picks · we re-settled their own picks at the best closing price (1,136 of 1,909 we could match)" and links the ledger JSON. **Methodology trap, filed as ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #29**: the verification script prices at `MAX(odds)` over ALL snapshots ever, which is right for *"was this reachable"* and wrong as an *execution* price — settling at MAX-ever rates Forebet at **+37.66%, better than they claim themselves**. Execution uses `DISTINCT ON (bookmaker)` closing quotes filtered to `timestamp <= m.date`. Smoke `FOREBET-REPRICE` pins the closing-line filter, per-book DISTINCT, that `their_stats` is the repriced figure carrying its own coverage, that the claimed figure is still published, and that repriced < claimed; verified to fail when the closing-line filter is removed.


> **✅ WINNERODDS-MARKET-RESOLVE + TIPSTRR-DROPPED Done 2026-09-02** (engine + odds-intel-web + smoke) — Audit of the two landing competitors whose picks were not market-resolved. **(1) WINNERODDS-MARKET-RESOLVE**: their `apuesta` pick code fully identifies the market and we were discarding it as `market="mixed"`, so the landing compared their ALL-markets ROI against our 1X2+OU2.5 ROI. Their record is **47% Asian Handicap (a market we do not model) + 16% OU 3.5**; only **690 of 1,852** bets are comparable. They also stake Kelly-style (mean €37) where we stake €10 flat. `their_stats` is now the comparable subset re-settled at flat stake — **+7.67% (n=690)** instead of +4.50% (n=1,852) — with the all-markets figure kept as `their_stats_all_markets` and the full split as `their_by_market`. **This corrects against us**: our published lead over WinnerOdds drops from +9.32pp to +6.15pp. **Also fixed a silent data bug**: their loss status is spelled **`LOOSE`**, and the picks-CSV export tested for `"lose"`, so all **752 losses became blank** — the CSV behind the landing's "Verify" link contained only wins and voids and recomputed to **+86% ROI**. The aggregate JSON was correct because `wo_summary` used the right vocabulary; the two paths had drifted. Both now derive from one `result_of_status()` mapping. CSV and JSON now reconcile exactly (+7.67%, n=690). **(2) TIPSTRR-DROPPED**: removed from the landing. Not broken — unfixable. Tipstrr is a tipster **marketplace**, so any figure is a consequence of which tipsters we listed: we pooled 3 of 8 hand-written slugs out of thousands, **all three marked `active=False`**, across all bet types at month grain (per-bet market detail is genuinely paywalled, so it can never be made like-for-like). The scraper and audit still run and the ledger JSON is still written; we just no longer present it as a competitor benchmark. Smoke `COMP-FALLBACK-DRIFT-GUARD` pins the drop **as an absence** (the ledger key still exists, so it would otherwise be re-added by someone who does not know why it went). ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #28.

> **⬜ COMPETITOR-PRODUCT-VS-MARKET Filed 2026-09-02 (P3)** — The landing now compares competitors **market-level**: restricted to the markets we both price (1X2 + OU 2.5), at a common flat stake. That answers *"on the same bets, who prices better?"* — a claim about model skill, and the version that survives scrutiny. It is **not** the question a prospective customer asks, which is *"which subscription returns more?"* — and there a rival's Asian Handicap coverage is a **feature**, not a confound to be filtered out. WinnerOdds makes 47% of its bets in AH and we make none, so on the product question their breadth counts for them and our market-level framing quietly hides it. Decide whether the landing should show both (market-level as the model-skill claim, product-level as the buyer's-eye view), and note the honest tension: a product-level comparison is only fair once we actually cover comparable markets, which is what `AH-MODEL` would change. Related: `AH-MODEL + EPICBET-AH-COVERAGE`, ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #28.


> **✅ FOREBET-ODDS-CROSS-SOURCE + COMP-FALLBACK-DRIFT-GUARD Done 2026-09-02** (engine + odds-intel-web + smoke) — Two tasks about one thing: whether the numbers the landing page publishes about competitors are true. **(1) FOREBET-ODDS-CROSS-SOURCE**: `FOREBET-OU-VERIFY-2026-08-01` showed Forebet quoting prices no book offered, but rested entirely on our own `odds_snapshots` — dismissible as thin coverage. New `scripts/verify_forebet_odds_cross_source.py` re-runs the test with **Betaminic as a third-party control**, because Betaminic names its book (Bet365) and can therefore be checked against it. **Step 1 validates our data**: Betaminic's published Bet365 prices agree with our recorded Bet365 prices on **55% exactly, median +0.000, mean +0.003** (n=1,253) — two parties that never spoke report the same numbers, so our snapshots are no longer the weak link. **Step 2/3 test Forebet**: Forebet claims **>1.5x the best price available anywhere on 9.5%** of picks vs Betaminic's **0.7%**; against a single shared reference book it is **13.3% vs 0.7%**, and inside Forebet the inflation is concentrated on winners — **18.1% of winners vs 8.4% of losers (+9.8pp, p=2.4e-06)** — where Betaminic is flat (**0.8% vs 0.7%, p=0.77**). A bettor cannot get better prices on the bets that happen to win. Concrete examples: Forebet published 10.00 on a Maitland home win that 13 books priced at 1.83, and 4.33 on an Inter Miami over 2.5 priced at 1.29 (both fixtures verified as correctly matched, 13-14 books, ~3k snapshot rows each). **Correction made mid-task**: the first version reported "Forebet is 1.9x the honest floor" using exception *frequency*. That was wrong — Bet365 is top-of-market only **19.3%** of the time, so the control's rate is structurally suppressed and the comparison measured book choice, not honesty. Rewrote around exception *magnitude* plus a matched reference book. Filed as `ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #27`. **(2) COMP-FALLBACK-DRIFT-GUARD**: while checking those claims, found the landing's own numbers were false. `COMP_FALLBACK` in `page.tsx` — what renders when the request-time ledger fetch fails — was **59 days stale for 5 of 6 sources**, publishing **Tipstrr at -5.22% ROI when the ledger says +1.49%** (a sign error about a named competitor, on the page whose entire pitch is auditability). Root cause: the engine's daily audit workflow refreshes it only behind `steps.pat_check.outputs.has_pat == 'true'`, and **exits 0 with a plain echo when `FRONTEND_REPO_TOKEN` is absent** — so it never ran once, and nothing said so. Same silent-skip class as EPICBET-403 and the InplayBot UUID bug. **Fixes:** `betaminic` added to `LEDGER_KEYS` (it was on the landing but not in the sync list, so it could never be refreshed); fallback regenerated from the ledger for all 6; new `odds-intel-web/scripts/check_comp_fallback.mjs` + `comp_fallback_guard.yml` (push + daily 07:00 UTC) — deliberately **in the web repo**, which owns the published claim and can read the *public* ledger over HTTPS, so the guard needs no PAT and cannot be skipped for want of a credential. Tolerance-based (3pp ROI / 25% n / 30-day age) so daily ledger movement doesn't cry wolf, with an always-fatal **sign-flip** check. **Verified by replaying the real pre-fix values: the guard flags all 5 stale sources and names the Tipstrr sign flip explicitly.** The engine's PAT skip now emits a `::warning` annotation instead of an invisible echo. Smoke `COMP-FALLBACK-DRIFT-GUARD` pins `LEDGER_KEYS` completeness + the annotation, verified to fail when `betaminic` is removed.

> **⬜ AH-MODEL + EPICBET-AH-COVERAGE Filed 2026-09-02 (P3)** — We have no Asian Handicap model, so AH is absent from every competitor comparison and from our own pick surface. Two linked pieces: **(a)** build an AH head (the Poisson goal-difference distribution we already fit gives AH prices almost directly — quarter lines split across two half-lines, so the main work is line handling and calibration, not new modelling); **(b)** measure whether **Epicbet offers more AH lines than Coolbet**, which would make it the better AH execution venue. `scripts/book_uplift_report.py --a Coolbet --b Epicbet` already does line-matched cross-book comparison (matches on `handicap_line` — see ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #25/#16) so the coverage half is mostly a query. **Blocker to respect:** Coolbet carries only full/half AH lines, no quarters (`project_coolbet_limitations`, AH-NO-QUARTER filter) — if Epicbet does carry quarters, that is the actual argument for the venue switch. Not urgent: no AH model means no AH picks to place either way.


> **✅ COOLBET-BTTS-COMBINED + USER-PICK-MARKS-STATE + BOT-NO-PIN-TIER0-GUARD + PICK-CONFIDENCE-FLAGS Done 2026-08-23** (engine + odds-intel-web + smoke) — Four fixes shipped as a cluster. **(1) COOLBET-BTTS-COMBINED**: "Both Teams To Score & Over 2.5 Goals" combined markets on Coolbet were overwriting the pure BTTS yes/no slot. Root cause: `_looks_like_sub_period()` only caught half-time variants, not combined markets. Added `_COMBINED_MARKET_HINTS = (" & ", " and over", " and under", "+ over", "+ under")` and `_looks_like_combined_market()` helper in `workers/automation/coolbet_explorer.py`. The `is_btts` name-fallback now includes a `not combined` guard so "BTTS & Over 2.5" (result_key="yes", odds ~2.46, p≈40%) can no longer clobber the pure BTTS yes slot (odds ~1.49, p≈67%). Smoke `COOLBET-COMBINED-MARKET-FILTER` pins helper + guard. **(2) USER-PICK-MARKS-STATE**: upgraded the per-row bet-mark from a binary checkbox to a tri-state (0=untouched circle / 1=reviewed eye amber / 2=bet placed checkmark emerald). Migration 280 adds `state SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 2` to `user_pick_marks` (existing rows, all bet-placed, default to 2). `/api/me/pick-marks` route now accepts `{ pickId, state: 0|1|2 }` where 0=delete. `PickBetMark` component cycles 0→1→2→0 with SVG icons. `fetchUserPickMarkStates` returns `Map<string, 1|2>`. **(3) BOT-NO-PIN-TIER0-GUARD**: `bot_no_pin_home_v1` was firing on Saudi Division 1, Costa Rica Liga de Ascenso, Germany Regionalliga (tier=null/0) — leagues with zero model training coverage where the ensemble returns a uniform (1/3,1/3,1/3) Poisson fallback. Al Saqer vs Damac at 5.75 odds (58% model, 26% Pinnacle implied) was the concrete example. Fixed `_run_no_pin_shadow_pass` to JOIN leagues and filter `(l.tier IS NULL OR l.tier > 0)`. Smoke `BOT-NO-PIN-SHADOW` updated. **(4) PICK-CONFIDENCE-FLAGS**: display-only real-money warning badges on the upcoming-picks section of `/admin/shadow-bots`. Computed per-row from already-available `botName`, `tier`, `gapPp`, `signalOdds`, `u.market`, `u.selection`. Red (`border-rose-500/60` left border + `✕` badge) for: `bot_pin_1x2_draw_tier4_v1` (10 settled 1W/9L −65%), tier=null/0 (no model coverage), `gapPp < 10` (negative ROI for every bot below 10pp). Yellow (`border-amber-500/50` + `⚠` badge) for: `gapPp ≥ 25` on draw/OU35 (likely Coolbet phantom), home pick with odds ≥ 3.5 (pin_home underperforms at high odds −4.7% vs +58% sweet spot). Each flag carries a `title` tooltip with the specific reason. Bots keep firing to collect data — flags are display-only, operator uses them to decide which picks to back with real money. Smoke `PICK-CONFIDENCE-FLAGS` pins the four patterns.

> **✅ MATCH-STATUS-SWEEPER + MIGRATE-VPS-CUTOVER Done 2026-08-22** (engine + workflows + smoke) — Two infra fixes forced by USER-PICK-MARKS shipping earlier today. **(1) MIGRATE-VPS-CUTOVER**: migrate.yml had been silently failing since SUPABASE-CLEANUP-DROP-2026-07-13 (still running `supabase db push` against Supabase after we dropped the public schema there). Operators were hand-applying migrations via psql; when USER-PICK-MARKS shipped migration 278 (`user_pick_marks`), the shadow-bots checkbox POSTed to a table that didn't exist and reverted every tick. Rewrote to open VPS SSH tunnel + apply pending files via psql, tracked in a new `_schema_migrations(filename, applied_at)` table. First run backfills prefixes ≤ 277 as applied (they landed via the manual process before this fix). `ON_ERROR_STOP=1` so failures surface loudly instead of getting swallowed for six weeks. Serialized via `concurrency: db-migrations`. **(2) MATCH-STATUS-SWEEPER**: audit of pending shadow_bets revealed **1,760 zombies on 65 matches stuck at `status='scheduled'` past kickoff, plus 172 on 7 stuck-`live` and 64 on 8 postponed** — because the scheduler-based `settlement.py::fix_stale_live_matches()` (which handles exactly this) hadn't run in ~9 hours (last match transitioned to `finished` at 05:15 UTC; systemd scheduler stalled + `coolbet_health_ping` was alerting `consecutive_failed>=3`). Shadow bots concentrate in Australian NPL / State League / Japan J2 lower-tier fixtures — exactly the set AF's `/fixtures?live=all` doesn't push status for, so LivePoller's primary path misses them. Shipped `scripts/match_status_sweeper.py` + `.github/workflows/match_status_sweeper.yml` — 30-min GitHub Actions cron independent of the pipeline scheduler. Batch-fetches via `get_fixtures_batch` (20 fixtures per AF call), handles FT/AET/PEN/ABD/WO → finished + settlement_status='ready', PST/CANC/SUSP/AWD/INT → postponed AND voids BOTH `simulated_bets` AND `shadow_bets` (the scheduler version voids simulated only — that's why the 64 postponed-shadow zombies accumulated). Records a health-state ping every run so future stalls surface via the existing failure-alerter surface. Smoke `MATCH-STATUS-SWEEPER` pins the filter, status_short handling, both-table void discipline, batch fetch, and workflow cron schedule. Smoke `MIGRATE-VPS-CUTOVER` pins no more `supabase db push`, VPS tunnel present, `_schema_migrations` bootstrap, `ON_ERROR_STOP=1`, and concurrency gate. **Followup filed:** `SCHEDULER-STALL-RCA` — investigate why workers/scheduler.py stopped emitting settle_ready runs after 05:15 UTC today. LivePoller state or systemd-side issue; not urgent now that the standalone sweeper covers status refresh, but the scheduler runs other critical jobs (odds ingestion, prediction refresh, coolbet placement) that also silently stopped.

> **✅ SCHEDULER-STALL-RCA + SCHEDULER-AF-429-DEADLOCK + COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-RCA Done 2026-08-24** (engine + smoke) — Three P0/P1 reliability tasks closed as one cluster; the first two turned out to be the **same bug**. **ROOT CAUSE (confirmed from journalctl, not hypothesised):** `settlement.fix_stale_live_matches()` fetched every overdue fixture individually via `get_fixture_by_id()` — 80 that morning, and 225-351 on a normal day (max seen: 351) — serially, each call carrying AF 429 retry sleeps, inside `settle_ready` which fires every 15 min with `max_instances=1`. On 2026-08-22 the 06:15 run **never returned**; APScheduler then correctly skipped 06:30, 06:45, … through 11:45+ (5.5h). That is exactly what left 439 matches unsettled and 1,760 zombie shadow bets. **The filed premise was wrong in a useful way**: the scheduler process never died and other jobs kept running — settle_ready ran 60× that day. It was one wedged worker thread, not a systemd stall, which is why nothing external caught it. Same mechanism explains the Jul 12/15 hangs and the 43 `closing_snap_5min` / 37 `settle_ready` / 22 `backfill_coaches` max_instances blocks in the last 7d. **Fixes: (1)** the sweep is now batched (`get_fixtures_batch`, 20 ids/call → 351 matches costs 18 calls not 351) and bounded by `STALE_SWEEP_BUDGET_S` (300s, well inside the 15-min cadence), ordered oldest-first so a truncated run still drains the backlog. Also now voids `shadow_bets` on postponed matches, not just `simulated_bets` (the gap MATCH-STATUS-SWEEPER flagged). **(2)** `api_football._get` hardened: named `AF_TIMEOUT_S`/`AF_MAX_ATTEMPTS`/`AF_RETRY_BUDGET_S`/`AF_MAX_BACKOFF_S` constants, a **wall-clock deadline across all attempts including backoff** (previously unbounded at attempts × (timeout + sleep)), `Retry-After` honoured and capped, and `time.sleep()` moved **out of `_rate_lock`** — sleeping under the global lock convoyed every AF-touching thread behind the slowest one, quietly turning 12 scheduler workers into 1. **(3)** New `job_stall_watchdog` (5-min interval, deliberately **not** wrapped in `_run_job` so it survives every worker being wedged): tracks in-flight jobs by thread, and when one passes `JOB_STALL_WARN_S` (45 min) dumps that thread's stack via `sys._current_frames()` and Telegrams once per hang. This RCA cost hours precisely because the only journal evidence was a repeating "max_instances blocked" line — proof something hung, no clue where. Next time it is one log line. **(4) COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-RCA**: the `is_ou` name-fallback accepted any market containing "total goals"/"over/under" — which also matches **team totals** ("Total Goals Home Team"), non-goal totals (corners/cards/shots/bookings) and parity markets, each carrying its own `line` that collided with the real goals ladder. Added `_NON_GOALS_TOTAL_HINTS` + `_looks_like_non_goals_total()`, applied to the **fallback path only** so trusted `_MTID_OU` markets still win (no regression for the non-standard-mtid leagues in COOLBET-MARKET-NAMES 2026-05-23), plus the `not combined` guard the BTTS branch already had. Also shipped the raw-payload dump the task asked for: `_dump_ou_mislabel_payload()` writes `dev/active/coolbet-raw-<match_id>.json` (capped at 3/process) the first times the monotonicity guard fires, capturing market names + mtids + lines + outcomes — we store parsed rows only, so without it the culprit name is unrecoverable. **Honest limits:** the Coolbet RCA is evidence-*shaped* but not payload-*confirmed* — Coolbet snapshots stopped writing 2026-08-23 11:30 (~25h before this work), so no live payload could be pulled; the dump closes that loop on the next daemon run. Kept the guard's whole-OU-set drop rather than per-line: monotonicity tells you the ladder is inconsistent, not which rung lies, so dropping the offending line alone risks keeping the wrong one. Smoke: `SCHEDULER-AF-429-DEADLOCK`, `SCHEDULER-STALL-RCA`, `SCHEDULER-STALL-WATCHDOG`, `COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-RCA`. **Followups filed below:** COOLBET-SNAPSHOT-STALL-2026-08-24, AF-RATE-LIMIT-CROSS-PROCESS-2026-08-24.

> **✅ MOVE-ACTIVE-TO-BETA + RETIRE-DORMANT-OPT Done 2026-08-22** (engine + migration 279 + smoke) — ROI-by-cohort audit against /performance's public-cohort filter (flat €10 stake, since 2026-05-04): **calibrated n=468 ROI +16.12%**, **beta n=263 ROI +5.01%**, **active n=81 ROI +6.74%**. Investigated the `active` cohort — 5 bots, all with `maturity_label='active'` since April 2026 launch (pre-taxonomy label). Only 2 have ever fired: `bot_opt_home_lower` (n=58 ROI +4.55%, retooled 2026-07-31) and `bot_conservative` (n=23 ROI +12.26%, starved-by-selectivity). Other 3 (`bot_opt_away_british`, `bot_opt_away_europe`, `bot_opt_ou_british`) have **0 fires ever** — 4 months of zero volume. Config audit: `bot_opt_away_europe` has min_prob=0.40 for AWAY at odds 2.20-3.50 (extremely tight gate, likely bug copied from HOME template); `bot_opt_ou_british` has odds 2.50-4.00 for OU (longshot range, typical OU 2.5 prices 1.60-2.30 — config almost certainly stale); `bot_opt_away_british` config looks reasonable but production data never produces matching candidates. **Action: migration 279** (a) moves `bot_opt_home_lower` + `bot_conservative` to `beta` (semantically correct — production strategies being volume-tested); (b) retires the 3 zero-fire opt-* bots outright with `is_active=false + retired_at=NOW() + maturity_label='retired'`, since the fresh shadow-bot lineup (bot_sweep_*, bot_no_pin_home_v1, bot_pin_*) covers similar territory with better-tuned floors. Effect on /performance breakdown: 3 buckets → 2 (calibrated 468 · beta 344). Placement pipeline unaffected (COOLBET_RECORD_ALLOWED_MATURITY='calibrated' — none of these were placing real money). Smoke `MOVE-ACTIVE-TO-BETA` pins all 5 bot names + beta target + 3 retirement gates. **Column comment on `bots.maturity_label` updated** to reflect the new taxonomy (calibrated/beta/experimental/retired; `active` deprecated).

> **✅ PICKS-USER-GATE + USER-PICK-MARKS Done 2026-08-22** (odds-intel-web + engine smoke + migration 278) — Two features shipped as a pair. **(1) PICKS-USER-GATE**: the /picks page + `/api/v1/upcoming` public endpoint used to return the wider `calibrated + beta + active` cohort (~9 picks today) while the public Telegram channel `@oddsintelpicks` ships `calibrated` only (~2 picks today). Users saw 4× more picks on the web than in Telegram with no clear "why". Narrowed `/api/v1/upcoming` to `calibrated` only so the public JSON matches Telegram one-to-one. The wider cohort now renders only for signed-in visitors on `/picks`, via server-side helper `fetchUpcomingPicks(maturityLabels)` in `src/lib/upcoming-picks.ts` called directly from the page (never exposed as a client-fetchable route — network tab can't scrape the wider set). Anonymous visitors see a "Sign in to see every pick" nudge. **(2) USER-PICK-MARKS**: per-row "Bet placed" checkbox on `/admin/shadow-bots` (superadmin-only surface) Upcoming picks section — operator ticks off shadow-bot picks they've manually placed with a book so they don't double-place or forget which are still open. Persists in new `user_pick_marks(user_id uuid, pick_id uuid)` table (migration 278). New route `/api/me/pick-marks` GET + POST, cookie-auth gated, service-role client bypasses RLS with explicit `.eq("user_id", user.id)` filter. Client component `PickBetMark` (compact + labeled variants) does optimistic toggling; shadow-bots page uses the compact variant as a leading column on the upcoming-picks grid. Smokes `PICKS-USER-GATE` (pins calibrated-only PUBLIC_MATURITY_LABELS + SIGNED_IN_MATURITY_LABELS not-importable-in-any-api-route + /picks branches on isSignedIn) and `USER-PICK-MARKS` (pins migration 278 + route auth + checkbox wired on shadow-bots + superadmin gate + /picks doesn't render PickBetMark). TypeScript passes. **Followup filed:** `TELEGRAM-DISPATCH-MARKER` — public Telegram send path has no persisted marker on `simulated_bets` recording which pick actually went to the channel; today we approximate via `maturity_label='calibrated'` cohort but a `signaled_public_at` column would let /picks show a definitive "sent to Telegram" badge and let the operator audit dispatch coverage.

> **✅ SIGNAL-PAUSE-DECOUPLE + SELFPAUSE-STICKY-FIX Done 2026-08-27** (engine + smoke) — **Telegram went silent for 4 days and nothing errored.** Operator reported "today we have 1 pick, why is it not sent". Root cause was NOT Telegram: `coolbet_session_state.placement_paused` had been `true` since **2026-08-23 03:53 UTC** with reason `daemon self-pause: 7 consecutive errors over 207m` — the Mac daemon self-paused on the ongoing Coolbet Imperva/403 + maintenance-probe outage (`session_healthy=f`, `last_error_at` still current at time of fix). `betting_pipeline.py::signal_coolbet_bets` treated that flag as a *full* mute and returned before `signal_all_bets()` ran, so the public-channel post (which lives inside the signaler, after the operator-side send) was never even attempted. DB confirms the cliff exactly at the pause: signaled/picks by day — 08-23 4/12, 08-24 **0/5**, 08-25 **0/3**, 08-26 **0/3**, 08-27 **0/1**. Env was fine all along (`TELEGRAM_PUBLIC_CHANNEL=@oddsintelpicks` set on VPS). **Two bugs fixed. (1) SIGNAL-PAUSE-DECOUPLE**: placement and notification shared one kill switch. A *daemon self-pause* is a placement-side outage, not an operator decision, and must not mute notification — least of all the public `@oddsintelpicks` channel, which makes zero Coolbet calls. The signal gate now branches: daemon self-pause → **signaling continues** (notification-only: no Coolbet API calls, no `real_bets` writes, so always safe while placement is down); operator `/pause` → still mutes everything (that is the deliberate behaviour). Both sides use a new shared `coolbet_state.is_daemon_self_pause()` + `DAEMON_SELF_PAUSE_MARKER` constant, so the reason substring can't drift between the writer and the two readers. **(2) SELFPAUSE-STICKY-FIX**: the daemon's auto-clear sat inside the `consecutive_errors > 0` recovery branch **and** was gated on the in-memory `self_paused_this_burst`. Both reset on daemon restart — so a self-pause that outlived one restart could *never* auto-clear: the restarted daemon ticks cleanly from zero and reaches neither gate. That is why this ran 4 days instead of self-healing. The clear now runs on any clean tick, branches on the durable DB reason, and is sequenced by a `clear_check_pending` flag armed at boot (so the first clean tick after a restart clears a pause left by the previous process), re-armed on error ticks, and cleared only on a successful check so a DB hiccup retries. **Pause cleared manually 2026-08-27**; today's pick signaled. Smokes `SIGNAL-PAUSE-DECOUPLE` (functional test of the helper + self-pause branch must not `return` + operator branch must + daemon stamps the shared marker) and `SELFPAUSE-STICKY-FIX` (clear not gated on the burst flag, indentation check proving it is not nested in the recovery branch, flag armed at boot + re-armed on errors + cleared only inside the try). Both verified to **fail against the pre-fix code**, not just pass against the new. `COOLBET-DAEMON-SELFPAUSE` updated for the new block structure. **Note:** the underlying Coolbet session is still unhealthy — this fix stops that outage from silencing picks; it does not fix Coolbet auth itself. **Followup filed:** `SIGNAL-SILENCE-ALERT` — nothing alerted on 4 days of zero signals; "0 signals" and "no qualifying picks" are indistinguishable from every observability surface.

> **✅ SIGNAL-SILENCE-ALERT Done 2026-08-27** (engine + smoke) — Four days of zero Telegram signals produced **no alert of any kind**; `/status` and the daily summary both showed the red paused glyph, but both are pull-only, so "0 signals today" and "no qualifying picks today" were indistinguishable from every surface. Same silent-failure class as the InplayBot UUID bug. Shipped `health_alerts.check_signal_silence()`, wired into the hourly `run_snapshot_check()` bundle (10-22 UTC — no new scheduler entry). **Two complementary conditions: (A)** `load_signal_candidates()` returns picks passing every signaling gate but the oldest has waited > `SIGNAL_STUCK_AFTER_MIN` (180 min — deliberately clears the widest legitimate betting_refresh gap of ~2.5h, given windows at 09:30/11:00/13:30/15:00/17:30/19:00/20:30). **(B)** no `simulated_bets` row written for > `NO_PICKS_AFTER_HOURS` (48h) — condition A is *structurally blind* to this, since zero candidates means zero stuck candidates, so a pipeline producing nothing looks exactly like one whose output all went out fine. Threshold is generous on purpose: pick volume swings hard by fixture list (34 on 08-16, 3 on 08-25), so anything tighter cries wolf on a thin weekend. **Design notes that are load-bearing:** condition A calls the signaler's own `load_signal_candidates()` instead of reimplementing its WHERE clause — the candidate set has non-obvious gating (per-market edge floors, 36h lookahead, `real_bets` dedup, combo exclusion, `DISTINCT ON` collapsing multi-bot picks) and a hand-rolled copy would drift and alert on picks that were never eligible; reusing it means the check follows any future change for free. Alerts fire on **both** Telegram and email — an alert about Telegram silence sent only over Telegram cannot report a dead bot token, so the email leg is not redundant (the Telegram leg calls `send_telegram` directly with no pause gate in front of it, which covers the logic-mute class this was built for). An active pause is named in the alert text so an operator `/pause` is immediately obvious as the cause rather than something to go digging for. **Verified live on the VPS**: quiet run confirmed (no stuck candidates, last pick 12.2h old); firing path exercised end-to-end with the send legs stubbed — both conditions fired on both channels with the real DB queries running (`ANY(%s::uuid[])` cast and the 730-min age computation confirmed). Smoke `SIGNAL-SILENCE-ALERT` pins reuse-not-reimplement, both channels, condition B's presence, the wiring into the hourly bundle, and both threshold floors — verified to **fail** both when the check is left unwired and when the grace period is tightened to 30 min.

> **⬜ TELEGRAM-DISPATCH-MARKER Filed 2026-08-22 (P3)** — 1-2h — `workers/automation/coolbet_signaler.py::send_telegram_public` currently logs the public channel `message_id` in memory but never writes it to the DB (only the operator channel populates `simulated_bets.signal_message_id` + `bet_telegram_alerts`). Add `simulated_bets.public_channel_message_id BIGINT` (or a new `public_telegram_dispatched_picks` table if we want per-message audit) and set it right after the successful public send. Enables: (a) exact "was in Telegram" badge on /picks instead of the current calibrated-cohort proxy; (b) coverage audit if the public sender ever regresses silently; (c) idempotent skip on retry paths that currently only key on `signaled_at`. Non-urgent — the calibrated-cohort proxy is close enough for the /picks user gate we just shipped.

> **✅ COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-GUARD + SHADOW-AUDIT-2026-08-22 Done 2026-08-22** — Full audit of today's 7,649 shadow picks (699 unique bot×match×market×selection) turned up two data-integrity bugs and two model-calibration flags. **Shipped defensive fix** for the Coolbet OU line-mislabel bug: 51/302 matches with today's shadow picks had Coolbet OU rows where Under-probability was NOT monotone-nondecreasing across .5 lines — mathematically impossible for a real market. Root cause hypothesis: `is_ou` gate in `coolbet_explorer.parse_market` accepts any market with "total goals" / "over/under" substring in name (line 273) — non-goals totals (halftime goals, corners, cards) with matching line values likely write to the goals-OU slot and clobber it. **Fix**: added `_ou_rows_monotone` helper + buffer-and-validate in `store_coolbet_snapshots_for_match` — the whole (match, book) OU set is dropped when U-prob monotonicity fails (2pp tolerance). Zero data > lying data. Non-OU markets unaffected. Five shadow picks TODAY were consuming these mislabeled OU prices: Shanghai Port II U2.5@1.80 (sweep_ou25), Leon vs Monterrey O2.5@2.11 (sweep_ou25), Gijang United O3.5@2.77 (sweep_ou35), Dandenong Thunder U2.5@3.05 (sweep_ou25), Northern Tigers O3.5@3.05 (sweep_ou35). Smoke `COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-2026-08-22` pins the helper + writer buffer + functional check (known-bad rejects, known-good passes). **Followups filed below** (BOOKMAKER-LABEL-MISMATCH, SWEEP-HOME-BOTS-CALIBRATION, BTTS-CHINA-CALIBRATION, /picks dedupe, /picks Coolbet column, refresh-pre-KO-odds, Coolbet-OU RCA with API dump). **Audit numbers to remember**: 353 of 699 unique shadow picks (51%) show model-vs-market edge > 20% — dominated by `bot_sweep_1x2_home_v1` and `bot_no_pin_home_v1` overconfident on Home selections, especially reserve/II teams and cross-tier matchups. This is a calibration flag not a bet-placement bug (shadow-only) but merits investigation before any of these bots graduate to production.

> **📊 AUDIT-FEATURE-VECTOR + SLIPPAGE-DIAGNOSIS + AF-QUOTA-DIAGNOSIS 2026-08-21** — Three diagnostic investigations run today, findings that reshape the priority stack. **NOT DOING** — just findings + followup tasks filed below the log.
>
> **(1) Feature-vector coverage audit** (v20260712, MFV rows since 2026-08-01, n=7,965):
> - 67-feature vector loaded from `data/models/soccer/v20260712/feature_cols.pkl` looks rich on paper
> - **But real coverage is terrible**: `injury_severity_score` 1.0%, `xg_overperf` 2.8%, `team_avg_player_rating` 1.0%, `weather_temp_c` 7.2%, `referee_cards_avg` 6.5%, `fixture_importance` (non-default) 29.9%
> - Model is being fed medians in 60-99% of feature-cells → XGBoost training on essentially-null data for its non-market features
> - The gap isn't "missing features" — it's "features exist but never populated"
> - **Path forward: coverage-fix job, not new features.** Backfill `injury_severity_score` from AF injuries endpoint (~4h), populate `xg_overperf` from existing xG data (~2h), etc.
> - Filed as `FEATURE-COVERAGE-BACKFILL-2026-08-21` below
>
> **(2) Slippage / CLV-vs-realized-ROI by market and book** (n=803 pre-match settled since 2026-05-04):
> - Per-market realization gap (stated edge vs realized ROI): 1x2 ~0pp gap (edge realized), OU ~0pp gap, **BTTS +7.7pp gap (worst market for slippage)**
> - **Per-book slippage — this is the finding**: **Bet365 loses money despite +10% CLV** (n=178, flat ROI −10%, CLV +10%, gap 20pp). Marathonbet +30% ROI at +15% CLV. Coolbet +40% ROI at +17% CLV (n=46). Betano +24% ROI, Unibet +10% ROI. 10Bet marginal at −3% ROI at +5% CLV. Bet365 has the most concentrated bets AND the worst realization gap.
> - **Diagnosis**: Bet365's odds capture is likely stale or their prices unreachable at scale — 20pp CLV-to-ROI gap on 178 bets is not variance. Either (a) fix odds capture freshness, (b) route away from Bet365, or (c) tighten Bet365-specific edge threshold. Filed as `BET365-EXECUTION-AUDIT-2026-08-21`.
>
> **(3) API-Football quota diagnosis** — Aug 1 + Aug 8 both hit ~149,800/150k (99.9% ceiling). Root cause identified by sub-agent:
> - 81% of quota consumed by live-polling: `/fixtures/statistics` (40.7%) + `/fixtures/events` (40.3%)
> - LivePoller HIGH-priority mode (45s cadence) fires whenever a match has a pending paper bet OR is in minute 25-75 with ≤1 goal — but inplay bots are paper-only, no real money placed
> - **We're burning 9-10% of daily quota for zero revenue**
> - Multiple concrete cuts identified saving 6,000-7,400 calls/day: disable `/odds/live` entirely, move stats/events from 135s → 180s, remove HIGH-priority 45s acceleration
> - Freed quota could fund: `/fixtures/players` live polling (per-player xG for prematch improvements), `/standings` intraday refresh, `/predictions` hourly refresh for top leagues
> - Filed as `AF-QUOTA-REALLOCATION-2026-08-21` — operator confirmation needed before shipping (removes inplay bot data source; verify no downstream UI/alert dependency)

> **✅ FLAT-ROI-EVERYWHERE + METHODOLOGY-COPY-REWRITE Done 2026-08-21** (odds-intel-web + engine smoke) — Public-facing ROI numbers now compute at **€10 flat stake per pick** instead of internal Kelly stakes. Rationale: (a) competitor comparison card on the landing was mixing our Kelly ROI (+9.4%) with competitors' flat ROI (WinnerOdds / Tipstrr / SignalOdds / Forebet all publish flat) — not apples-to-apples; (b) recent Kelly-vs-flat audit across four windows (60d / 90d / walk-forward / since 2026-05-04) showed Kelly's advantage is inconsistent — three of four windows had Kelly at parity or losing vs flat. Publishing flat is the honest, defensible, comparable number. **Numbers change**: landing hero moves from +9.4% (Kelly) → **+10.04% (flat)** on n=803 since 2026-05-04. Internal bots continue staking Kelly (proportional to divergence) — Kelly stays on admin dashboards. Also rewrote /methodology "The model" + "Why flat stake" sections to describe the actual strategy honestly: primary signal is a Pinnacle-implied probability anchor (97% weight on Tier 1 1x2), with the in-house Poisson+XGBoost blend contributing 3-45% depending on market/tier. Old copy claimed a "prediction model" without disclosing the anchor mechanism — a legal/marketing risk before paid-tier launch. New copy explains the CLV-verification story (why positive CLV across all buckets is the real proof). Smoke `FLAT-ROI-EVERYWHERE` pins the FLAT_STAKE_EUR constant, the flat-PnL computation, and the pnlFlat/stakeFlat accumulators — so a rename or refactor can't silently revert to Kelly. **Followups filed**: `PREDICTION-HEADROOM-EXPANSION` (sub-agent research on lower-tier + niche-market paths, agent report captured below).

> **✅ ADMIN-PLACE-COHORT-CLEANUP Done 2026-08-21** (odds-intel-web + engine smoke) — `getPlaceableBets()` used to show every pending `simulated_bets` row — including retired-bot bets (2 pending today; 122 retired-combo bets in 60d contributed −€501 PnL), inplay-bot bets (296 rows in 60d, −€244 PnL, wrong operator surface), and exotic markets (combo, double_chance — both retired, both always net negative when they leak). Added JS-side filter to drop three classes: (a) `bot.retired_at != null`, (b) `bot.name.startsWith("inplay_")`, (c) `market IN ("combo","double_chance")`. Bot join now selects `maturity_label + retired_at` so the filter has fields to read. **NOT retiring bot_summer_specialist** despite ROI −56% at n=14 — CLV is **+16.6%** on the main 1x2 home line, textbook "model has real edge, variance hasn't converged" pattern (same class the OUT-OF-BETA-CUTOFF-RECHECK 2026-07-08 correctly held on). Bot's own kill rule is `ROI < -5% AND CLV < 0%` — CLV condition fails, so kill rule does not apply. Followup **BOT-SUMMER-SPECIALIST-N30-CHECK** filed to re-evaluate at n=30 settled per original gate. Root cause for retired bots firing was already fixed 2026-08-16 (ACCA-RETIRED-LEAK-FIX) — this commit is defence-in-depth for mid-flight retirement + a cleaner operator surface as prep for the two-tier "raw model vs filtered picks" UX. Smoke `ADMIN-PLACE-COHORT-CLEANUP` pins the four moving parts (bot select fields + three filters). **Followup filed**: `BOT-CONFIG-GOLDEN-MIDDLE` — walk-forward validation held (+13.7% flat ROI post-2026-06-06 vs +11.5% no-floor), next step is designing the two-tier /performance UX (raw model at +8-11%, gated cohort at +13-14%). **Followup filed**: `WIDER-BACKTEST-NO-FILTERS` — explore what the model does across the full pool without any threshold/filter, mid-season when league volume is high, to spot new bot ideas or config levers.

> **✅ PICKS-COHORT-ALIGN Done 2026-08-21** (odds-intel-web + engine smoke) — `/api/v1/upcoming` (the feed for `/picks` + the Telegram-referenced public list) was scoped to `maturity_label IN (calibrated,beta,active)` + standard markets, but **did not** exclude in-play bots or retired bots. `/api/v1/track-record` (the ledger + hero + landing) applies both filters. Result: a Telegram subscriber clicking a pick on /picks and cross-checking it on /performance could see the same pick vanish from the ledger — a trust-loss class of bug (same shape as LANDING-COMP-COHORT-DISCLOSURE 2026-07-06). **Fix**: added `.not("bots.name", "like", "inplay_%%")` + `.is("bots.retired_at", null)` to the upcoming query, updated the scope note in the response to reference the ledger cohort, and rewrote the docstring so future edits keep the two endpoints in lock-step. Verified against current live window: 16 rows → 15 rows (one inplay-bot pick removed) — small delta today but every row now reconciles with /performance. Cross-cohort study (2026-05-04 → today, settled): /picks scope pre-fix showed n=1,027 ROI +13.4%; /performance scope showed n=798 ROI +8.5%. Post-fix the two describe the same 798-bet cohort. Smoke `PICKS-COHORT-ALIGN` pins the maturity gate + inplay filter + retired filter + market whitelist on both endpoints. **Followup filed** (BOT-CONFIG-GOLDEN-MIDDLE below): edge-floor sweep on the aligned cohort suggests a 12%/5%/12% floor (1x2 / o/u / btts) would raise ROI from +8.5% → +21.0% at n=402 vs the placement config's +15.3% at n=571 — worth a controlled experiment.

> **✅ NAV-AUTH-VISIBLE + PROFILE-MINIMAL Done 2026-08-21** (odds-intel-web + engine smoke) — Follow-up to PERF-SIGNUP-HISTORY. User reported "I can't really see if I'm logged in or not" — the previous nav rendered near-identical LogIn / LogOut lucide icons (same neutral-grey colour, same size) and both were `hidden sm:flex` so on mobile signed-in state was invisible entirely. Signup could complete and the user wouldn't notice. **Fix in three parts.** (1) **Sign-in button** (signed-out state): swapped icon-only affordance for a colour-accented pill with LogIn icon **+ "Sign in" text**, blue accent (`border-blue-500/40 bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-300`), always visible incl. mobile. (2) **Avatar** (signed-in state): 32px circle with the user's first initial, deterministic per-user colour (6-way hash → blue/emerald/amber/fuchsia/cyan/rose ring), opens a popover on click with signed-in email + Profile link + Sign out. Uses `useAuth().isAnonymous` so a lazily-created anon session (from favorites/tracker) still shows the Sign-in CTA — otherwise the auto-anon path would swallow the conversion hook. Skipped Google avatar images to avoid adding `lh3.googleusercontent.com` to `img-src` CSP + `remotePatterns` — colored initials solve the visual-state problem without config plumbing. (3) **Restored /profile page** — minimal server-rendered page at `src/app/(app)/profile/page.tsx`: shows email, display_name, tier (with superadmin badge if applicable), member-since date; unauthenticated visitors get `redirect("/login?next=/profile")`. Client sign-out button in `src/components/profile-sign-out-button.tsx` calls `useAuth().signOut()` then `router.push("/")` + `refresh()`. Not linked from anywhere except the avatar dropdown — deliberately minimal, "more coming as tiers return". Smoke `NAV-AUTH-VISIBLE` pins six moving parts: nav gates on `isRealUser` (not just `user`), renders `avatarColor` + `avatarInitial` helpers, has labeled Sign-in text + blue accent border, exposes ARIA `role="menu"` popover; profile page has `redirect("/login?next=/profile")` + renders the sign-out button; sign-out button calls signOut + routes home. TypeScript passes.

> **✅ PERF-SIGNUP-HISTORY Done 2026-08-21** (odds-intel-web + engine smoke) — Poll of 29 Telegram subs (7 responses, 24% response rate) showed 57% wanted "full pick history + filters (sort by league, market, edge)" on the web, but the honest read was that these features were already Pro-gated protecting a paywall no one buys (TIER-COLLAPSE 2026-06-24 → only 2 legacy Pro subs). Flipped the `/performance` history gate from `isPro` to `!!user`: anonymous still sees the 10-bet ledger teaser + Sign-up-free CTA, logged-in users get the full filterable ledger (league + market + bot filters, sort by newest/oldest/biggest win/biggest loss/highest odds/lowest odds, up to 200 rendered rows). Perceived speed unchanged — `getAllBets()` still slow (~20K row scan) but already streams via `<Suspense>` with cached leaderboard/hero as fallback, so users see the fast path first and history hydrates in. Sanitize helper now also passes `closingOdds` for Elite; new `toFullBetItems` mapper builds the `FullBetItem[]` from `SanitizedBotBet[]`. `PerformanceHistory` prop renamed `isPro` → `isLoggedIn` (three call sites, all in performance/page.tsx). **Bundled:** (a) `PerformanceLeaderboard` subhead now surfaces the full strategy funnel — "Tested to date: N strategies · X proven · Y underperforming · Z maturing · W retired" — so visitors see the full 50+ strategies we've tried, not just the current survivors. Reconciles the "why does the visible count not match /admin/bots" question. (b) Anonymous ledger CTA changed from "Upgrade to Pro" → "Sign up free" (matches the actual funnel; Pro is deprecated). Smoke `PERF-SIGNUP-HISTORY` pins all four moving parts (page gate on isLoggedIn, LoggedInPerformanceSection stream, toFullBetItems mapper, history component leagueFilter + SORT_LABELS + Sign-up-free CTA, leaderboard retiredBotCount prop + PERF-BOT-FUNNEL anchor). TypeScript passes. **Followup filed:** `PERF-CONFIG-THRESHOLDS-REVIEW` — review /admin/place edge cutoffs (Cautious/Bet/edge<10% flags) against current backtest data now that some bots + markets have matured; thresholds haven't been revisited since original setup.

> **✅ FOREBET-OU-VERIFY-2026-08-01 Done 2026-08-01** — **Smoking gun.** Cross-referenced Forebet's 806 OU 2.5 picks (which drove their +30.20% headline) against our own `odds_snapshots` on the same matches. Fuzzy-joined 366/806 to matches in our `matches` table; pulled OU snapshots for 321 of those. Results are conclusive: Forebet publishes odds that **were never reachable** in the actual market. **(1)** On **184 of 321 (57%) matched picks, Forebet's stated pick_odds are HIGHER than the BEST book we ever saw across every bookmaker at any time** — mathematically impossible for a real bettor to have taken. **(2)** On the 145 winning picks where we have Pinnacle-close reference odds, the **median inflation is +0.360, mean +0.390** in odds terms — e.g. Pinnacle close 1.65 shown as Forebet 2.01. **(3)** Extreme examples on winning picks: FC Honka vs HJK Helsinki over 2.5 (Pinnacle **1.16**, Forebet **4.00**, score 1-7 both win but the odds are fantasy); Brazil vs Haiti over 2.5 (Pinnacle **1.31**, Forebet **3.35**); Barracas Central vs Huracán under 2.5 (Pinnacle **1.33**, Forebet **3.20**). Five of top-10 inflations show Forebet at odds ≥ 3.00 where Pinnacle was ≤ 1.50 — systematic, not variance. **(4) Replay ROI on the same 321 picks at real odds:** Forebet stated **+37.44%**, our Pinnacle-close **−0.26%**, best-price-across-every-book **+11.69%** (upper-bound theoretical). **The 37pp gap is entirely fabricated odds on winners.** **(5)** Settlement flag (`predict_y`/`predict_no`) is basically clean: 1 mismatch in 321 — so the fraud is on the odds column, not on won/lost status. **Implication for the landing:** Forebet's +12.91% headline is not comparable to our +6.19% on reachable-market prices. On honestly-settled OU + 1x2, Forebet's realistic overall ROI is somewhere between **−3% and +2%** (their 1x2 was already −0.67% independently). **Ship script**: `scripts/verify_forebet_ou_odds.py` reproduces the finding; **output CSV**: `ledger/forebet_ou_verify.csv` (321 rows with side-by-side FB odds vs our_pinnacle vs our_max vs actual score). **Landing decision deferred to user**: could add a footnote to the Forebet row noting "Forebet's published `pick_odds` inflate on winners — audited median +0.360 vs Pinnacle-close in our fixture-matched sample", or leave the raw comparison as-is and let the CSV speak for anyone who checks. Naming Forebet in accusation is a business call, not an engineering one.

> **✅ COMPETITOR-PICKS-CSV-2026-08-01 Done 2026-08-01** — Aggregate `their_stats` numbers on the landing hero aren't verifiable by themselves. Shipped per-pick CSVs + a fixture-level matrix so a visitor can pull the underlying rows and audit any single fixture. **(1)** Every `audit_vs_*.py` now writes `ledger/picks_<source>.csv` alongside `comparison_<source>.json`, using shared schema in `scripts/_picks_csv.py` (kickoff_date, league, home_team, away_team, market, pick, odds, result, pnl_per_unit, ref_url). Per-source row counts: DeepBetting 313, Forebet 1,832, SignalOdds 1,157, Tipstrr 7 (monthly aggregates only — per-pick paywalled), WinnerOdds 1,367. **(2)** New `scripts/dump_oddsintel_picks_csv.py` writes `ledger/picks_oddsintel.csv` from `simulated_bets` (536 rows) using the same filter as the audit `our_stats` query. **(3)** New `scripts/build_matched_picks_csv.py` fuzzy-joins the joinable sources (OddsIntel + Forebet + SignalOdds — DeepBetting/WinnerOdds/Tipstrr don't publish per-pick team names) on `(kickoff_date, market, home_team, away_team)` via rapidfuzz `token_set_ratio >= 85`. Output `ledger/picks_matched.csv` is 3,433 rows: 100 fixtures where 2+ sources overlap (fully verifiable side-by-side rows), rest are competitor-only or OddsIntel-only. **The 1.5% Forebet ∩ OddsIntel overlap is the real story:** Forebet publishes heavily on South American + Nordic + New Zealand lower leagues that our production bots skip; on shared dates (32 in-window) we and Forebet pick almost totally different fixtures. That reframes the Forebet head-to-head — we're not "losing to Forebet at +12.91% vs +6.19%" on the same picks, we're picking different leagues in the same window. **(4)** Landing page (`odds-intel-web/src/app/page.tsx`) gets a small "Verify · download fixture-by-fixture picks CSV ↗" link right under the head-to-head hero rows, plus a "per-source picks_*.csv" link pointing at `github.com/msellin/odds-intel-engine/tree/main/ledger`. **(5) INPLAY-EXCLUDED FROM OUR COHORT**: the same commit adds `AND b.name NOT LIKE 'inplay_%%'` to every `our_stats` query — the head-to-head compares against pre-match tipsters, so in-play strategies aren't apples-to-apples. Our headline dropped from +12.76% n=780 → **+6.19% n=536** as a result. Honest but harsh: WinnerOdds pre-match at +6.14% is essentially tied with us; Forebet's +12.91% beats us. **(6)** Daily audit workflow now runs `dump_oddsintel_picks_csv.py` + `build_matched_picks_csv.py` after the 5 audits and commits `ledger/picks_*.csv` alongside `comparison_*.json`. Smoke `COMPETITOR-PICKS-CSV-2026-08-01` pins all 4 audit scripts emit their CSV, all 5 our_stats queries filter out inplay, the dump script exists, and the matrix builder exists. **Followup**: Forebet-vs-OddsIntel pattern analysis (task filed) — cross-reference the 27 shared fixtures + look at league distribution + market split (`1x2` vs `over_under_25`) to see if Forebet's edge is real or comes from taking wide books, and whether OU vs 1X2 is dragging our headline down.

> **✅ COMPETITOR-SCRAPES-WEEKLY-2026-08-01 Done 2026-08-01** — Landing head-to-head hero had been comparing a growing OddsIntel cohort against 5-week-frozen competitor snapshots (last actual scrape was 2026-06-24/25). The daily audit cron re-ran the arithmetic but never re-fetched competitor picks, so `their_stats` blocks in `ledger/comparison_*.json` for DeepBetting/SignalOdds/Forebet/Tipstrr were byte-identical across six commits since Jun 24, and WinnerOdds was stuck at "→ Jun 25" from a hardcoded `WINDOW_END_DEFAULT = "2026-06-25"` in `audit_vs_winnerodds.py`. **Fix in three parts:** (1) new `.github/workflows/competitor_scrapes_weekly.yml` runs the 4 scrape scripts every Sunday 01:00 UTC (before the existing 02:00 UTC audit cron, so same-day audit consumes fresh input); WinnerOdds intentionally not included — its audit already fetches via GraphQL each run. (2) `audit_vs_winnerodds.py` `WINDOW_END_DEFAULT` now derives from `date.today() + timedelta(days=1)` so the window grows daily. (3) `scrape_forebet.py` now merges fresh picks into any existing snapshot (dedup on `(match_date, market, match_name)`) — Forebet's public date-strip has shrunk from the doc-comment's ~40 days to ~7 days, so a plain overwrite wiped ~5 weeks of accumulated coverage each run. **Ran scrapes locally + re-audit against the tunneled VPS DB.** New landing numbers (competitor ROI · vs. frozen state on the right for comparison): DeepBetting **n=313 ROI −7.17%** (was 235 / −9.15% since 2026-06-24), Forebet **n=1,832 ROI +12.91%** (was 1,434 / +15.33%), Tipstrr **n=335 ROI −5.21%** (was 209 / −5.22%), WinnerOdds **n=1,366 ROI +6.15%** with window → 2026-08-02 (was 1,156 / +6.73% frozen at Jun 25), SignalOdds unchanged at 1,157 / −0.44% (SignalOdds 429-blocked our IP mid-scrape locally — GitHub Actions IP will refresh it next Sunday). Our cohort n dropped 1,020 → 780 because inplay_e / inplay_p_v2 were retired 2026-07-31 and their maturity_label='retired' now excludes them from the audit filter (methodologically correct — the cohort represents "currently active" strategies). Smoke `COMPETITOR-SCRAPES-WEEKLY-2026-08-01` pins all three: workflow existence + Sunday cron + four scrape script references; winnerodds default uses `date.today()`; forebet merge includes match_date and match_name. **Followup B (deferred until we confirm the Monday audit really moves competitor numbers):** per-pick CSV export + `/verify` link under the landing table so users can download the underlying rows.

> **✅ MODEL-CALIBRATION-BATCH-2026-07-31 Done 2026-07-31** — Two quantified, backtest-driven calibration tweaks shipped from the 07-31 model-scouting audit. Both are veto/gate changes, no retrain needed. **(1) PIN-ANCHOR-GAP-MID-BAND-2026-07-31** — 60d audit found the 0.06-0.10 anchor-gap band losing -13.85% ROI (1X2) / -14.81% (OU) on ~47% of stake (~$5,346 of $11,376), while the >0.10 band WINS +7.63% / +6.79%. Losses cluster in the "somewhat overconfident" middle, not the "wildly overconfident" tail the 0.12 veto was built for. Added a mid-band gate BEFORE the existing PIN veto: 1X2/OU bets with 0.06 ≤ (cal_prob − pinnacle_anchor) < 0.10 must clear a +2pp higher edge floor to pass. Env-gated via `ANCHOR_GAP_MID_BAND_ENABLED` (default true) for rollback. Expected uplift: **+2-3pp portfolio ROI**. Verifiable via `simulated_bets` replay on n=863 in the affected band. **(2) TIER-C-T3PLUS-GATE-EXPAND-2026-07-31** — Original TIER-C-T3PLUS-GATE (2026-06-30) added +3pp for T3/T4 across markets. Post-gate audit showed T3 1X2 still at -24.30% ROI (n=170), T4 at -22.86% (n=176) — mean edge on those bets was +3.6pp/+0.3pp (below the friction floor at these tiers). Bumped 1X2-specific gate to +5pp; other markets stay +3pp because they don't show the same T3/T4 degradation. Expected uplift: **+2.5pp on 1X2 portfolio**. Combined expected uplift ~+5pp. Smokes: PIN-ANCHOR-GAP-MID-BAND-2026-07-31, TIER-C-T3PLUS-GATE-EXPAND-2026-07-31. Rollback for #1: `ANCHOR_GAP_MID_BAND_ENABLED=false` env + restart. Rollback for #2: one-line commit revert. **Re-eval in 30 days** — if either move regresses, revert.

> **✅ LANDING-COMP-STALENESS-FIX-2026-07-31 Done 2026-07-31** (odds-intel-web) — Landing head-to-head hero was serving pre-`COMPETITOR-AUDIT-DB-TUNNEL-2026-07-31` data (May 4 → Jul 13, +11.94% n=1,060) despite the workflow now producing fresh JSONs daily. Root cause: `loadCompetitors()` in `src/app/page.tsx:155` had `next: { revalidate: 21600 }` (6h ISR). Combined with the daily audit cron that meant up to 30h of staleness in the worst case. Dropped to `1800` (30 min). Also purged `.next/cache/fetch-cache/` on VPS + rebuild + `pm2 restart` to bust the pre-fix ISR entries immediately. Post-deploy live check: hero card now shows +11.24% ROI n=1,021 May 4 → Aug 1, audit Jul 31 — fresh.

> **✅ PQ-CLEANUP-2026-07-31 Done 2026-07-31** — Audit-driven cleanup of stale + superseded open items. **Closed as ❌ Superseded**: (1) `BOT-BTTS-ALL-WIDEN-EXPERIMENT` (line 996) — the "widen experiment" was designed against RAW-edge 12%; root cause turned out to be Platt calibration compressing edges to 4-8%, fixed 2026-07-19 by `BTTS-CALIBRATION-GAP-LOOSEN` (12% → 7% edge floor). Trigger condition ("≥30 post-reactivation settled bets, ROI < 0%") never met — volume was ~0. (2) `BOT-OU25-OVER-WIDEN-EXPERIMENT` (line 997) — piggybacked on BTTS above; OU also now tracked separately via the `MODEL_VERSION_OU` override lineage (v20260621 → v20260719 as of 07-31). (3) `BOT-OU15-OVER-EXPLORE` (line 998) — folded into `ACCA-LEG-SHADOW-EVAL-REPEAT` as a sub-bullet (same shadow-eval mechanism, same 2026-09-01 gate). **Closed as ❌ Stale**: (4) `COOLBET-LEAGUE-INGEST` (line 1084) — filed 2026-05-20, 72 days no movement, blocker (Imperva TLS-fingerprint 403 on POST endpoint) unchanged, no downstream consumer waiting (per-match fuzzy search still works). **Refiled**: `BTTS-EDGE-RESWEEP-CALIBRATED-P2` below — preserves the actionable followup from the 07-19 loosen that would have been lost when the widen-experiment closed.

> **⬜ BTTS-EDGE-RESWEEP-CALIBRATED-P2 Filed 2026-07-31 (P2)** — 1-2h — `BTTS-CALIBRATION-GAP-LOOSEN-2026-07-19` provisionally loosened `bot_btts_all` edge threshold 12% → 7% across all tiers because the original 12% was set against RAW-edge but production applies Platt calibration that compresses edges to 4-8%. The 7% is a stopgap; a proper re-sweep against CALIBRATED edges (using `simulated_bets.calibrated_prob − 1/odds` as the edge signal, not the raw model_probability) is the correct follow-up. Wait until `bot_btts_all` has ≥50 settled bets at the new 7% threshold before running — expected sometime late-Aug once European leagues resume BTTS-friendly fixtures. Same script pattern as `scripts/per_bot_edge_threshold_sweep.py`; adapt to read calibrated edges. Decision output: keep 7%, tighten (e.g. 8-9%) if calibrated ROI curve peaks higher, or loosen further to 5% if the calibration compression is more severe than we thought.

> **✅ PAUSE-INPLAY-E-2026-07-31 Done 2026-07-31** — Retired `inplay_e` after investigation revealed the 07-31-filed model-regression was a misdiagnosis. **The bot doesn't consume `predictions.model_probability`** — it computes its own Bayesian posterior from live xG + pace ratio. `simulated_bets.model_version` is just a time-bucket tag written by `_active_model_version()`. The real cause is a fixture-mix shift post-2026-06-07 as European regular season ended: post-Jun-07 breakdown is WC 2026 -54% ROI (n=10), Brazil Serie A/B -66.7% (n=6), club-only -20.7% (n=18); pre-Jun-07 club was +6.4% (n=209). Also avg odds dropped 1.98 → 1.75 — market efficiently priced the "dead game under" pattern in low-goal-prior fixtures (South American summer, MLS, WC). Same fixture-mix pattern also degraded `inplay_j` (+10.0% n=7 → -52.8% n=22 on new-model window) and `inplay_p_v2` (already paused 07-31). `inplay_l` stayed stable because it doesn't share the low-goal thesis. Pause via DB `is_active=false + retired_at + maturity_label='retired'` + config comment-out at `workers/jobs/inplay_bot.py:118`. **Un-pause after 2026-08-15 European season restart + apply INPLAY-E-FIXTURE-FILTER.** Closes `⬜ INPLAY-E-MODEL-VERSION-REGRESSION-2026-07-31` — no model-version pin needed (would be a no-op, bot has no hook for it).

> **⛔ INPLAY-E-FIXTURE-FILTER — CLOSED 2026-09-03, superseded by INPLAY-RETIRED.** The whole in-play fleet is retired: n=1,246 settled at ROI −0.31%, t=−0.07, and −20.76% when priced at odds actually live at pick time. `inplay_e` was the best of them at +3.38% (t=0.56, n=247) — still indistinguishable from zero, so tuning its fixture filter would have been polishing a bot with no demonstrated edge. Reviving in-play is now a product decision (re-enable `INPLAY_STRATEGIES_ENABLED` plus the AF quota gates), not a filter change. Original text below.

> **~~⬜ INPLAY-E-FIXTURE-FILTER~~ Filed 2026-07-31 (P2)** — the whole in-play fleet has been silent since 2026-08-21 (last pick across all 18 in-play bots), so un-pausing `inplay_e` today would produce zero picks. **That silence is NOT a fault**: `AF-QUOTA-REALLOCATION` shipped 2026-08-21 (7179abf, efbe538) and gated in-play polling off by default via `INPLAY_LIVE_ODDS_POLL_ENABLED` and `INPLAY_STATS_EVENTS_POLL_ENABLED`. So this ticket is blocked on a **product decision, not a bug**: reviving any in-play bot means re-enabling those env gates and re-accepting the AF quota cost that was deliberately cut. Decide whether in-play has a future before spending time on `_check_strategy_e`'s fixture filter. The filters themselves are still absent (`inplay_bot.py:1931-2020`) and the config still commented out (`inplay_bot.py:118-125`). — 1-2h — Concrete fix for the inplay_e fixture-mix regression that PAUSE-INPLAY-E worked around. Two filter additions in `_check_strategy_e` (`workers/jobs/inplay_bot.py:1912-2020`): **(1) competition exclusion** — reject WC 2026, Copa America, international friendlies, CONMEBOL club comps (drops 12/30 post-Jun-07 bets at -47.5% combined ROI). **(2) prematch OU 2.5-under odds floor** ≥1.85 (post-June avg 1.75; below this the "dead game" edge is already market-priced). Expected uplift ~15pp ROI recovery. Do this **after** 2026-08-15 season restart so we have fresh European club fixtures to test against — otherwise we're tuning against a starved window. Shared helper opportunity: `inplay_j` shows the same fixture-mix regression pattern (+10.0% → -52.8% window-over-window) so the competition-filter helper could serve both. Deferred to post-August because premature filter engineering on n=30 bets is over-fitting.

> **✅ DNB-ZOMBIE-DIAGNOSIS-2026-07-31 Done 2026-07-31** — Ran the just-shipped funnel instrumentation against a live shadow-mode pipeline pass. **Result**: bot_dnb_specialist is not broken, it's seasonally starved. Today's 32 candidate matches ALL dropped at `drop_league_name_filter` (zero fixtures in the 10 whitelisted leagues — Austria Bundesliga, Mexico Liga MX, Russia PL, Israel Liga Leumit, Uruguay Segunda, England League Two/Championship, Sweden Allsvenskan, Brazil Serie B, Argentina Primera Nacional).
