> ## 2026-06-06 — COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER
>
> **✅ COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER Done 2026-06-06** — User flagged Shanghai Port II vs Shanghai Second (China League Two, 2026-06-06 11:00 EET, bot_high_alignment double_chance/x2) showing `⚠ no market` in /admin/place even though Coolbet's site clearly listed the fixture (`Shanghai Port B - Shanghai Segenda`, event 5609979) with the Topeltvõimalus market. Root cause: Coolbet's `/s/sbgate/sports/search/v2` returns events across every sport (each carries `sport_id` / `sport_category_id`), and `_do_search` was passing the raw payload straight to `_parse_event`. The basketball game `Shanghai - Liaoning` (id 5620895, `sport_category_id=65035`) leaked into the fuzzy-match candidate pool, and `fuzz.partial_ratio` scored both teams 100 (`"shanghai"` is a full substring of `"shanghai port ii"` AND of `"shanghai second"` — basketball "Shanghai vs Liaoning" → min(100, 100) = 100). The football match scored only ~86 so basketball won. The placer then fetched the basketball event's `Match Point Handicap` / `Match Total Points` markets, dropped every market as unknown in `parse_market`, and wrote only the 1X2 presence-marker snapshot — leaving `coolbetOdds=null` for DC/x2 and the frontend correctly degrading to `no_market`. DB confirms: 15 Coolbet snapshots for this match, all `1x2` (no DC, no AH, no OU, no BTTS), while the actual football event offers 26 markets including DC. Fix: filter `raw_events` in `_do_search` to only `sport_category_id == _FOOTBALL_CATEGORY_ID` (62) before parsing, so non-football candidates never reach fuzzy_match_event. Verified with probe: now matches `Shanghai Port B vs Shanghai Segenda` at score 86, resolves DC/x2 → odds 2.17. Smoke: COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER.
>
> **✅ COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER-FOLLOWUP Done 2026-06-15 — operator was right, root cause is bot universe** — Built two diagnostic scripts (`scripts/coolbet_market_mix_audit.py` + `scripts/coolbet_early_ko_drilldown.py`, re-runnable any time). 14d window, pre-match settled bets only.
>
> **Hypothesis (a) cohort timing — WRONG.** Picks created at 04 UTC (the morning cohort, 168 picks/day) are 44% 1x2 / 35% AH / 8% DC. 1x2 IS the dominant market at the morning cohort — the cohort itself isn't biased.
>
> **Hypothesis (c) data-side gaps — WRONG.** Pinnacle coverage is full: 1517/1522/1349 matches for 1x2/AH/OU25 over the window. Not a data availability problem.
>
> **Hypothesis (b) bot universe — CORRECT, by a wide margin.** When bucketed by **kickoff time** (not pick-creation time), the operator's observation is confirmed:
> | KO bin | n matches | % with ONLY AH/DC bets |
> |---|---|---|
> | early-KO (00-11 UTC) | 64 | **56%** |
> | midday-KO (12-15 UTC) | 70 | 51% |
> | late-aft-KO (16-18 UTC) | 120 | 33% |
> | evening-KO (19-23 UTC) | 80 | 26% |
>
> The dominant 1x2/OU bot **bot_v10_all** (calibrated, the only 1x2/OU producer at scale) fires **15 early-KO picks vs 61 late-aft** — 4× fewer at early KO. **bot_conservative** and **bot_proven_leagues_v2** produce ZERO early-KO picks. Meanwhile **bot_ah_home_fav** is biased to early-KO (26 early vs 7 evening — lower-tier leagues with early kickoffs are where home-favourite AH bets cluster). Result: an early-KO match typically gets ONE bet from ONE bot (avg distinct markets per match: 1.02 early, 1.13 late) — and that one bot is overwhelmingly bot_ah_home_fav or the AH side of bot_high_alignment.
>
> **Not a bug, but a model-coverage observation.** v10's training universe under-represents the leagues that play early-KO matches. The fix isn't a placer/queue change — it's a model coverage change (or accepting that early-KO matches just have less diverse signal). Smoke `COOLBET-MARKET-MIX-AUDIT` pins script existence + query shape so a future refactor can't drop a key axis. Follow-up filed below — `V10-EARLY-KO-COVERAGE-INVESTIGATE`.
>
> **⬜ V10-EARLY-KO-COVERAGE-INVESTIGATE** — Surfaced 2026-06-15 by COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER-FOLLOWUP audit. `bot_v10_all` (calibrated, dominant 1x2/OU producer) fires 4× less at early-KO matches (00-11 UTC kickoff) than at late-afternoon. `bot_conservative` and `bot_proven_leagues_v2` produce zero early-KO picks at all. Together this drives the 56% "only AH/DC" rate for early-KO matches. Investigate: (a) does v10's edge threshold + bot_v10_all's league filter combination systematically exclude early-KO leagues? (b) is the v10 training set biased to top-tier leagues that play afternoon/evening? (c) check `match_feature_vectors` coverage for early-KO matches — are signals systematically thinner? Effort ~2h. Not urgent — operator now knows the cause, and AH-only is acceptable; this just opens diversity if there's a no-cost fix.

> ## 2026-06-04 — COOLBET-INGEST-ANON
>
> **✅ COOLBET-INGEST-ANON Done 2026-06-04** — User flagged France vs Ivory Coast (Coolbet event 5575227, live on their site) showing `⚠ no match` across every AH line in `/admin/place`. Root cause: `coolbet_explorer.run_bulk()`, `run_league_sweep()`, and the single-match CLI debug path all defaulted to authed `CoolbetSession()`. When `COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT` expired on 2026-05-28, the 30-min snapshot job (`:03/:33 UTC`) started raising `RuntimeError: COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT is expired` before any `search/v2` call — so no Coolbet `odds_snapshots` got written for any fixture added after that date. The frontend's `matchIdsWithCoolbetEvent` set was empty → every row fell through to `no_event` (`engine-data.ts:1929`). But Coolbet's `search/v2`, `fo-category`, `fo-match`, and `sidebets` endpoints all work with Imperva cookies only — `--record` mode (placer) has been using `require_auth=False` since COOLBET-ANON-READ (commit `673b9e1`). The ingest path just never got the same treatment. Switched all three explorer entry points + `scripts/coolbet_match_probe.py` to `CoolbetSession(require_auth=False)`. Verified with the live probe: `France vs Ivory Coast` matches at score 100, `resolve(asian_handicap, away -1)` → odds 3.42 placement target. Placer's actual bet POST keeps the authed default (only path that genuinely needs a live cbauth). Smoke: COOLBET-INGEST-ANON (pins all four sites + the `require_auth` short-circuit in `_ensure_auth`).

> ## 2026-06-04 — CSV-FULL-EXTRACT + DRIFT-FEATURE + AH-BOT-PROTOTYPE
>
> **✅ CSV-FULL-EXTRACT Done 2026-06-04** — Audit found 479 football-data.co.uk CSVs on disk with 120 columns/row but `extract_odds_rows()` only stored 4 (Pinnacle + Bet365 1X2 closing + OU 2.5 closing). Rewrote to capture 9 bookmakers (added Betfair Exchange, Max/Avg consensus, BetWin, Betfred, William Hill, 1xBet) across 1X2/OU2.5/AH markets, closing **and** opening lines, with `handicap_line` set on AH rows. Also backfilled match stats (HS/HST/HC/HY/HR/HF + away counterparts) into `match_stats` and `matches.referee` where AF was NULL. Perf rewrite to hit usable throughput: bulk in-memory match-lookup (~150x faster than per-row find_match), COPY for the big odds_snapshots insert (~3-5x faster than execute_values), single pinned connection per league-season, scoped existing-snapshot dedup query. Found + fixed Pinnacle prefix bug in extraction (1X2 uses PSC/PS but OU/AH uses PC/P) via smoke test before publishing the ingest. Final row deltas: ~118K Betfair Exchange (was 0), ~80K Max, ~80K Avg, ~18K Pinnacle AH closing with line, ~23K Bet365 AH closing with line, ~17K Betfair Exchange AH closing with line, plus opening rows across the same books. Backtest results in `dev/active/csv-full-extract-backtest-results.md`: (a) **Pinnacle vs Betfair Exchange as shrinkage anchor — identical to 4 decimals** (Brier 0.5886/0.5887 on 7,328 paired matches), no anchor swap warranted; (b) **AH market efficient** at flat stakes (home −5.4%, away +0.9% over 8,868 matches) — but the backtest universe now exists for AH bot development; (c) **Pinnacle open→close drift produces a +8.76pp home win-rate spread top vs bottom quintile** on 8,850 matches — strong monotonic signal worth materialising as a meta-model feature. Smoke: CSV-FULL-EXTRACT (extract_odds_rows + extract_match_stats unit test).
>
> **✅ DRIFT-FEATURE Done 2026-06-04** — Migration 179 adds `pinnacle_drift_home/draw/away` to `match_feature_vectors`. New `scripts/backfill_pinnacle_drift.py` joins is_opening + is_closing Pinnacle 1X2 rows and computes `close_implied − open_implied` per selection, bulk UPDATEs MFV in one round-trip via VALUES table. Distinct from existing `pinnacle_line_move_*_at_t6h` (migration 128) which uses a ≤T−6h cutoff that excludes CSV closing snapshots timestamped at kickoff. Open question for follow-up (DRIFT-FEATURE-APPLY): once backfill runs against the ~8,850-match CSV-historical population, add the new column to `FEATURE_COLS` in `workers/model/train.py` so the next Sunday weekly retrain picks it up. Result-doc cited the 8.76pp WR spread as the empirical justification.
>
> **✅ AH-BOT-PROTOTYPE Done 2026-06-04** — `scripts/backtest_ah_bot_prototype.py` ran on 5,254 matches with paired Pinnacle AH closing + ensemble 1X2 prediction (lines restricted to ±0.5, ±0.25, 0 — subset cleanly derivable from 1X2 alone). Result: **negative ROI at every edge threshold**, getting worse as the filter tightens (−2.65% at 0 edge → −5.01% at +10% edge). Classic noise-not-signal signature. Naive "ensemble 1X2 → AH probability" approach does not beat Pinnacle's AH market. Filed (`dev/active/ah-bot-prototype-results.md`) the negative finding plus a follow-up sketch — a real AH bot needs a dedicated goals model (Poisson + team form), lineup/injury/drift signal layers, or non-Pinnacle anchor. **AH-BOT-MODEL** (the proper goals-model variant) deferred to post-WC. Strategic value of this run: confirms Pinnacle anchor is sharp; rules out the cheap version of an AH bot before sinking model effort.
>
> **✅ DRIFT-FEATURE-APPLY Done 2026-06-04** — Migration 179 + fix-forward 183 applied. `scripts/backfill_pinnacle_drift.py` populated 260 of 10,260 paired open+close Pinnacle matches with drift values (the rest don't have MFV rows yet — MFV table starts 2026-04 via live pipeline). `train.py` gained `DRIFT_FEATURE_COLS` + `--include-drift` flag mirroring `--include-pinnacle`. `scheduler.py` weekly retrain initially passed `--include-drift` but REVERTED 2026-06-04 (see DRIFT-FEATURE-REENABLE below) to avoid confounding the June 7 / June 15 calibration + bot-threshold work with a feature that's 97% NULL at current coverage. The capability is wired; the auto-activation is paused.
>
> **⬜ DRIFT-FEATURE-REENABLE** — Re-enable `--include-drift` on the auto Sunday retrain in `workers/scheduler.py:job_weekly_retrain`. Trigger conditions: (a) the Batch 2 calibration eval (target 2026-06-15) is baselined cleanly without drift, AND (b) MFV coverage on `pinnacle_drift_home` is ≥30% of training rows (currently 2.6%). Coverage grows two ways: live pipeline writes opening + closing Pinnacle snapshots for every new match in our window, and we can build an MFV-historical backfill that fills MFV rows for the 10K CSV-paired matches. Earliest realistic re-enable date: 2026-06-22 retrain. Activation step is a 3-line edit + comment removal + commit.
>
> **✅ AH-BOT-MODEL Done 2026-06-05** — `scripts/backtest_ah_bot_model.py` ran the production Poisson + Dixon-Coles AH function (`_ah_model_prob` from daily_pipeline_v2.py) against the full 8,441-match Pinnacle AH backtest universe (all line types — whole / half / quarter). Inverts ensemble (p_h, p_d) via `_solve_lambdas_calibrated()` to recover (exp_h, exp_a), then computes fair AH probabilities at the actual handicap line. Result: **negative ROI at every edge threshold**, monotonically worsening (−2.93% at 0 edge → −4.65% at +0.10 edge). Same textbook noise-not-signal pattern as the naive prototype. Two consistent observations across both backtests: (a) ensemble systematically rates away sides higher than Pinnacle AH implied — N(away bets) ≈ 2.1× N(home bets) at zero edge, suggesting a residual away bias the Platt calibration leaves; (b) Pinnacle AH closing is too sharp for our current model. **Both the cheap path and the proper goals-model path confirm: no edge over Pinnacle AH closing.** Results in `dev/active/ah-bot-model-results.md`. **Verdict: shelve AH bot development.** Future revisit options filed (line movement / soft-book disagreement / dedicated AH goals model / lineup signals — each its own substantial project). The 8,868-match AH closing universe remains a reusable asset for any future AH model attempt.
>
> **⬜ CSV-HT-GOALS — backlog (gated on HT model decision)** — Extract half-time goals (`HTHG`/`HTAG`) from football-data.co.uk CSVs. Pre-requisite for half-time prediction markets (HT 1X2, HT/FT, first-half OU, HT BTTS), none of which are in BOTS_CONFIG today. Implementation: migration adds `ht_score_home`/`ht_score_away` to `matches`; extend `extract_match_stats()` to grab HTHG/HTAG; re-run idempotent ingest. ~30min once the HT model decision is committed. **Not actionable until HT infra is on the roadmap** — moved to backlog 2026-06-06 audit pass.

> ## 2026-06-03 — COOLBET-SELECTION-CASE
>
> **✅ COOLBET-SELECTION-CASE Done 2026-06-03** — `/admin/place` flagged every Coolbet 1X2 + DC bet as `⚠ no market` even when Coolbet had the event and the market. Root cause: `coolbet_explorer.parse_market` + `_normalise_our_target` emitted Title-case selections (`Home`/`Away`/`Draw`/`1X`/`X2`) that got written into `odds_snapshots`, but the frontend's `_mapPaperToSnapshotKey` lowercases (`home`/`away`/`1x`/`x2`) to match every OTHER bookmaker's storage convention. Lookup missed → `coolbetOdds = null` → match had a Coolbet snapshot row so badge fell through to `no_market`. Confirmed with Olympic vs Lund (Sweden / Ettan - Södra, KO 2026-06-03 17:00 UTC, bot_v10_all 1x2 home @ 2.52): Coolbet snapshot `1x2/Home @ 2.33` captured 07:14 UTC today, but frontend showed ⚠ no market. DB-wide: 2,800 Coolbet rows (1x2 + double_chance) had capital case; every other bookmaker was already lowercase. Fix: `parse_market` + `_normalise_our_target` + `_outcome_id_for_selection` + `_write_presence_marker_snapshot` now emit/consume lowercase across 1X2 + DC; one-off `UPDATE odds_snapshots SET selection = LOWER(selection)` backfilled the existing 2,800 rows. New smoke COOLBET-SELECTION-CASE pins parse_market + _normalise_our_target + end-to-end resolve. Updated smokes: COOLBET-MARKET-NORM, COOLBET-ODDS-SNAPSHOT, COOLBET-MARKET-TYPE-IDS, COOLBET-NO-MARKET-PRESENCE, DC-CASE-AND-RESULTKEY-FIX.

Reuses the existing `match_previews` table (migration 033) — distinct match_ids vs. the club preview job so no clash; the match-detail page's existing `getMatchPreview()` picks up WC previews automatically. New cron registered at `07:30 UTC` daily, gated inside `job_wc_match_previews` to `2026-06-04 ≤ today ≤ 2026-07-19` so APScheduler doesn't burn Gemini quota the rest of the year. Idempotent — fixtures whose preview is < 24h old are skipped; `--force` regenerates everything in the window. Rate-limited to 1 req/sec; Gemini errors caught per-fixture (loop never crashes). Frontend: new `getWorldCupPreviews()` batch-loader in `src/lib/world-cup.ts`; `/world-cup` schedule + group cards render a native `<details>` "AI preview" expander under each fixture row (collapsed by default, free to all tiers — lead-gen SEO content). Cost: ~728 calls × ~$0.01 ≈ **$7 per tournament cycle**. Deferred: post-match recap (1-2 sentences); multi-language. Smoke: WC-AI-PREVIEW-JOB + WC-AI-PREVIEW-CRON + WC-AI-PREVIEW-FE.

> ## 2026-06-02 — WORLD-CUP-PREP spike done; phased plan filed; Pro tier rethink deferred
>
> 9 days from kickoff. Spike (2026-06-02) confirmed: AF has 72 WC group-stage fixtures available via `get_fixtures_by_league_season(1, 2026)` but **zero are currently in our DB** (pipeline only pulls today's date). National-team data thin (73 friendlies in DB; AF has Euro 2024 + WC 2022 + WC quals ~320 matches available to backfill). Existing club-trained model won't handle national teams — needs separate ELO + Poisson predictor. No bookmaker odds (AF says no, Odds API not configured for WC). Pro tier rethink ran in parallel — Top-N hypothesis killed (-10% ROI over 3 years), "95% bankers" killed (model calibration ceiling at 66% WR), bot-maturity + prob-threshold intersection is the best lead (+10.36% ROI n=314 backtest, but needs re-validation against live simulated_bets). **All findings written to `dev/active/world-cup-prep-{plan,context,tasks}.md` + `dev/active/pro-tier-rethink-findings.md`** so we don't lose state across context compressions.
>
> **✅ WC-PHASE-1 Done 2026-06-02** — Backfilled 72 WC 2026 group-stage fixtures. Added `--league`/`--season` CLI to `workers/jobs/fetch_fixtures.py` (backfill mode skips daily-featured + ops_snapshot, passes today's date as log run_date to satisfy DATE column). Migration 163 flips `show_on_frontend=true` on the WC league row (was hidden despite `is_active=true` + `coverage_predictions=true`). Matches land with `season=2025` (our football-season convention: June = previous year) — this is correct and the frontend filters by date+`show_on_frontend`, not season. Smoke: WC-FIXTURES-IN-DB.
>
> **✅ WC-PHASE-2 Done 2026-06-02** — `scripts/backfill_internationals.py` ran across 59 (league, season) tuples, **6,921 fixtures stored (6,651 finished)** covering WC 2018/2022, Euro 2020/2024 + qual, Copa America, AFCON, Asian Cup, Gold Cup, all 4 UEFA Nations League editions, all 6 confederations' WC qualifiers for 2022 + 2026 cycles, Friendlies 2022-25, regional comps. Phase A (fixtures) complete; Phase B (lineups/events/stats/players) runs at ~4 matches/min because of per-match SQL fan-out — left running in background (~25h ETA), but Phase 3 (ELO + Poisson predictor) only needs scores + dates + teams which are all already in. Follow-up `OPT-BACKFILL-INTL` filed in `dev/active/world-cup-prep-tasks.md` to parallelise the enrichment post-WC. Smoke: INTL-BACKFILL.
>
> **✅ WC-PHASE-3 Done 2026-06-02** — National-team predictor (separate parallel model — club model's league/season features don't map to internationals). Stack: ELO walked over 6,651 finished international matches (K=40 for tournament / K=25 for qualifier+NL / K=10 for friendly, home_advantage +60 only on qualifier_nl); Poisson goals model from last-20-internationals weighted by competition. **1X2 backtest on 141 holdout matches (WC 2022 + Euro 2024 + Copa 2024 + AFCON 2023): log-loss 1.0697 vs 1.0986 baseline (+2.6% improvement), 45.4% top-pick accuracy (vs 33.3%), Euro 2024 hit rate 63.6%, WC 2022 48.4%.** Best config from sweep: softening_factor=1.3, draw_base=0.30. Goals model is calibrated but doesn't beat 50/50 baseline meaningfully — **ship 1X2 only; goals shown as informational, not as confidence claims**. Files: `workers/model/national_team_predictor.py`, `scripts/compute_international_elo.py`, `scripts/backtest_national_team_model.py`, migration 164 (`team_elo_international`). ELO ranking sanity-checks well: Spain #1, Morocco #2, France #3, England #4, Argentina #5 at the top; Andorra/Gibraltar/San Marino/Liechtenstein at the bottom. Smoke: NATIONAL-TEAM-PREDICTOR-V1.

> **✅ SEASON-CONVENTION-FIX Done 2026-06-02** — `bulk_store_matches` + `store_match` used to compute `season` from match_date alone (Jan-Jun → year-1, Jul-Dec → year). Correct for club leagues (PL 2024-25 = season=2024) but inverts the year for summer tournaments — WC 2026 fixtures (June 2026) landed as season=2025, WC 2018 split between 2017 + 2018, Euro 2024 partially under 2023. > ## 2026-06-01 — Cherry-pick Phase 2 open questions (decide 2026-06-05/06)
>
> User confirmed direction: real_bets should follow most-profitable bots / profiles, decision target = post-Phase-3.5 (Sunday 2026-06-07). Two unresolved design choices need a call BEFORE the 06-07 env flip, otherwise the gate behaves in ways we didn't intend.
>
> **Q1 — bot-level vs profile-level gate.** Phase 1 filter is `bots.maturity_label = ANY(...)`, per-bot. But several active bots have multiple `strategy_profile` values (bot_ou_specialist has Under 2.5 Specialist + Over 2.5 Sweden + Over 3.5 Global; bot_dnb_specialist had DNB Home + DNB Away). Per-bot promotion includes profiles that haven't earned it (e.g. Over 3.5 Global is unproven). Per-profile promotion would need a new column or a `(bot_id, strategy_profile)` allowlist. Recommend: per-bot in Phase 1, add per-profile in Phase 2 only if a multi-profile bot has clear winning + losing profile splits in shadow_bets. Filed as ⬜ open.
>
> **Q2 — starting allowlist is currently too narrow.** Today only 3 *actively-firing* bots are tagged `calibrated`: bot_v10_all (strong), bot_1x2_specialist (n=4 last 30d, marginal), inplay_e (n=215 / +8% ROI). Flipping `COOLBET_RECORD_ALLOWED_MATURITY=calibrated` on 06-08 would shrink real_bets to ~5-10 bets/day. Two paths to widen sensibly:
>   • Promote 2-3 more bots to `calibrated` via `/admin/promotion-candidates` (Phase 2 ships 06-05/06). Likely candidates per today's data: bot_aggressive_v2 (+57% last 7d but n=8 — needs more samples), bot_high_roi_global_v2 (+35% last 30d on n=12), bot_opt_home_lower (+52% n=20 last 30d).
>   • Start with `active,calibrated` as the env value; tighten to `calibrated` only after a week of clean data.
> Recommend path 1 (manual promotion) so the maturity_label semantics stay tight — but the decision waits until promotion criteria are written in Phase 2.
>
> Both questions land in `dev/active/cherry-pick-placer-plan.md` for the Phase 2 work.

> ## 2026-06-01 — EMAIL-DELIVERY-CHECK ran live; DMARC gap surfaced
>
> Verified Resend deliverability for `oddsintel.app` via the rewritten `scripts/check_email_deliverability.py`. Script now uses Resend's `/domains` API as authoritative record list (avoids guessing selector / subdomain) and cross-checks each expected record against live DNS. Live run:
>   • ✅ DKIM TXT at `resend._domainkey.oddsintel.app` (root) — matches Resend key
>   • ✅ SPF MX at `send.oddsintel.app` → `feedback-smtp.eu-west-1.amazonses.com`
>   • ✅ SPF TXT at `send.oddsintel.app` → `v=spf1 include:amazonses.com ~all`
>   • **❌ DMARC missing** at both root and `send` subdomain — recommended fix surfaced in script output
>
> **Action item for operator** (Cloudflare DNS — manual): add TXT record:
>   ```
>   _dmarc.oddsintel.app  TXT  "v=DMARC1; p=none; rua=mailto:postmaster@oddsintel.app; pct=100"
>   ```
> Start with `p=none` (monitoring-only — no impact on delivery). After 14d of clean DMARC aggregate reports, raise to `p=quarantine`. Major mailbox providers (Gmail, Outlook, Yahoo) increasingly require DMARC for senders >5K msgs/day. We're well below that, but with the Telegram alerts + value bet emails this trip threshold faster than expected. Original script had bugs (looked for DKIM as CNAME when Resend uses TXT, checked SPF on root domain when Resend uses `send` subdomain) — both fixed. Smoke: EMAIL-DELIVERY-CHECK.

> ## 2026-06-01 — SCHEDULER-HANG-MITIGATION post-mortem + partial fix
>
> Root cause of the 14:35 UTC scheduler hang identified. APScheduler is configured with `max_workers=4` in the default executor (workers/scheduler.py:1161). Three long-running jobs share the same 30-min firing minute under that constraint:
>   • `betting_refresh_interval` — fires `:05/:35` every hour 07-22 UTC
>   • `shadow_interval` — fires `:05/:35` every hour 07-22 UTC
>   • `coolbet_odds_interval` — fires `:03/:33` every hour 07-22 UTC (often still running into `:05/:35`)
>
> Plus `manual_placement_drain` (every 10s) and `hist_backfill` / `backfill_coaches` / `backfill_transfers` (every 25 min). On a normal day this is fine because each long-running job finishes in 1-5 min; but if any one of them hangs on a shared resource (Coolbet session lock waiting on Imperva refresh, AF rate-limit semaphore, or DB pool exhaustion), the others queue up. Once three of four worker threads are occupied, the fourth typically also blocks on the same shared resource — full deadlock. APScheduler doesn't kill long-running jobs; the rows stayed in `pipeline_runs.status='running'` for 22+ minutes until Railway's auto-redeploy reset them.
>
> **Partial fix shipped today** (workers/scheduler.py):
>   1. **Staggered `shadow_interval` to :10/:40** (was :05/:35). Removes the 0-second overlap between betting_refresh + shadow at the same firing minute. Worst-case overlap drops from "simultaneous" to "5 min apart". Doesn't eliminate the shared-resource bug but reduces the chance two long-running siblings collide.
>   2. **Added `EVENT_JOB_MAX_INSTANCES` listener** that logs to console + `_recent_errors` whenever APScheduler skips a fire because the previous instance is still running. On 2026-06-01 this would have produced visible warnings at 14:50, 15:05, 15:20 etc. instead of silent thread-pool exhaustion.
>
> **Still TBD** (would need Railway log access I don't have):
>   • **Identify the actual shared lock** that all three jobs waited on. Strong suspects: Coolbet `CoolbetSession.refresh_jwt_if_needed()` which retries with exponential backoff up to ~10 min; AF `RateLimitedSession` semaphore; DB pool at 20-connection ceiling.
>   • **Per-job timeout**. APScheduler has no built-in per-job timeout. Options: wrap each job body in `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor.submit(...).result(timeout=…)`, or use a signal-based watchdog (Unix only). Either requires substantial refactor; recommend deferring until the shared-lock root cause is confirmed.
>   • **Separate executor for placer-touching jobs** so they can't starve the rest of the pool. E.g. `executors={"default": ThreadPoolExecutor(4), "placer": ThreadPoolExecutor(1)}` and assign `betting_pipeline`, `betting_refresh`, `coolbet_odds_snapshot`, `shadow_interval` to `placer`. Forces them to serialize, freeing the default pool. Worth a follow-up dev/active/ plan if the hang repeats.
>
> Smoke: SCHEDULER-HANG-MITIGATION.
>
> ## 2026-06-01 — bot_high_alignment RETIREMENT-TRIGGER recorded
>
> Explicit decision locked in before context fades. `bot_high_alignment` is the current biggest paper-prematch firehose (n=210+ last 7d) and is bleeding −5% ROI. BUT it's the only bot firing on BTTS / DC / DNB markets (the multi-profile specialists are starved by data availability + tight edge thresholds — see Task A funnel diag above). Therefore: do NOT retire today. CLV is positive +8.9% so the model is finding sharp picks; the calibration miss is what v20260531 promotion is expected to fix.
>
> **Retirement trigger** (operator decision, not automated): retire `bot_high_alignment` if, after the v20260531 promotion (target Sunday 2026-06-07), BOTH conditions hold over a 7-day window starting from the promotion date:
>   • Live ROI < −2% on n ≥ 50 settled bets
>   • Avg CLV < +5% (calibration failed to recover — picks no longer sharp)
>
> **Hold trigger**: if CLV stays ≥ +5% but ROI is still negative, do NOT retire — the picks are still sharp and the variance can flip; wait another 7 days. Only retire when both signals agree.
>
> **Don't-retire-because-coverage trigger** still applies: if `bot_ou_specialist` Over 3.5 Global (now at 10% edge after today's loosen) + a DNB-coverage solution still aren't firing on BTTS/DC/DNB, hold bot_high_alignment regardless — coverage trumps performance until specialists can pick up the slack.

> Re-evaluate this rule on 2026-06-15 after the first post-promotion week. Filed as ⬜ scheduled.

> ## 2026-06-01 — OU35-EDGE-LOOSEN + RAILWAY-SCHEDULER-HUNG (incident)
>
> **RAILWAY-SCHEDULER-HUNG** — at 14:35 UTC three jobs (`betting_pipeline`, `shadow_1435`, `betting_refresh`) entered "running" state and never completed (still showing 22+ minutes runtime). After 14:45 the scheduler stopped accepting new jobs entirely. Last DB writes by major path: simulated_bets 12:08, shadow_bets 14:11, live_match_snapshots 14:56, dashboard_cache 14:48. Diagnostic via `pipeline_runs` table. **Action required: restart Railway service** (cannot trigger from local session). Suspect process death or deadlock — three jobs hung simultaneously suggests shared resource (DB pool exhaustion, AF rate limit lock, or worker thread starvation). Filed for post-restart investigation: review APScheduler timeout config + identify whether the three jobs share a thread group. ✅ **RESOLVED 2026-06-03** — Railway was restarted (exact date unrecorded). Audit on 2026-06-03 confirmed: zero zombie "running" jobs from 2026-06-01 left in pipeline_runs; current scheduler activity normal (4-5 jobs/15min completing cleanly); no current stuck jobs >30min old. Root-cause investigation deferred — pattern hasn't reappeared in the 48h since restart, so the suspected DB-pool / worker-thread starvation may have been a one-off rather than a structural deadlock. Filed lighter follow-up RAILWAY-SCHEDULER-DEADMAN below if it recurs.
>
> **OU35-EDGE-LOOSEN** — `bot_ou_specialist` "Over 3.5 Global" profile edge threshold lowered 14% → 10%. Diagnostic showed **ZERO candidates** passed 14% in 30d of shadow data, despite the bot being designed to cover the over 3.5 market globally. 14% was a 2023-2026 backtest sweep optimum; calibration drift since then means no live candidates clear that bar. 10% is the first iteration — paper bot, safe to observe live ROI. Adjustment plan in code comment: if -ROI consistent, raise back to 14%; if ROI ≥ 5% on n ≥ 30, try 8%. Takes effect after Railway scheduler restart. Smoke: OU35-EDGE-LOOSEN.
>
> ## 2026-06-01 — STALE-FLAG-AUDIT + Task A specialist funnel diag + Task B edge calibration on shadow
>
> User asked "do all three investigation tasks" (A specialist funnel, B edge calibration, C stale-flag audit) after spotting the +0.7% pre-match ROI on the hero looked inconsistent with the bot leaderboard.
>
> **Cache freshness issue**: the +0.7% the user saw came from a stale 14:15 UTC cache row (pre-bot_aggressive-retirement). Cache refresh on Railway stopped running after 14:45 UTC (3h gap). Triggered a fresh write via local run; live data shows pre-match 30d active ROI is actually **+3.38% on n=616**, much closer to the lifetime bot-weighted ~+5.9%. **Railway `dashboard_cache_refresh` cron health needs investigation** — separate issue.
>
> **Task C — stale-flag audit. Migration 162** retires 2 more bots (bot_draw_specialist, inplay_f — match the original retired_reason: -100% / -17% ROI, last fires May 13 / May 8) and clears retired_reason on 2 recovered bots (bot_conservative +104% ROI n=8 last 30d, bot_opt_home_lower +51.9% ROI n=20 — both were re-enabled via migration 122 but the stale reason text was never cleared). Smoke: STALE-FLAG-AUDIT-MIGRATION-162.
>
> **Task A — specialist funnel**: the multi-profile specialists fire MUCH less than expected:
>   • `bot_ou_specialist`: **0 simulated, 0 shadow** in 14d. Root cause: "Over 3.5 Global" profile requires 14% edge; zero candidates pass that bar in 30d shadow data. League-whitelist profiles ("Under 2.5 Specialist" + "Over 2.5 Sweden") may also see no qualifying matches.
>   • `bot_dnb_specialist`: **0 simulated, 0 shadow** in 14d. Root cause: DNB odds coverage is thin in whitelist leagues — Austria Bundesliga 0/3 matches with DNB odds, Mexico Liga MX 0/2, Russia 0/4, Israel 0/16, England Champ 0/2. Only Argentina Primera Nacional, Brazil Serie B, Sweden Allsvenskan, Uruguay Segunda have partial DNB odds coverage (~40-50% of matches). After 5% edge filter, few qualify.
>   • `bot_1x2_specialist`: 35 shadow / 4 simulated in 14d. Filter at a later cohort/eligibility step (not yet root-caused). Maturity calibrated, 30d ROI +13.1% on n=4 — too small to act on.
>
>   **Conclusion**: the specialists are real coverage placeholders but constrained by data availability + tight edge thresholds. Don't aggressively retire bot_high_alignment until the specialist filters are loosened OR the underlying DNB/OU3.5 data coverage improves. Specifically: lowering bot_ou_specialist "Over 3.5 Global" edge from 14% to 10% would surface more candidates (still needs a backtest on shadow to confirm those are profitable).
>
> **Task B — edge calibration on shadow_bets** (much larger sample, n=10K+ across buckets). Findings UPEND the earlier simulated_bets "5-12% is dead, 12-20% is sweet spot" narrative:
>   • `<5%` edge: -36.2% ROI on n=370 (consistent with simulated)
>   • `5-8%`: -5.6% on n=3,457
>   • `8-12%`: -7.6% on n=4,470
>   • **`12-20%`: -13.9% on n=1,982** (vs simulated's +20.3%)
>   • **`20%+`: +14.7% on n=328** (only universally-positive bucket)
>
>   The "12-20% is the sweet spot" was a **bot-mix artefact**, not a universal edge-bucket truth. Shadow data shows even 12-20% edge calls are mostly losing — the active bots in simulated_bets happen to be the ones whose 12-20% edge calls win. After v20260531 promotion (calibrated 1X2 head) re-evaluate; do NOT raise edge floors globally on the current model.
>

> **Change 6/6 — PERF-HERO-NEXT-MODEL — done.** New "Next upgrade" callout strip below the existing Model v2 callout. Data check from `model_versions.cv_metrics`: v20260531 beats v20260524_market on 9/11 markets (1X2 avg log_loss −10.0%, AH −2.6%, BTTS −1.3%, OU +2.7% regression). Migration 161 adds `upcoming_model_summary JSONB` to dashboard_cache. New `_build_upcoming_model_summary()` helper in settlement.py reads `MODEL_VERSION` env to identify production, finds the newest unpromoted candidate trained AFTER production with cv_metrics populated, computes per-head log_loss deltas (grouped 1x2 / ah / btts / ou), counts markets better/worse, returns None when no candidate is improving (avoids misleading callout). Frontend `NextModelCallout` in performance-hero.tsx renders the headline best-head delta + the better/worse counter + an "offline tests" caveat so we're not claiming production data we don't have. Markets_worse is surfaced so the OU regression isn't hidden — honest, not cherry-picked. Smoke: PERF-HERO-NEXT-MODEL.
>
> **Change 6b/6 — PERF-HERO-NEXT-MODEL-DETAIL — done 2026-06-06.** Callout was previously showing only the single best head ("1X2 −10.0% log-loss") — felt thin and hid the AH / BTTS wins. Rendering change in `NextModelCallout` (performance-hero.tsx) now renders every group as a colored chip in fixed order 1X2 / AH / BTTS / OU: improvements (<−1%) in sky, regressions (>+1%) in rose, ties in muted. Data is the same `summary.group_deltas` already in dashboard_cache — no DB change, no new pipeline work. Considered also surfacing retro-ROI numbers from `scripts/retro_roi_candidate_model.py` and rejected: the naive 14d run (n=268, +157pp lift) is 60% contaminated by training-window leakage (v20260531 trained through 2026-05-31, prod's bet window is 2026-05-23→2026-06-06), and the honest OOS slice (kickoff ≥ 2026-06-01) is n=31 — too thin to publish without one high-odds match swinging the headline 40pp. Decision: stick with cv_metrics, which is the same signal cleaner. Reassess if cv_metrics ever lets a regression slip past the promotion decision over the next 2–3 cycles. Smoke: PERF-HERO-NEXT-MODEL extended.
>
> **All 6 perf-page-attractiveness changes resolved**: 4 shipped (cohort split, equity sparkline, recent wins reel, next-model callout), 1 already in place (maturity badges), 1 shelved on data gate (30d rolling — would have been a no-op).

> ## 2026-06-01 — CHERRY-PICK-PLACER planned
>
> Strategic separation between paper firing (broad, all bots → simulated_bets → honest performance page + retraining volume) and real-money placement (curated subset → real_bets only from bots that pass an explicit maturity bar). Gate column = existing `bots.maturity_label`. New env var `COOLBET_RECORD_ALLOWED_MATURITY=calibrated` filters the three placer loaders (singles + combos + inplay). Defaults to no-filter so the code can ship safely before Phase 3.5 closes; gate flips to `calibrated` on Sunday 2026-06-07 after promotion criteria are written and 2-4 bots are manually promoted. Plan + context + tasks in `dev/active/cherry-pick-placer-{plan,context,tasks}.md`. Three phases: P1 code lands (06-02→06-04), P2 promotion surface + rules (06-05→06-06), P3 gate flip + 7-day monitor (Sunday 06-07+). Status: ⬜ ready to start P1.

> ## 2026-06-01 — RETIRE-DC-SPECIALIST + META-VALIDATOR-FIXES + OU-MODEL-PIN-RUNBOOK done
>
> Three follow-ups after analysing Sunday 2026-05-31's auto-retrain (`v20260531`) against production `v20260524_market`. Held-out window 2026-05-17..2026-05-31, n=5,506 matches. New bundle wins on 9/11 markets (1X2 log_loss −10%, AH −2.5–2.8%, BTTS −1.3%) and regresses **over_under +2.7%** (predicts under at 56.3% vs actual 44.4% — TIER-C-EXPAND drag, exactly what `OU-TIER-C-DRAG-AUDIT` predicted). Production prematch ROI since v2 deploy: −4.5% on 871 settled bets but **CLV +6.83%** — model is on the right side of Pinnacle, just miscalibrated. Inplay decoupled (no prematch heads on the read path): +41% ROI on 212 bets.
>
> **RETIRE-DC-SPECIALIST** — `bot_dc_specialist` paused via migration 155. −7.53% ROI on 58 settled bets since 2026-05-24, +3.68% CLV. DC is a derived market computed from 1x2 outputs; `v20260524_market` has no DC training target or Platt calibration, so the bot's "edge" is a derived-prob artefact (same root cause that retired `bot_dc_value` in migration 137). The three profiles (X2 Value, 1X Israel, DC Global) all paused — strategy_profile was NULL on all 62 production bets so we can't surgically isolate the loser, and the league-whitelist profiles inherit the same probabilistic foundation as the broad one. Re-activation trigger documented in retired_reason: Sunday June 7 retrain ships DC-specific calibration OR shadow_bets shows X2 Value / 1X Israel holding ROI > 5% on n≥30. Smoke: RETIRE-DC-SPECIALIST.
>
> **META-VALIDATOR-FIXES** — `scripts/validate_meta_b_ml3.py` had two latent bugs that surfaced on first non-trivial pre-flight (`--since 2026-05-25`, n=657 settled bets): (a) `_score_one` passed NaN-containing rows directly to LogisticRegression bundles (5 of 7 bundles rejected the matrix); training imputes MFV nulls like `form_momentum_*` to 0, inference now mirrors via `aligned.fillna(0.0)`. (b) `df["clv_used"].mean()` crashed with `Decimal + float` because psycopg2 returns numeric columns as Decimal; coerced via `pd.to_numeric(..., errors="coerce").astype(float)`. **Pre-flight verdict at n=657: all 7 bundles FAIL the 5pp gate** — top quintile CLV-beat rate is INVERSELY related to bottom quintile (Δ = −7 to −20 pp across bundles). Signal is anti-predictive on real data. Keep `META_B_ML3_ENABLED=false`; the formal verdict still runs on 2026-06-10 but the data so far says retrain v3 with more data rather than activate any current bundle. Smoke: META-VALIDATOR-FIXES.
>
> **OU-MODEL-PIN-RUNBOOK** — when v20260531 (or whichever Sunday retrain we ultimately promote) is flipped on Railway, OU must be pinned to v20260524_market via the existing per-market routing in `workers/model/xgboost_ensemble.py` (Phase C-light, 2026-05-24). Promotion env vars on Railway:
>   • `MODEL_VERSION=v20260531`  (or later)
>   • `MODEL_VERSION_OU=v20260524_market`  (pin OU to today's production)
>   • `MODEL_VERSION_GOALS=v20260524_market` (BTTS derives from joint goals matrix — pin together so BTTS doesn't quietly inherit v20260531's regressed OU head)
>   • leave `MODEL_VERSION_1X2` unset (inherits global v20260531 — the 1X2 head is the −10% log-loss win)
> Unpin OU only after `OU-TIER-C-DRAG-AUDIT` ships a fix or a later retrain (v_20260607+) reverses the regression. Smoke: OU-MODEL-PIN-RUNBOOK.
>
> ## 2026-05-30 — INPLAY-SCORE-ODDS-CONSISTENCY done
>
> User flagged a bogus +56.3% edge inplay bet on Yanbian Longding vs Changchun Yatai (1x2 away @ 4.00, min 25, claimed score 0-1). Real state was 1-1 — home equalised at 23'. Production data: at `07:25:10` our snapshot had post-goal odds (home 2.10 / away 4.00) but `score_home`/`score_away` still showed 0-1; bot fired at `07:25:19`; score column corrected at `07:25:53`. **Root cause: 43-second lag between API-Football's odds feed and score feed.** Bookmaker reacted to the goal within seconds; our score column was the laggard. Bot saw "away leading 0-1 at min 25 + away still @ 4.00" → fictional edge. Fix: two new safety checks in `inplay_bot._evaluate_candidates` candidate-eval loop:
>   • `_score_odds_consistent(cand)` — cheap in-memory check; rejects when the score-implied leader has *longer* 1X2 odds than the trailing team (physically impossible if data is fresh).
>   • `_odds_drift_recent(execute_query, match_id)` — DB lookback; rejects when any 1X2 leg moved ≥30% in the last 60s (unmistakable goal/red-card signature).
> Funnel counters added (`score_odds_inconsistent`, `odds_drift_event`) so we can see how often this fires in production. Smoke: INPLAY-SCORE-ODDS-CONSISTENCY.
>
> ## 2026-05-29 — INPLAY-RESOLVE-ARGS-FIX + CLI inplay flag done
>
> Two stacked silent-failure bugs in `place_all_inplay_bets` surfaced the moment Imperva cookies were refreshed and the placer started reaching the markets step (search_blocked had been aborting the run early on every recent attempt). (a) `resolve_placement_target` was being called with args reversed — function signature is `(markets, odds_map, our_market, our_selection)`, caller had `(mkt, sel, markets, odds_data)`. (b) The return value (a 4-tuple) was being treated as a dict with `target["market_id"]`. Both fixed. First clean inplay placement after the fix: `Shelbourne vs Galway United | o/u under 2.5 @ 1.47, edge +26%, €5 paper`. Same `place_all_inplay_bets` is the one `inplay_bot.py:511` calls per inplay-bet alert — so this also unblocks the live auto-record loop. Smoke: INPLAY-RESOLVE-ARGS. Also added `--include-inplay` / `--inplay-only` flags to `scripts/place_coolbet_bets.py` for manual catch-up.
>
> ## 2026-05-29 — MANUAL-PLACE + ADMIN-TG-CLARITY done
>
> Two stacked changes to the admin Telegram experience after user flagged that ~50 bets/day was becoming unreadable noise.
>
> **MANUAL-PLACE** — Telegram inline-keyboard button on every admin pre-match + inplay alert. Admin taps "📝 Record at Coolbet" → Vercel webhook (`/api/telegram/webhook` extended for `callback_query`) admin-gates by `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID`, inserts a row into `manual_placement_queue` (migration 153). Railway scheduler runs `_drain_manual_placement_queue` every 10s — claims pending rows via `UPDATE … WHERE status='pending' RETURNING`, calls new `coolbet_placer.place_bet_by_id(simulated_bet_id)` which routes singles/combos/inplay to the right placer with a new `bet_id_filter` parameter on each loader (bypasses the date/edge/dedup qualifying filters since admin already authorised). Idempotency: short-circuits to `already_recorded` when a `real_bets` row already exists for the bet, so double-taps + auto-record racing the button are both safe. Outcome edits the Telegram message in place. Smoke: MANUAL-PLACE.
>
> **ADMIN-TG-CLARITY** — three-message-per-cohort firehose (user broadcast → admin per-bet → batch list → coolbet --record summary) collapses to one alert + a counter. New side table `bet_telegram_alerts` (migration 154) maps `simulated_bet_id → (chat_id, message_id, original_text)`. Per-bet sites in `daily_pipeline_v2` + `inplay_bot` write the row right after `send_telegram` returns the new `message_id`. Auto-record (`_run_coolbet_record` + the inplay path) edits each per-bet alert with the outcome (`✓ Auto-recorded €X.XX` / `✗ no_event` / `⚠️ search_blocked`), removing the inline button. Also: `send_telegram_to_users` skips the admin chat id so the "🔔 New value bet" duplicate broadcast stops. Pre-match + Coolbet --record summaries collapse to single-line counters (`🎯 5 found` / `🤖 Coolbet: 3 placed · 2 no_event`), silent unless something's blocked. Smoke: ADMIN-TG-CLARITY.

> ## 2026-05-29 — COOLBET-FUZZY-CASE-INSENSITIVE done
>
> Follow-up to COMBO-FALLBACK-FO-CATEGORY — the fo-category fallback never even got reached for MyPa vs Pepo because Coolbet's `/search/v2` was already returning the correct candidate (`home="MyPa"`, `away="PEPO"`), but the fuzzy matcher was rejecting it. Production log: `Fuzzy match FAILED for 'MyPa vs Pepo' — best was 'MyPa PEPO' (score 40 < threshold 70)`. Root cause: `rapidfuzz.fuzz.partial_ratio` is case-sensitive — `partial_ratio("Pepo", "PEPO")` = 40, well below the 70 threshold. `_ascii()` was diacritic-normalising but not lowercasing. Fix: one-liner in `_ascii()` — `.lower()` at the end. Both query and candidate names already flow through `_ascii()` symmetrically, so the change is safe. Smoke: COOLBET-FUZZY-CASE-INSENSITIVE.
>
> ## 2026-05-29 — COMBO-FALLBACK-FO-CATEGORY done
>
> User flagged that a Coolbet `--record` run skipped 4 combos with `leg 2: no Coolbet event for MyPa vs Pepo` even though the match is live on Coolbet (URL `/sport/match/5545414`, Finland Kakkonen - Lohko A). Root cause: the combo placer's per-leg resolution only used `search_coolbet_event` and gave up on a search miss — unlike the singles loop, which falls back to the full `fo-category` tree when `/search/v2` returns nothing. Coolbet's search/v2 doesn't index lower-tier Finnish football, so every combo containing one of those legs was killed at leg 2. Fix in `_place_combo_bets`: lazy-load `_category_events` once per run, fuzzy-match remaining legs against the full tree on a search miss. Also fixed a related silent gap — combo legs were calling `search_coolbet_event(session, home, away)` without `match_date`, bypassing the COOLBET-FUZZY-DATE-GUARD for same-team-different-day candidates. Now passes `leg_kick` from the existing team_rows query. Smoke: COMBO-FALLBACK-FO-CATEGORY.
>
> ## 2026-05-29 — SEARCH-RETRY-TRANSIENT done
>
> User flagged that the admin Telegram summary said "search blocked" while Coolbet's search endpoint was actually fine (live probe returned HTTP 200). Root cause: `_do_search` raised `CoolbetSearchBlocked` on *any* non-200, including transient 429/5xx — a single hiccup tripped the singles loop's `except`, which then marked every remaining bet in the batch as `search_blocked` and bailed before the combo phase. The error text also told the user to refresh `COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT`, but `--record` runs in anon-read mode since COOLBET-ANON-READ (commit `673b9e1`) — no JWT involved. Fix: `_do_search` now retries once with 1s sleep on `{429, 500, 502, 503, 504}` before raising; the exception message drops the cbauth/JWT advice and points at Imperva/Kambi + `COOLBET_IMPERVA_COOKIES` instead. Persistent failures still abort the batch as designed. Smoke: SEARCH-RETRY-TRANSIENT.
>

> ## 2026-05-26 — ADMIN-PLACE-COOLBET-ONLY-EVIDENCE + COOLBET-FUZZY-DATE-GUARD done
>
> Two more follow-ups on /admin/place after ADMIN-PLACE-STRICT-COOLBET landed.
> - **ADMIN-PLACE-COOLBET-ONLY-EVIDENCE** — done. User flagged Racing Club Res. vs Tigre Res. (Argentina Primera B Metropolitana Reserves) showing `⚠ no market` across all four bot picks (bot_high_alignment BTTS no, bot_dc_value DC x2, bot_v10_all + bot_ou25_global O/U under 2.5) when in fact Coolbet doesn't carry that league at all. Root cause: `matchIdsWithCoolbetEvent` was built from snapshots in `bookmaker IN ("Coolbet", "Unibet")`. Unibet (global) has the fixture via AF's bulk-odds endpoint — no fuzzy match involved — so Unibet rows existed even though Estonian Coolbet has nothing. The set therefore claimed Coolbet "has the event", and rows where Coolbet has no snapshot for the specific market resolved to `no_market` instead of the correct `no_event`. Fix: query is now `.eq("bookmaker", "Coolbet")`. Real_bets ground truth (Coolbet bookmaker only) was already correct. The placer writes a presence-marker snapshot (1X2 home) at no_market time, so legitimate Coolbet-no-market rows still chip correctly. Smoke: ADMIN-PLACE-SKIP-REASON (extended).
> - **COOLBET-FUZZY-DATE-GUARD** — done. User asked: "when you find a match, also validate the date of the match is the same." Same-team double-headers (reserves vs first team, two-leg cup ties, women vs men) were vulnerable to name-only fuzzy matches resolving to the wrong-day event. The placer would then write a Coolbet snapshot under our match_id pointing at the wrong fixture, masking it on /admin/place as `no_market` and risking a real-money placement on the wrong game in `--execute` mode. Fix: `fuzzy_match_event` now takes an optional `match_date`; candidates whose `start` is more than ±6h away are rejected before scoring. Tolerance covers tz/reschedule drift but rejects different-day fixtures. Threaded through all four call sites — `search_coolbet_event` (placer's pre-match search), placer's category-tree fallback (`run_singles_loop`), explorer's bulk `run_bulk` sweep, and explorer's `--odds-mode league-mapped` (default in production). Smoke: COOLBET-FUZZY-DATE-GUARD.
>
> ## 2026-05-26 — ADMIN-PLACE-STRICT-COOLBET + DNB-MAP done
>
> Two follow-ups on /admin/place after user spotted false-positive "⏵ auto-place" badges.
> - **ADMIN-PLACE-STRICT-COOLBET** — done. Cape Town City vs Milford FC (South-Africa PSL) showed `⏵ auto-place` on a `bot_dc_value` x2 row where Coolbet doesn't carry the DC market at all — the 1.70 displayed in the Coolbet column was the Unibet proxy fallback (`*` marker), not real Coolbet pricing. Estonian Coolbet and global Unibet share Kambi but expose different regional market catalogs (Coolbet often lacks DC, AH quarter lines, exotics). `getPlaceableBets`' status logic was using `livePrice = coolbetOdds ?? unibetOdds` and gating no_market on `livePrice == null`, so the Unibet proxy was being mistreated as proof Coolbet supports the market. Fix: the auto-place gate now uses `coolbetOdds` strictly (new `coolbetGateEdge` for the edge_eroded check); the Unibet proxy continues to render in the Coolbet column for display only. Result: rows where Coolbet has the event (presence-marker snapshot + real_bets ground truth) but no `coolbetOdds` for this exact `(market, selection)` now correctly show `⚠ no market`. Smoke: ADMIN-PLACE-SKIP-REASON (extended with `coolbetGateEdge` + strict-`coolbetOdds == null` assertions).
> - **DNB-MAP** — done. `_mapPaperToSnapshotKey` in `engine-data.ts` had no case for `draw_no_bet`, so even after yesterday's DNB-PARSE landed real DNB rows in `odds_snapshots`, the /admin/place table couldn't surface them — Mariehamn vs Lahti (`bot_high_alignment`, DNB home) showed `— — —` across Coolbet / Bet365 / Pinnacle despite Coolbet carrying the market. Added `draw_no_bet` → `(market="draw_no_bet", selection="home"|"away")` mapping. Smoke: ADMIN-PLACE-SKIP-REASON (extended with `m === "draw_no_bet"` assertion).
> - **5% edge threshold** unchanged. Mariehamn DNB at 4.0% pick edge correctly shows `✗ edge < 5%` — `coolbet_daemon.py --min-edge=0.05` filters it at the SQL level so the placer never tries, and that's the intended behavior.

> Last updated (prev): 2026-05-24 — **DC-CASE-FIX + DC-RESULTKEY-FIX done — every double_chance bet has been silently failing at placement.**
> User flagged Gagra vs Dila and Jezero vs Mornar (both `bot_dc_value`) as "⚠ no match" rows when both matches were on Coolbet. Wrote `scripts/coolbet_match_probe.py` (mirrors the placer's match-finding flow without placing) and used it to bisect. Match was found cleanly (Gagra vs Dila → 'FC Gagra Tbilisi vs FC Dila Gori' fuzzy score 100, 42 markets including "Double Chance" line=0), but `resolve_placement_target` returned None. Two stacked bugs:
> (1) **DC-CASE-FIX** — `_normalise_our_target` required uppercase `"1X"/"X2"/"12"` (`if s in ("1X", "X2", "12"):`) but paper bots write lowercase. Every DC bet failed the equality check and returned `(None, None, None)`.
> (2) **DC-RESULTKEY-FIX** — even after that, `_outcome_id_for_selection` used `.strip("[]")` which only strips leading/trailing brackets. Coolbet's DC `result_key` is `"[Home]/Draw"` (starts with `[`, no trailing `]`) → strip produced `"Home]/Draw"`, target_key was `"[home]/draw"`, never matched. The `"12"` outcome (`"[Home]/[Away]"`) was off by both brackets. **DC has never resolved a single bet at placement** — explains the recurring "no Coolbet odds" DC rows the user has been flagging.
> Fixes: uppercase DC selection in `_normalise_our_target`; store target_key without brackets (`"home/draw"`) and use `.replace("[","").replace("]","")` to strip *all* brackets from `result_key`. Re-probe confirmed: Gagra/Dila DC 1x resolves at 1.78, Jezero/Mornar DC x2 resolves at 1.32. Smoke: DC-CASE-AND-RESULTKEY-FIX. Diagnostic kept: `scripts/coolbet_match_probe.py` for future "no match" / "no market" triage.

> Last updated (prev): 2026-05-24 — **AH-CAL-BYPASS done — AH bot ACTUALLY unstuck.**
> Manual same-day pipeline verification of AH-VETO-WIDEN revealed it was a necessary but insufficient fix. Verbose funnel diagnostic showed `bot_ah_away_dog` generated ~170 candidates today, ALL killed at the post-calibration `↓edge` gate (zero PIN-VETO drops). Root cause: double-shrinkage. AH probabilities come from `_solve_lambdas_calibrated` (which inverts already-Platt-calibrated 1X2 probs back to lambdas, then computes AH from those calibrated lambdas) — so the AH `raw_mp` is ALREADY calibrated. But `calibrate_prob` then applied tier-level shrinkage AGAIN (T1 α=0.20 means 80% weight on bookmaker implied, only 20% on our model). Edge collapsed to ~1pp, way under the 5% gate. Pre-AH-HOME-BIAS-fix (May 21) the bug had inflated raw probs ~7.5pp, masking the double-shrinkage; once the bias fix landed the bot went silent.
> Fix: `calibrate_prob` now early-returns when market starts with `asian_handicap` or `double_chance` (DC has the same derivation — direct sums of calibrated 1X2 probs). Skips stage-1 shrinkage; `apply_platt` already no-ops for these markets (no stored fits). Same-day pipeline re-run with fix: **`bot_ah_away_dog` placed 47 bets** (raw=cal=0.53-0.75 range, edges 11-21%), `bot_ah_home_fav` placed 36 bets. Total: 176 bets across all bots (vs 2 before AH-CAL-BYPASS).
> Smoke: AH-CAL-BYPASS (with behavioural assertion verifying AH bypasses shrinkage but 1X2 still shrinks).

- **☑** — `⬜` not started · `🔄` in progress · `✅` done
- **Ready?** — `✅ Ready` pick up now · `⏳ Waiting [reason]` blocked

| **BOOK-UPLIFT-REPORT-2026-09-02** | 🔄 In Progress 2026-09-02 | 1h | Now that Epicbet ingests again (EPICBET-403-FROM-VPS), quantify what a second book is actually worth before anyone opens an account or builds a placer. **First 3h snapshot: 177 of ~236 upcoming fixtures priced by BOTH books, 1,840 comparable outcome pairs, weighted line-shopping uplift +2.00%** — against a bot that fires at a 3% true edge, that is most of the threshold again. Neither book dominates (Epicbet better 48–54% on everything except **BTTS, where Coolbet wins clearly: 29.4% / −0.56%**). **No cron and no new table**: `odds_snapshots` already retains 3.5 months / 66M rows, so the uplift is computable retrospectively over any window — a scheduled snapshotter would be a new failure mode for data we already hold. Deliverable is a re-runnable script. **Trap baked in**: AH `selection` is only `home`/`away`, the line lives in `handicap_line`; joining without it compares different handicaps and reported **+22.62%** instead of the true **+0.86%** (same class as gotcha 16). |
| **COOLBET-SQUAD-GUARD-API-2026-09-02** | ✅ Done 2026-09-02 (**found while measuring AF-STALE — bigger than the ticket it came from**) | 1h | The ticket asked for a rate before building. Measuring it produced **218 warnings over 12 distinct fixtures**, and the most recent were not postponements at all: `'Seoul W vs Incheon Red Angels W'` was being matched to Coolbet's **`'FC Seoul vs Incheon United'` at score 80** — a **women's fixture matched to the men's match**. Root cause: `coolbet_placer.fuzzy_match_event` had **no squad check whatsoever**. That is what "COOLBET-SQUAD-GUARD carries a partial fix; the API path is still exposed" meant in practice — only the UI placer was guarded. **Verified before the fix, at an identical kickoff**: women→men MATCHED (80), first team→its own reserves MATCHED (100), U21→senior MATCHED. Each would store the wrong book's price under our fixture and feed it to the value bots. **It only looked safe because the date guard fired first** — the two fixtures happened to kick off at different times, so it surfaced as a DATE MISMATCH warning instead of as bad odds. Same kickoff and it goes straight through. **Fixed** by rejecting on squad qualifier *before* scoring, reusing `epicbet_explorer._squads_compatible` so all three matching paths agree, with a `skipped_squad` counter in both log lines. Verified no over-rejection: women→women, U21→U21 and men→men all still match. Smoke `COOLBET-SQUAD-GUARD-API`, mutation-checked (removing the guard, and neutering it to never fire, both turn it red). |
| **COOLBET-UI-PLACER-AUDIT-WARN-2026-09-01** | ✅ Done 2026-09-01 | 1h | `place_coolbet_ui.py` ends nearly every pass with `WARNING: N picks processed but only M recorded — audit trail is incomplete`. **Both sides of the comparison are wrong**, so it fires when nothing is wrong and would keep firing if something were. (a) `recorded` counts `coolbet_placement_attempts` rows from **the last hour across all runs**, not this run — the job runs hourly, so a pass at :00 picks up the tail of the previous one. (b) `len(picks)` counts **every** loaded pick, but three branches deliberately write no attempt row: `already_placed` (the dedup that makes re-running safe — so the warning is guaranteed once anything is placed), the kickoff-cutoff skip, and the daily-cap `break`. Rows are written only by the `exposure_guard` branch and by `stage_bet`, which writes on every exit. **Why it matters**: the check exists because an earlier version printed "all N recorded" while the audit INSERT was silently rolling back — the exact failure the table exists to expose ([[feedback_silent_failures]]). A warning that is wrong on nearly every run trains the operator to ignore the one run where the audit trail really is broken, on the code path that places real money. **Fixed**: `recorded` is now bounded by a `run_started` stamp taken after the lock (`single_run_lock()` is `LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB` and held for the whole pass, so no other run can interleave rows), and compared against `expected_rows`, incremented at exactly the two write sites — the `exposure_guard` branch and each `stage_bet` call (which writes on every exit). The kickoff-cutoff and daily-cap branches correctly contribute nothing. **Confirmed against `dev/active/coolbet-ui-placer.log`: in every warning case the audit trail was actually complete** — `placed=1 skipped=4 already-placed=7` recorded 5 of an expected 5, and `placed=0 skipped=7 already-placed=1` recorded 7 of 7. Every fire was the `already_placed` count. Smoke `COOLBET-UI-PLACER-AUDIT-WARN`, mutation-checked: reverting to `len(picks)`, reverting to the last-hour window, or dropping either increment each turn it red. |
| **COOLBET-MATCH-EXPOSURE-GUARD-2026-09-01** | ✅ Done 2026-09-01 | 3h | On 2026-08-31 the live real-money bot placed **19 bets / EUR 190 for -EUR 92.80** (4W-14L settled, -51.6pct ROI), wiping out August (+EUR 2.74 on EUR 718). Two structural faults, both in `scripts/place_coolbet_ui.py` — **not** `coolbet_placer.py`, which has a `(match_id, market, selection)` dedup the UI path lacks entirely. **(1) Both sides of one market.** Colwyn Bay v Llandudno: `1x2/away` @3.10 placed 13:03, `1x2/home` @2.65 placed 16:00. The model legitimately flipped on the 15:10 refresh (away calibrated_prob 0.3412 -> home 0.4333) and emitted a **new pick with a new UUID**; `already_placed()` is keyed on `shadow_bet_id`, saw an unfamiliar id, and let it through. Net -EUR 3.50 on EUR 20 for pure vig. Not a one-off — same shape 2026-08-27 (Ferencvaros away+draw, Guairena away+home). **(2) Correlated stacking.** Airbus UK v Holywell took `1x2/away` + `U2.5` + `U3.5` (finished 6-2, all three lost together, EUR 30 in one correlated block); Barcelona v Rayo took `U3.5` + `U2.5` (5-2, both lost). Under-2.5 and under-3.5 are one opinion sized double, not two bets. `load_picks()` returns every unsettled shadow row with no per-match state anywhere; `MAX_BETS_PER_DAY=20` / `MAX_STAKE_PER_DAY=200` are the only blast-radius limits and the run stopped **one bet short** of the cap. **Latent, not yet fired on singles**: of 359 shadow rows on 08-31 only **197** are distinct `(match_id, market, selection)` — ~45pct are duplicate UUIDs for the same logical pick, and only run timing (not any guard) stopped a double-place. Combos already did double-place twice this month (`1a5d30cc` has two `combo/straight` + two `combo/fours_up` rows). **Trap for any fix**: `real_bets` holds two vocabularies for the same thing — `o/u`+`'over 2.5'` (API placer) and `over_under_25`+`'over'` (UI placer) — so a naive exposure query misses half the book. **Fix**: max **2 bets per match, from different market families** (`result` = 1x2/DC/DNB/AH · `totals` = over_under_*/o-u · `btts`), never two from one family, plus `MAX_STAKE_PER_MATCH`; logical-pick dedup on the normalised triple instead of UUID-only; shared vocabulary normaliser; in-memory exposure map seeded from `real_bets` and updated per placement (Airbus's three bets were 13:00/13:02/13:02 — same pass, so a DB-only check is racy). Guards evaluate in dry-run too and every block writes a `coolbet_placement_attempts` row via `record_attempt()` so firing rates are measurable ([[feedback_silent_failures]]). **Deliberately out of scope**: 7 of 19 bets were Cymru Premier / U21 and went 1-for-7 — league-tier gating is a strategy change on n=7, filed separately as COOLBET-LOW-TIER-LEAGUE-REVIEW. Cancelling the stale side on a model flip needs a cash-out path we do not have; the guard holds one position instead of two. **SHIPPED 2026-09-01**: `MAX_BETS_PER_MATCH=2` / `MAX_STAKE_PER_MATCH=20` + `MARKET_FAMILY` (result = 1x2/DC/DNB/AH · totals = the whole OU ladder · btts), one bet per family; `canon_bet()` collapses both vocabularies; combos excluded (placeholder match_id); exposure seeded by `match_exposure()` from `real_bets` unfiltered by `placed_at`/result and updated in-memory per placement; blocks write `coolbet_placement_attempts` with `stage='exposure_guard'`. Smoke `COOLBET-MATCH-EXPOSURE-GUARD` (mutation-checked: removing the family rule or unwiring the loop call both turn it red). Whitepaper 8.3.7. **Validated on the live board 2026-09-01** — all three failure modes were present and about to fire again: Preston v Bristol City (`1x2/draw` + `1x2/away`, the Colwyn Bay shape), Rapperswil v SC Kriens (`O2.5` + `O3.5`), and Atletico Grau v FBC Melgar, where yesterday's **still-pending** draw bet had a fresh duplicate-UUID pick queued behind it — the double-place this guard was written for, caught before it happened. 3 of 11 open picks blocked. **Counterfactual is NEGATIVE and must not be oversold**: replayed over August's 79 settled singles the guard blocks 8 bets worth **+EUR 19.30 (+24.1pct ROI)**, cutting the month from +EUR 24.43 to +EUR 5.13. At n=8 and odds ~3.0 that is noise, and the case for the guard is **variance/sizing, not edge** — it restores the flat-staking assumption the whole audit rests on and stops positions that pay vig against themselves. Re-evaluate on CLV once a few hundred blocked-vs-placed decisions exist. |
| **COOLBET-LOW-TIER-LEAGUE-REVIEW-2026-09-01** | ✅ Step 1 done 2026-09-01 — **measured; verdict is DO NOT GATE** | 2-3h | **Step 1 (measurement) complete. Four findings, and the proposed mechanism does not work.** **(1) `leagues.tier` cannot express the concern.** Wales Premier League, Iceland `2. Deild`, Norway `2. Division - Group 2`, New South Wales NPL 2 and England `Premier League 2 Division One` (U21) are **all `tier = 1`** — the same tier as Bundesliga, La Liga and Eredivisie. A tier gate would not target thin markets at all; it would hit the Bundesliga. **(2) The tier split is empty of signal**: over the full ui-placer history (n=51), tier 1 is n=47 / ROI −3.13% / CLV **+0.065**, tier 2 is n=4 / ROI −48.75% / CLV −0.024. n=4 decides nothing. **(3) The documented kill rule REFUSES the gate.** Worst league by P&L is Wales Premier League (n=8, 2W, −€35.50) — but its CLV is **+0.122, the second-highest in the entire book**. The rule is `ROI < −5% AND CLV < 0%`; Wales fails the CLV leg outright. This is exactly the BOT-GATE-OU-BTTS-2026-08-31 pattern: negative ROI with strongly positive CLV means variance has not converged, not that edge is absent. **(4) The BOT-NO-PIN-TIER0-GUARD precedent does NOT reproduce.** No degenerate ~uniform 1X2 fallback in any league we bet. The failure mode is real but lives elsewhere — Uganda **75%** of matches near-uniform (max−min 1X2 prob < 0.05), Botswana 57%, Lesotho 25% — and **no active bot has taken a single shadow pick there in 60 days** (1 historical real bet, pre-dating the current fleet). So there is no live exposure and no independent-of-ROI reason to gate. **Verdict: no league gate.** The whole ui-placer book is 51 bets; league-level ROI at n=8 is noise. **Step 2** if this is revisited: the useful guard is not league tier but a **prediction-degeneracy check** — refuse to bet when the 1X2 spread `max−min < 0.05`, which is model-quality based, independent of ROI, and would have caught the Uganda/Botswana case before it ever reached a bet. |

| **BOT-GATE-OU-BTTS-2026-08-31** | ⚠️ **REWRITE — the ticket's numbers were wrong three ways (re-verified 2026-09-02)** (P1) | 3-4h | Re-measured on **deduped** shadow rows priced at `odds_at_pick_live`: `bot_sweep_ou25_v1` n=232, ROI **−0.49%** (ticket: −4.48%), Pinnacle CLV +4.82% t=9.1; `bot_sweep_ou35_v1` n=187, ROI **+5.76%** (ticket: −7.00%), CLV +3.33% t=5.9; `bot_coolbet_value_v1` n=301, ROI +6.95%. **The three errors:** (i) the ticket's n=2,301/1,932 are RAW `shadow_bets` counts — deduped they are 232/187, so **every t-stat was inflated ~√10**; (ii) the "t=38 on a +0.05% mean is statistical not economic significance" counter-argument is a **units error** — the stored mean is 0.05 = **+5%** — so that argument is void; (iii) the negative ROIs were an artefact of cohort duplication plus STALE-BEST-ODDS. **Still only marginal net of vig:** o/u de-vig costs ~3pp (`clv_pinnacle` +0.25% → `clv_pinnacle_devig` −2.76%), so honest OU CLV is ~+1.8% (ou25) / +0.3% (ou35). The "do not blanket-gate" verdict survives, but for the opposite reason — **the OU bots are not losing.** BTTS stays unadjudicable: **zero Pinnacle BTTS rows exist** (0 of 3,076,350 BTTS snapshots since Aug 1), so there is no sharp anchor to judge it against at all. | ⬜ superseded text follows —  Opened to gate OU/BTTS betting after the offline no-skill finding. **Investigation says do not.** Applying the project's own documented kill rule (`ROI < −5% AND CLV < 0% → retire`) to every ACTIVE OU/BTTS bot over 90 days retires **zero** of them, because every negative-ROI OU bot has *positive* CLV at high significance: `bot_sweep_ou25_v1` −4.48% ROI / **CLV +0.05%, t=38.2** (n=2,301), `bot_sweep_ou35_v1` −7.00% / **+0.04%, t=25.9** (n=1,932), `bot_coolbet_value_v1` OU35 −15.23% / +0.06%, t=10.1. This is the documented "edge is real, variance has not converged" signature and OUT-OF-BETA-CUTOFF-RECHECK already records that killing on ROI at low n with strong CLV is a systematic mistake. **But** two counter-arguments must be resolved first: (a) a t of 38 on a **+0.05%** mean is statistical, not economic, significance — it does not cover the vig; (b) the offline head genuinely loses to a base-rate constant, so the CLV may be coming from the bots' selection filter rather than from the model. BTTS cannot be adjudicated either way — gotcha 4, Pinnacle quotes no BTTS, so those bots have **no CLV at all** and are unvalidatable. **Next step**: settle whether +0.05% CLV net of vig is positive EV before touching any bot. Do not act on ROI alone. |

| **SIGNALER-MATURITY-SHADOWING-2026-08-28** | ✅ Done 2026-08-28 | P2 | 30min | **6.4pct of calibrated picks never reach the public Telegram channel.** `load_signal_candidates` in `workers/automation/coolbet_signaler.py` collapses multi-bot picks with `DISTINCT ON (match_id, market, selection) ... ORDER BY edge_percent DESC`, so the **highest-edge row becomes canonical** and that row's bot decides the public-post gate at line 356 (`b.get("maturity") == "calibrated"`). When a **beta** bot happens to quote a higher edge than a **calibrated** bot on the identical (match, market, selection), the public post is silently skipped even though a calibrated bot backed the pick. **Measured 2026-08-28**: over 60 days, **7 of 109** calibrated picks were suppressed this way (over 30 days it was 2 of 55). No log line fires — the operator channel post succeeds normally, so the only symptom is a pick missing from @oddsintelpicks. This is how the original 'why isn't the 2nd public pick sent' investigation started; that specific case turned out to be a genuine beta pick, but the bug it uncovered is real and separate. **Fix**: compute a group-level `bool_or(b.maturity_label = 'calibrated') OVER (PARTITION BY sb.match_id, sb.market, sb.selection)` as `group_has_calibrated` in the same query that already computes `bot_count`, and gate the public post on THAT rather than on the canonical row's `maturity`. Keep the canonical row for message content (highest edge is still the right price to publish). **SHIPPED 2026-08-28**: `bool_or(b.maturity_label = 'calibrated') OVER (PARTITION BY sb.match_id, sb.market, sb.selection) AS group_has_calibrated` added to the query; the gate at line ~356 now reads `b.get("group_has_calibrated")`. Canonical row selection deliberately left alone — re-ordering DISTINCT ON to prefer calibrated rows would let a lower-edge calibrated row fall under the per-market floor in `load_signal_candidates` and drop the pick entirely, so the highest-edge row still supplies the message content (same pick either way). **Verified against history**: 8 picks in the last 60d that a calibrated bot backed but that were withheld would now publish (FC Tallinn, Pirin Blagoevgrad, Philadelphia Union II, Uruguay Montevideo, 3 de Noviembre, Neman, Grotta). Smoke `TELEGRAM-PUBLIC` extended to pin the group-level flag, to assert the old per-row gate is GONE, and to check the window partition matches the DISTINCT ON key. |

Replaced with the same **t-test the admin page uses** — CLV basis once n>=100 else ROI once n>=200, promote t>=+1.65, demote t<=-1.65, >=14d observed — which holds both error rates at the nominal ~5pct. **Constants are now shared between engine and frontend and smoke pins the pairing**, so the two gates cannot diverge. Real-money demote kept as an explicitly-labelled **tripwire, not a verdict** (t~-0.17 at n=20) because the loss function is asymmetric. **Point-in-time replay run instead of waiting** (`lineshop_replay.py`): over the ENTIRE multi-book archive — line-shopping is impossible before 2026-04-28, only Pinnacle existed, so four months is the honest maximum — `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` n=583 ROI +6.91pct **t=+1.20**, `bot_sweep_ou35_v1` n=379 +1.77pct t=+0.29, `bot_sweep_ou25_v1` n=365 +1.18pct t=+0.21. **None is significant**; the raw gate would have promoted pin_1x2_home outright. | **COOLBET-FEED-PAIRING-2026-08-28** | ✅ Done 2026-08-28 (migration 289) | **P1** | 3h | **The real bottleneck, and it affects the LIVE real-money bot.** `_run_coolbet_value_pass` pairs books with `DISTINCT ON (match_id, market, selection, bookmaker) ... ORDER BY timestamp DESC` — each book's latest row, with **no time-window constraint between them**. Measured on the bot's own last 79 picks: median pairing gap **1.63h**, **56pct paired >1h apart**, **37pct >4h**, max **15.6h**. Coolbet swings 14-19pct intraday, so a stale pairing can manufacture edge out of nothing — that is exactly what produced fake **+55.6pct** DC edges in the 2026-08-28 probe, one quoting x2 at 2.20 against Pinnacle away-alone of 2.26, which is structurally impossible. **Reassuring finding, recorded so it is not over-read**: claimed edge does NOT rise with gap — **7.65pct** at ≤1h, **7.24pct** at 1-4h, **5.82pct** at >4h — so staleness is not currently inflating edge, and no live pick is known to be bogus. The exposure is real but latent, and it grows every time the Coolbet scraper stalls (6h outage 2026-08-28, 4.5h 08-27, 80h in COOLBET-FEED-WATCHDOG). **Fix**: (a) constrain pairing to a window (start ≤2h, measure the pick-loss); (b) record both source timestamps on the pick so staleness is auditable after the fact rather than reconstructable only by probe; (c) skip a match rather than pricing it against a stale quote. **SHIPPED 2026-08-28**: pairing is now time-bounded (`_PAIR_MAX_GAP_H`, env `COOLBET_PAIR_MAX_GAP_H`, default **4h**) and every pick records `shadow_bets.pair_gap_hours` (migration 289). **The 4h default is a judgement call, not a derived number**, and deliberately loose: claimed edge does not rise with gap, and there is no natural cliff to cut at — on 2026-08-28's 39 picks the loss runs 49pct at 1h, 41pct at 2h, 33pct at 3-4h, 23pct at 6h, with a median gap of 0.97h even after the scraper was repaired. The feeds are CHRONICALLY misaligned, not merely misaligned during outages, so a strict window would cost ~41pct of picks to guard a risk not currently materialising. Tighten it once settled data correlates gap with CLV — that is the honest way to choose the number and it does not exist yet. **Do this before building any new Coolbet bot** — DC and AH both measured differently depending on which alignments happened to exist, so neither market can be judged until pairing is deterministic. Fixing it improves the live bot first. |
