The usable half is still 51,742 rows. **Measured with a same-window pairing guard** (the guard the DC probe lacked, which manufactured +55pct edges): 398 same-match same-line pairs, **187 paired within 30 minutes**, 361 priceable outcomes, **14 firing at a 3pct edge (~7/day)**. **The edge distribution is far healthier than DC's**: p50 **-6.7pct**, p90 -1.6pct, **max +13.2pct** — no absurd tail, where DC's top three were +55.6/+50.7/+45.4pct pure artifact. That is the main reason to prefer AH as the second experiment. **Caveat to watch**: the qualifying picks cluster heavily on **line=-1.0** (4 of the top 6), so early results may measure one line rather than the market; check line diversity before reading anything into ROI. **Build**: (1) new bot row, `maturity_label='experimental'`; (2) pair on `(match_id, handicap_line, selection)` within ≤30min — grouping without `handicap_line` is what produced a bogus +17pct favourite-drift finding on 2026-08-28, see ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #3; (3) drop quarter lines rather than snapping to a neighbour; (4) 2-way proportional de-vig on (home, away) at the same line; (5) outlier guard per ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #9. **Placer support is the extra work vs DC**: the UI placer currently REFUSES AH by design, because Coolbet renders it as team-name-plus-handicap ('FK Partizan +1.0') which collides with the 1X2 label space — it needs its own card reader, similar to the OU ladder (`read_ou_grid`). Not needed for shadow evaluation, only for placement. **REVISED 2026-08-28 — blocked, not rejected.** A clean re-read gave **1 qualifying pick from 42 fresh pairs**, not 14 from 187; the difference is entirely which 30-minute alignments happened to exist when each probe ran. Only **42 of 633** same-line pairs fell within 30 minutes, because Coolbet and Pinnacle are written by different jobs on different cadences. The market still looks healthier than DC (max edge +12.5pct, no absurd tail vs DC's +55pct artifacts), but **it cannot be evaluated honestly until the feeds can be paired in time**. Build COOLBET-FEED-PAIRING first. **BACKTESTED 2026-08-28 — negative.** The earlier 'blocked, cannot evaluate' verdict was wrong: Coolbet AH goes back to 2026-05-21 (57,263 rows, 4,322 matches, **3,845 settled**), so a real backtest was available all along; the 2-day probe window was self-imposed. Over the full settled history with 4h pairing and the 1.30x outlier guard: **1,678 same-line settled pairs → 65 picks at ≥3pct edge → won 18 / lost 35 / push 12, ROI -15.33pct** on EUR 530 staked, average claimed edge **+7.44pct** against realised **-15.33pct**. That gap is the signal. It also matches the two retired AH bots: `bot_ah_home_fav` **-12.99pct ROI** (n=133) and `bot_ah_away_dog` retired on **-29.87pct CLV**. **A CLV cross-check was attempted and discarded as invalid** — see ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #16: fixing the handicap line gives a fake **+19.13pct CLV at t=+7.75** because Pinnacle quotes 7-10+ rungs at once and the last quote at a fixed rung averages 12.78h pre-kickoff. So the honest read rests on ROI at n=65 — small, but directionally consistent with 215 settled bets of prior AH history. **Not as conclusively dead as DC** (which had zero qualifying picks and n=198 of negative CLV), but the burden of proof has flipped: do not build without a positive signal from a larger or better-measured sample. **Real money stays off**: both new bots are excluded by `EXECUTE_ALLOWED_BOTS` in `scripts/place_coolbet_ui.py`, so they can run the full pipeline — matching, pricing, snapshots, audit rows — with no path to the account. |
| **COOLBET-DC-BOT-SCOPE-2026-08-28** | ❌ **Do not build** (evidence 2026-08-28) | — | **Scoped, not built** — a SEPARATE bot for double chance, deliberately not folded into `bot_coolbet_value_v1` because DC calibration is unproven and mixing it would contaminate a strategy now placing real money. **Why it is possible at all**: Pinnacle quotes no DC (zero rows — its AF feed carries 8 bet types and DC is not among them), but DC is *exactly* derivable from de-vigged 1X2 since each DC outcome is a union of 1X2 outcomes and the de-vigged probabilities partition the space — `P(1X)=P(h)+P(d)` etc. `get_devigged_pinnacle_close_prob()` (settlement.py:528) already implements the derivation, so no new maths is needed. Coolbet has **2,484 DC rows over 3 days** (1x avg 1.46, 12 avg 1.24, x2 avg 1.94) that this method could price and the current bot ignores. **Measured over a 2-day window**: 207 matches carried both Pinnacle 1X2 and Coolbet DC, giving 604 priceable outcomes; **13 would fire at a 3pct edge (~6/day)**. **But that number is not trustworthy yet.** The edge distribution is p50 **-5.7pct** (Coolbet DC is normally well worse than derived fair value, as expected from its margin), while the qualifying tail is dominated by artifacts: the top three at **+55.6pct / +50.7pct / +45.4pct** pair quotes taken **15.8h / 16.6h / 10.0h apart**, because the probe took each book's latest row independently. One of them offers x2 at 2.20 while Pinnacle's *away alone* is 2.26 — structurally impossible, since x2 must be shorter than either leg it contains. A same-window pairing guard is therefore the core of the work, not a nicety. **Data quality is otherwise sound**: a structural check across 241 matches carrying both Coolbet 1X2 and DC found only **3 x2 violations (1.2pct)**, all consistent with the same stale-pairing effect rather than mislabelling. **Build**: (1) new `bot_coolbet_dc_v1`, own row in `bots`, `maturity_label='experimental'`; (2) pair Pinnacle and Coolbet quotes within a tight window (≤30min) rather than latest-vs-latest; (3) structural guard — reject any DC price ≥ its shortest constituent leg; (4) outlier guard in the spirit of ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #9 (a +50pct DC edge is a data bug, not an opportunity); (5) add `double_chance` to `_COOLBET_MARKETS` **for the new bot only**. **Expect far fewer than 6 picks/day once guarded** — likely 1-2 — so budget ~3 weeks to reach a CLV read at n≈78. **Placer support**: the UI placer would need DC label handling (`Coolbet` renders 1x/12/x2 in its own card); 1x2 and OU are done, AH deliberately still refuses. **VERDICT 2026-08-28 — DO NOT BUILD.** Re-ran the probe with the same-window (≤30min) and structural guards the first pass lacked: **zero qualifying picks at any threshold**, median edge **-5.8pct**, and the single best DC price in the sample is still **-3.2pct** — worse than fair value. The earlier '~6/day at 3pct' was entirely stale-pairing artifact. This converges with the historical record from an independent direction: all three retired DC bots posted negative de-vigged-Pinnacle CLV — `bot_dc_value` **-3.63pct** (n=113), `bot_dc_specialist` **-5.71pct** (n=54), `bot_dc_strong_fav` **-3.69pct** (n=31), combined n=198, past the n≈78 CLV threshold. Their realised CLV matches the live probe's median almost exactly. Coolbet's double-chance margin is simply wider than the edge available; a different anchor does not fix a market that is not there. **Related, larger prize**: Asian handicap is Pinnacle's DEEPEST market — **118,347 rows / 642 matches over 3 days, 3x its 1X2 coverage** — and Coolbet quotes 9,364 AH rows. AH is not excluded because the model finds no edge there (the model is not involved at all — this bot values against de-vigged Pinnacle, not our ensemble); it is simply unimplemented, and blocked on line alignment ([[project_coolbet_limitations]]: Coolbet has no quarter lines, so Pinnacle -0.25 has no counterpart) plus `handicap_line` grouping discipline. File separately if DC proves out. |
| **COOLBET-MOVED-FIXTURE-2026-08-31** | ✅ Done 2026-08-31 | **P1** | 1h | **Operator call: a postponed fixture is a bet we should take, not one to throw away.** A kickoff that parsed and disagreed was a hard reject in `pick_event` at any name score. Atletico Grau v FBC Melgar moved 31 Aug → 1 Sept and both Coolbet and the press agree against our feed; refusing it discarded a real 3.14 draw pick. **The guard was not pointless though** — it is what stops us backing the wrong leg of a two-legged tie. The distinction is **ambiguity, not disagreement**: exactly ONE same-team candidate → nothing to confuse it with, take it; TWO OR MORE → the kickoff is the only thing separating them, refuse rather than guess. **Bounds so "moved" cannot widen into "any fixture with these teams":** a moved candidate is ranked strictly BELOW any date-agreeing one, held to a **higher** name bar (`min_score_moved=85` vs 80 — we gave up the corroborating evidence, so names must carry more), and must fall inside `max_move_days=7`. COOLBET-SQUAD-GUARD still outranks all of it: a reserve side on another day is never a postponement. `allow_moved=False` keeps the old behaviour reachable, so this is a policy switch not a one-way door. Acceptance is logged `MOVED FIXTURE accepted` and written to the attempt row as a note, because the stale date also drives `KICKOFF_CUTOFF_MIN`, `load_picks`’ `m.date > NOW()` window and settlement. **Checked before shipping:** settlement only settles when a result exists (`home_goals is None → continue`), so an unplayed match stays pending rather than mis-settling. Smoke `COOLBET-MOVED-FIXTURE`. |

Smoke `COOLBET-SEARCH-PROMO-RACE`. |

| **AF-STALE-FIXTURE-DATES-2026-08-31** | ✅ Done 2026-09-02 — **measurement done, and it reframes the task.** The ticket's own first step was "how often does this fire — one Peruvian postponement is not yet a rate". The other **10 are false alarms**: 7 outright different fixtures (Frome Town v Bath City reported against Cheltenham Town v York City) and 3 squad mismatches (Seoul W v Incheon Red Angels W against FC Seoul v Incheon United; Gremio U17 against Grêmio FBPA; Man Utd U21 against Man Utd). **Root cause of the false alarms, now fixed**: `date_rejects` is populated by the date check at the TOP of the candidate loop, before the squad guard runs, and the warning block then re-scored those candidates on NAME ONLY. So a fixture that could never have matched was announced as "OUR fixture date is probably stale". Since this ticket's build step (1) is *alert off this warning*, alerting before the fix would have been **77% false** — and an alert that cries wolf three times in four trains the operator to ignore the one real case. `_squads_compatible` now gates the warning block; smoke `COOLBET-DATE-GUARD-DEAD` pins that a squad-incompatible candidate stays quiet, verified to fail without it. **Steps 2-4 shipped, with (2)/(3) deliberately NOT built as specified.** The ticket proposed *book date wins over AF* plus a `date_source` column to survive the 04:00 re-sync. At **3 genuine cases per 10 days** that machinery buys very little and can be wrong in a way that is worse than doing nothing: the input is a fuzzy name match, so a bad correction moves a fixture we would otherwise have priced correctly — and would then be believed over AF indefinitely. Smoke `AF-STALE-FIXTURE-DATES` pins the column set, that suppressions ≥ fixture queries, that the recorder never writes `matches.date`, and that disputes are cleared; each verified by mutation — including a re-run after the first attempt failed on the *wrong* assertion. **Revised rate estimate: ~3 genuine stale dates per ~10 days of logs**, low enough that (4) matters more than (3) — we mostly need to stop pricing them, not to repair them. | 3h | **API-Football does not always follow a postponement, and nothing in our pipeline notices.** Found via [[COOLBET-DATE-GUARD-DEAD-2026-08-31]]: Atlético Grau v FBC Melgar moved 31 Aug → 1 Sept, Coolbet moved with it, AF fixture `1549469` still returns `2026-08-31T20:00:00+00:00` with status `NS` — checked live against the API, not just our copy. The daily 04:00 fixtures job re-syncs from AF, so **correcting `matches.date` by hand is overwritten within a day** — that is why this is a separate task and not a data patch. Consequences: the model prices a fixture that is not played, the bot raises picks on it, settlement at 21:00 finds no result, and CLV has no close to compare to. **Now visible but not corrected** — `fuzzy_match_event` logs `DATE MISMATCH … OUR fixture date is probably stale`. **Build:** (1) grep that warning off the snapshot log into a table or alert; (2) decide the trust order — Coolbet’s `match_start` is the book’s own money-backed date and was right here, so the likely rule is *book date wins over AF for scheduled fixtures*; (3) either re-fetch that fixture from AF before each pipeline stage or carry a `date_source` column so a book-corrected date survives the 04:00 re-sync; (4) suppress pick generation on a fixture whose date is disputed, rather than pricing it. **First measurement to take:** how often this fires across a full day of snapshots — one Peruvian postponement is not yet a rate. |

Which totals were reachable therefore depended on where Coolbet happened to centre the ladder — not on what Coolbet offered. New `expand_ou_card()` clicks the totals card’s expander (scoped to that card — Händikäp carries its own), clicks it through Playwright rather than JS dispatch, and **verifies by effect**: the price count must grow, else it warns rather than reporting success. A card with no expander returns False, never raises — every line already shown is the normal case. Confirmed live at 09:03 UTC: snapshot went **7 → 11 prices**, previously-hidden 0.5/1.0 rows appeared, and `over_under_25/under` **placed @ 4.05** (€10, captured 3.90, min-odds 3.67). **(2) Not a bug — FC Copenhagen v Sonderjyske had simply not been attempted yet.** Its pick was created **08:41 UTC**, after that morning’s 08:00 pass, so it had **zero** `coolbet_placement_attempts` rows. It placed on its very first attempt at 09:02 UTC, **1x2/draw @ 5.50**, matching on the whole word `Sonderjyske` — Coolbet folds the ø in `SønderjyskE` itself. The initial diagnosis (that whole-word queries could not reach the Coolbet spelling, by analogy with `coolbet_placer._search_event` needing `via query='Son'` for the same fixture) was **wrong**: that path’s failures were its own prefix set (`FCC`/`FCCo`/`FCCop`), not evidence about whole words. Name scoring was never in question either (home 72 / away 100, and the 20:00 Tallinn kickoff parses and agrees, dropping the bar from 80 to 55). **Prefix queries were still added, but as a LAST RESORT** — appended after every whole-word query, because `stage_bet` stops at the first query that matches and Coolbet blocked this IP on 2026-08-28 for traffic volume from one address. Interleaving them would have cost a live search round trip on every pick that never needed one. They are insurance for a name differing at its **start**, which words cannot cover. Smoke `COOLBET-UI-OU-EXPAND` (expander scoped, real click, growth verified, missing expander not an error), `COOLBET-UI-SEARCH-PREFIX` (asserts every whole word precedes every prefix, and that a `Son` prefix hit on Sondrio/Sondalo is still rejected by `pick_event`). |

Taking the max across books also **selects for whichever book is most wrong**, and `book_bias_probe` showed those are the worst-calibrated; fixing the shop removes that selection effect, so the edge measured is the edge received. **`bot_coolbet_value_v1`**: prices at Coolbet, values against the **Shin-de-vigged Pinnacle close**, fires at ≥3% true edge, tiers 1-2, outlier guard retained (COOLBET-OU-LINE-SHIFT found a bracketed team total at 17.00 vs Pinnacle's 4.19), one side per total. Fair value deliberately does NOT come from Coolbet — a book cannot look mispriced against itself; Coolbet decides what we pay, Pinnacle what it is worth, and only the former must be reachable. Runs on both the morning cohort and the 30-min refresh, error-isolated. **Verified live**: 1 pick — America de Cali v Junior, draw @ **3.85 at Coolbet**, +10.4% edge. Expect single-digit picks/day vs ~58 listed; at the CLV gate's precision that needs ~3 weeks to judge, and 5 placeable picks beat 58 unplaceable ones. **Note**: Coolbet is absent from the AF feed entirely — those rows come from the Mac launchd scraper at :03/:33, so a stale scraper silently empties this bot (COOLBET-FEED-WATCHDOG covers it). Existing bots and the Telegram picks feed are unchanged. Smoke `COOLBET-VALUE-BOT`. |

| **COOLBET-FEED-WATCHDOG-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 | 2h | Operator asked for a job that restarts the Coolbet daemon when it dies. Diagnosing first turned out to matter, because **the daemon never died**: `launchctl list` showed `coolbet-odds-snapshot` firing every 30 min on schedule and failing every single time for 80 hours, exit 1, with `Imperva cookies in DB are 79.5h stale → HTTP 403`. A naive restarter would have looped for three days without helping. **Root cause one level down**: CDP-Chrome was not running, and it is the only thing that can mint fresh Imperva cookies — Imperva trusts the operator's real browser and rejects FlareSolverr's. **Fixed live**: launched `local/launch_chrome_for_sync.sh`, opened a coolbet.com tab in the CDP profile, harvested all six Imperva cookies (`visid_incap_723517`, `incap_ses_1099_723517`, `nlbi_723517`, `nlbi_723517_2147483392`, `reese84`, `uuid`) into the DB — **feed came back at 0.01h stale from 80h**. New `workers/jobs/coolbet_feed_watchdog.py` classifies before acting: `NOT_LOADED` → reload, `STALE_COOKIES` → re-harvest from CDP, `CDP_DOWN` / `BLOCKED` → alert and **stop** (retrying a block hardens it). It judges on **output** — hours since the last Coolbet row in `odds_snapshots` — because every process-level proxy was green while the feed was dead, the same shape as [[feedback_silent_failures]]. Runs every 20 min via launchd on the Mac (both remedies need `launchctl` and CDP locally); installed and loaded. Smoke `COOLBET-FEED-WATCHDOG`. |

The warning makes the limitation visible rather than silent. Smoke `WEEKLY-EVAL-BASELINE`. |

Added a quiet **break-even price** under the odds — derived, not stored: `edge = odds × prob − 1` so `break-even = odds / (1 + edge)`, guarded against a −100% edge dividing by zero. Rendered at `text-[10px] text-neutral-600` so it does not compete with the odds themselves, with a tooltip spelling out that below it the bet is negative-EV. **Timely for a second reason**: the Coolbet feed is 74h stale (COOLBET-SNAPSHOT-STALL) and **zero of the 344 current upcoming picks carry a Coolbet price** — every recommendation is 10Bet (182), Unibet (78), Marathonbet (70) or Betano (14). Anyone placing at Coolbet is getting an unknown price, so a visible floor to check against is the difference between a validated bet and a guess. Smoke `PICKS-MIN-ODDS`. |

First paired run bears that out: v20260712 −3.59% vs v20260705 −4.25% CLV, newer better by 0.66pp but both negative — plausible for a raw component under an ensemble-tuned gate, and uninformative about production picks. **Do**: run the full ensemble path for the shadow version too and write those rows with `source='ensemble'`, `model_version=<shadow>`. `predictions` already keys on `(match, market, source, model_version)` so it is additive with no schema change, and `scripts/model_version_clv_scoreboard.py` is written and waiting. That makes model promotion a ~100-pick CLV measurement instead of a judgement call — which is how `v20260524_market` sat in production at 0% CLV while looking profitable on ROI. **Shipped 2026-08-26**: the shadow XGB leg is now blended with the **same** Poisson, **same** blend weights and **same** tier into a shadow ensemble, written as `source='ensemble'` with the candidate's `model_version`. `predictions` already keys on `(match_id, market, source, model_version)`, so both versions land on the SAME (match, market) — which is what makes the comparison paired. Skipped when the candidate resolves to the same version as production, since a version compared against itself is noise (exactly what the stale `SHADOW_MODEL_VERSION` pin produced pre-SHADOW-AUTOSELECT). **Two bugs found while wiring it**, both of which would have hit production: (1) `_shadow_ver` is assigned inside the Tier-A inference branch, so a Tier-B/C match would have raised `NameError` at the write and taken down the pipeline — now initialised per match; (2) `ensemble_prediction()` reads `xgb_exp_home`/`xgb_exp_away` to blend the expected-goals half, and the shadow dict never carried them because the XGB-leg-only A/B never called the blender — the shadow inference now runs the goals regressors too. Smoke `MODEL-AB-ENSEMBLE-LEG`. **Data starts accumulating on the next pipeline cohort**; `scripts/model_version_clv_scoreboard.py` was written earlier and needs no changes. |
| **MODEL-AB-SHADOW-SCORING-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 — NOTHING TO BUILD, the infrastructure already existed (P1 — model quality is the ONLY axis that moved CLV, and it is the one axis we cannot currently measure) | 4-6h | CLV-SLICE-SEARCH and COVERAGE-EXPANSION between them ruled out thresholds, threshold shape, market and league as ways to find a second edge (0 of 82, 0 of 70, and CLV −5.56% at t=−34 respectively). The one axis that moved CLV materially was **model version**. So the loop should be: ship a candidate model, score it on CLV, promote or reject in ~100 picks. **That loop cannot run today.** Model versions are routed **disjointly** — `v20260719` and `v20260712` both ran 2026-07-31 → 08-26 and share **exactly zero** match+market pairs, because `MODEL_VERSION_OU` / `MODEL_VERSION_OU_T1` send different versions to different market/tier slots. No fixture is ever scored twice. Consequently every comparison available is confounded: splitting one bot's bets by version confounds with **period** (bot_v10_all shows v20260712 at CLV **+15.17%**), pooling all bots by version confounds with **bot mix** (the same version reads **−1.42%** across 4,511 picks, dominated by the DC bots at −4.3%). Those two numbers are not a contradiction — neither is measuring the model. **Fix**: write shadow predictions from candidate versions alongside production on every match (predictions table already keys on `(match, market, source, model_version)`, so this is additive and needs no schema change). Then `scripts/model_version_clv_scoreboard.py` — already written and waiting on this data — does the paired comparison and CLV decides a promotion in ~100 picks instead of never. **RESOLVED 2026-08-26 — this was already built.** `SHADOW-INFERENCE (Phase B, 2026-05-24)`, `daily_pipeline_v2.py:2743`: set **`SHADOW_MODEL_VERSION`** and the pipeline loads that bundle alongside production, scores every match with both, and writes the candidate to `predictions`. It is **running right now** — `source='xgboost'` carries both `v20260705` and `v20260712` across **4,770 shared match+market pairs**. The reason it looked absent: shadow rows land under **`source='xgboost'`, not `source='ensemble'`**, so the overlap check that filed this task returned zero and looked exactly like "no such capability". Recorded in `docs/ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS.md` §1 so it is not re-derived. **First paired result** (v10 gate, point-in-time, on the 4,770 shared pairs): `v20260712` CLV −3.59% (n=1,044, t=−14.92) vs `v20260705` −4.25% (n=1,294, t=−21.16) — the newer version is better by 0.66pp on identical fixtures, but **both are negative**. Caveat: this is the raw **XGB component**, not the blended ensemble the bots actually bet, and it is scored under a gate tuned for the ensemble — so it does not say production picks are bad. **The remaining gap is that shadow scoring only covers the XGB leg**; extending it to write `source='ensemble'` shadow rows is the real follow-on and is filed as MODEL-AB-ENSEMBLE-LEG. |
| **COVERAGE-EXPANSION-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 — DO NOT widen the model's league range; measurably negative | 3h | Follow-on from CLV-SLICE-SEARCH, which located the edge in the newer model versions (`v20260712`: CLV **+15.17%, t=+5.79** on `bot_v10_all`'s picks) rather than in any threshold shape. The obvious next move was therefore coverage: since 2026-06-01 there are **261 leagues where v20260712 produces predictions and no active bot has placed a single bet** — 7,778 finished matches — against 166 covered. Most are placeable (232 have Pinnacle prices, 194 have Coolbet), so nothing mechanical was stopping us. `scripts/coverage_expansion_probe.py` replays **bot_v10_all's exact gate** (tier-scaled floors, longshot penalty, min_prob 0.30, odds 1.30–4.50, price-outlier guard) over those leagues. **1,968 picks would have fired, and every tier is negative with an overwhelming t-stat**: T0 −5.83% (t=−19.5), T1 −5.13% (t=−23.3), T2 −6.65% (t=−7.7), T3 −6.86% (t=−9.1), T4 −6.75% (t=−10.1); **overall CLV −5.56%, t=−34.15**, with ROI agreeing at −10.56% (t=−3.37). Not one of the ten best uncovered leagues is positive — the best, UEFA Europa League, is −2.47% (t=−2.37). **And it is not a training-data gap**: uncovered leagues carry 50,731 historical matches at 65.5% pre-2026, against 81,244 at 73.5% for covered ones — comparable depth. So the winning config is not portable; `bot_v10_all` works *because* it is confined to where it already bets, and the ~10pp CLV gap between its +4.82% and these leagues' −5.56% is the boundary of the model's real competence. **Actionable**: stop treating uncovered leagues as untapped upside. Smoke `COVERAGE-EXPANSION-PROBE`. |
| **CLV-SLICE-SEARCH-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 — negative result on threshold search, but located the edge | 4-6h | The shadow-bot programme exists to find a SECOND profitable bot alongside `bot_v10_all` — more markets, more leagues, more coverage. Its method was: guess a config, deploy it, wait months for ROI. That method cannot work, because ROI needs ~17,000 bets to resolve ±2% and the bots produce ~5/day. **Now that CLV decides in ~100 picks, the search can be run directly against history instead.** For every (match × market × selection) where an ensemble prediction and an accessible price both existed at kickoff−3h, compute the model edge and the de-vigged Pinnacle CLV, then group by market × league tier × odds band and find the slices where CLV is positive with a real t-stat. That searches thousands of candidate configs in one pass over data we already have, rather than one config per month. Deliberately gates on **CLV, not ROI** — PER-BOT-SWEEP-2026-08-24 showed selecting on backtest ROI is anti-predictive (−9.2% OOS), and CLV is both lower-variance and not the thing being optimised into. **Result 2026-08-26** (`scripts/clv_slice_search.py`): with production's outlier guard applied, **0 of 82 slices** clear CLV t ≥ 1.65 at a flat 3% edge floor — every slice negative, most from t=−5 to −18. Re-run with `--v10-gate`, replaying `bot_v10_all`'s exact structure (tier-scaled floors, higher bar on longshots than favourites, min_prob 0.30, odds 1.30–4.50): **0 of 70**. So neither a looser threshold nor v10's threshold *shape* finds a second edge. **First pass was contaminated and is worth recording**: before the outlier guard it reported `over_under_25 / 4.5+` at **CLV +173.95%, t=+42.49** — a normal OU 2.5 price is 1.5–2.5, so a 4.5+ quote so labelled is a mislabelled line, and it is fleet-wide, not one book (Marathonbet 2,413 such rows, Unibet 1,174, 888Sport 424 to 26.0, Coolbet 163 to 20.0). 14 slices "cleared" on that. **Where the edge actually is** — splitting `bot_v10_all`'s own bets by model version: v20260712 n=96 **CLV +15.17% (t=+5.79)**, v20260621 n=31 +17.85% (t=+2.97), v20260705 n=26 +8.96% (t=+2.45), against v20260524_market −0.72%, v14 −0.05%, v9a_202425 −0.04%. The edge is **entirely in the newer model versions**, and v20260712 is current production. **Limitation to respect**: the search replicates the edge gate but NOT the full production filter stack (league whitelist, PIN-VETO, model-sanity, odds-outlier); bot_v10_all placed 480 bets where the search fired 34,265, so it cannot rule out that those filters are what create the edge. **Next**: run the CURRENT model through the FULL production stack on new markets/leagues — not threshold tuning. |
| **DOUBLE-CHANCE-DEAD-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 — DC is structurally unprofitable at our books; close the re-enable path | 2h | Found while backfilling CLV for item 1. The three double-chance bots are the **highest-volume in the fleet** — `bot_dc_value` 2,436 settled picks, `bot_dc_specialist` 2,316, `bot_dc_strong_fav` 1,180 — and every one had **zero CLV coverage**, because Pinnacle quotes no double_chance market at all (API-Football's Pinnacle feed carries 8 bet types; DC is not one). DC fair value is nonetheless **exactly derivable**: each DC outcome is a union of 1X2 outcomes and the de-vigged 1X2 probabilities partition the space, so P(1X)=P(home)+P(draw) etc. Backfilled 66,628 shadow rows on that basis. **Result, and it is the cleanest demonstration of why CLV beats ROI as a gate**: <br><br>`bot_dc_value` n=2,436 — ROI −3.14% (t=**−1.83**) vs CLV −4.29% (t=**−28.18**)<br>`bot_dc_specialist` n=2,316 — ROI −2.84% (t=−1.62) vs CLV −4.36% (t=**−27.62**)<br>`bot_dc_strong_fav` n=1,180 — ROI **+0.40%** (t=+0.18) vs CLV −4.02% (t=**−21.08**)<br><br>ROI cannot reach significance on 2,436 bets; CLV settles it at t=−28. `bot_dc_strong_fav` even reads *profitable* on ROI while being −4% CLV. **And it is not bad price selection**: the bots already take essentially the best accessible DC price (0.6% off the max), and CLV **at the best available price is still −4.80%** — no accessible DC price beats fair. The ~4-5% DC margin at our books exceeds any edge available. All three were already retired 2026-05-28/06-01 and only shadow-track, so no live money was at risk, but their re-enable triggers (\"30+ shadow_bets at ≥3% ROI over a week\") are now **provably unreachable and should be treated as closed**. Do not build new double-chance strategies. Smoke `DOUBLE-CHANCE-CLV`. |
| **CLV-FIRST-DEV-LOOP-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 (P1 — makes every other bot idea ~200× cheaper to test) | 3-4h | Measured on 425 settled shadow picks: per-bet SD is **1.341 for ROI** and **0.090 for de-vigged Pinnacle CLV** — a 14.9× ratio, so **222× fewer bets** for the same precision. To pin a number to ±2% takes **17,259 bets on ROI and 78 on CLV**. And CLV is not a loose proxy: on 2,731 settled picks the quartiles run almost 1:1 with ROI (Q1 CLV −11.4% → ROI −11.1%; Q4 +17.1% → **+12.8%, t=+2.37**, the only statistically significant profitable slice anywhere in our data). **Action**: rank candidate configs on `clv_pinnacle` before ROI is even considered; a config needs a CLV bar to graduate to a shadow bot at all. This is the single highest-leverage process change available — a real 2% edge is invisible in ROI for years and obvious in CLV within weeks. It is also what would have caught FAVOURITE-BAND below before anyone bet on it. **Shipped 2026-08-26**: `/admin/shadow-bots` now gates on the **CLV** t-statistic (`gateT = hasClvGate ? clvTStat : tStat`, floor 100 picks-with-CLV rather than 200 settled, since 78 already gives ±2% at CLV's variance); ROI remains visible as a cross-check. Bots on markets Pinnacle does not quote fall back to the far slower ROI gate and are **labelled "no CLV anchor"** rather than silently judged on a weaker test. `scripts/clv_gate_report.py` ranks the whole fleet and flags disagreements. **The ranking immediately surfaced what nobody could see before**: `bot_v10_all` — active, `calibrated`, bankroll 1000 → **1,578.61** — is at n=480 real bets, **ROI +13.93% (t=+2.24)** and **CLV +4.82% (t=+5.27)**, positive in all four months (May +14.9 / Jun +6.2 / Jul +7.1 / Aug +35.8) and in both its markets. It is the only bot in the fleet with decisive positive evidence, and ROI alone could never have established that. Three disagreements also surfaced, all resolved in CLV's favour: `bot_dc_strong_fav` (+0.40% ROI vs −4.02% CLV, t=−21), `bot_ou25_global` (+1.98% ROI vs −3.05% CLV, t=−8.5), `bot_high_alignment` (−4.10% ROI vs +1.60% CLV). Smoke `CLV-FIRST-GATE`. |
| **META-MODEL-CLV-TARGET-2026-08-26** | ⚠️ Partly done 2026-08-26 — label fixed and shipped, RETRAIN BLOCKED on volume (P1 — the meta-model has been trained on a corrupted label) | 3-5h | `scripts/train_b_ml3.py --bets-mode` trains on `simulated_bets.clv_pinnacle`, which is the **raw, vig-inclusive** Pinnacle CLV — it carries the overround, so the zero point is off by ~2-4%. Worse, that column is computed via `get_pinnacle_closing_odds()`, whose fallback had **no kickoff cutoff** until `PIN-CLOSE-PRE-KO-FALLBACK-2026-08-26` (`2959c9d`): on any match where `is_closing` was never marked it returned the absolute-latest Pinnacle snapshot, which can be an **in-play tick**. So a share of the training labels were derived from live prices, not closing lines. Migration 283 added `simulated_bets.clv_pinnacle_devig` for the clean target but **it was never backfilled — 0 of 4,250 settled rows are populated** (my omission, 2026-08-26). **Done 2026-08-26**: backfilled 2,633 rows via `scripts/backfill_simulated_clv_devig.py`; `train_b_ml3.py --bets-mode` now selects `COALESCE(clv_pinnacle_devig, clv_pinnacle)`. **Measured improvement in label quality** — sorting the same 2,340 settled rows by each label: raw gives quintile ROIs +3.2 / −13.0 / −0.7 / +15.0 / +14.5 (**2/4 monotone**, and Q1 being POSITIVE while Q2 is −13% is the tell that its extreme-negative values are in-play garbage); de-vigged gives −1.9 / −3.6 / +2.9 / +9.0 / +12.5 (**4/4 monotone**), with the median split widening from +12.4/−4.8 to +13.4/−5.8. Also added a label sanity bound: **205 of 2,633 rows (7.8%) carried impossible CLV running to +397%**, all on longshots at odds 6.5–13.0 — a +397% CLV means the soft book offered 13.0 while de-vigged Pinnacle called it 38%, which is a data error, and left in they dominate a boosted fit. **RETRAIN NOT SHIPPED — blocked, and this is the real finding**: even at a 180-day window bets-mode yields only **436 rows against 44 features**, and walk-forward AUC is 0.78 / 0.77 / 0.88 / 0.85 / **0.53** — the last fold, which validates on the most recent data and is the only one resembling "how will this do next week", is a coin flip. Note also the trainer's headline `precision=1.000 recall=0.978` is computed by `_pick_threshold(model, scaler, X, y)` on the **full training set**; it is in-sample and is not evidence of anything. **Unblock is volume, not the label** — see CONSENSUS-ANCHOR-BOT. Smoke `META-MODEL-CLV-TARGET`. |
| **FAVOURITE-BAND-EXPERIMENT-2026-08-26** | ⬜ Filed 2026-08-26 (P2 — suggestive, NOT established; do not bet on it yet) | 2-3h | `scripts/book_bias_probe.py` found a clean favourite-longshot bias in the soft books: de-vigged probabilities too **short** below 25% (Betano −1.77pp t=−3.17) and too **long** above 50% (Betano +3.25pp t=+3.23, 888Sport +4.82pp t=+2.49). `scripts/favourite_band_probe.py` then tested whether it beats the vig at the best accessible price. **Read the history carefully, it is a lesson in itself**: 2026-06→08 gave **+8.59% (t=+2.10)** on the 75%+ band — but that was one favourable window, chosen after testing three bands, where the honest multiple-comparison threshold is nearer t≈2.4. The 2025-01→2026-06 out-of-sample came back **−6.43% (t=−2.53)**, the opposite sign. A clean split-half *within* the well-covered AF-Ultra era resolves it: **+4.49% (n=641)** and **+3.75% (n=649)**, pooling to **+3.87% ± 2.18%, t=1.78, 95% CI −0.4% to +8.1%** — consistent and positive but **not significant**. The older window is confounded: average best odds there were 1.200 vs 1.27 now, because far fewer books quoted, and line-shopping value scales with how many books you can compare. **Note the whole (small) effect is line-shopping, not the band**: ROI at the *typical* book is negative in every band (−0.6% to −6.0%); only the best-of-six price is positive. **Structural context worth acting on regardless**: 73% of every shadow pick we have ever made sits in the 25-50% implied band, where all six books are calibrated to within \|t\| < 1.4 — we are fishing where the market is correctly priced. Gate this on CLV per CLV-FIRST-DEV-LOOP before any money moves. |
| **COOLBET-OU-LINE-SHIFT-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 — root cause was a BRACKETED team-total market (P1 — real-money venue, and the ~11th time OU odds have been wrong) | 2-4h | `SHADOW-OU-EDGE-AUDIT` guarded the *consumption* of mislabelled OU lines but did not fix the *source*. Auditing 187 settled OU line-shop picks against Pinnacle's full ladder: Unibet 100% / Betano 100% / Marathonbet 100% / 888Sport 100% / 10Bet 91% of quotes match their stated line — **Coolbet only 65%, with 11 of 12 drifts landing on OU 3.5**. On picks labelled `over_under_25`/over, Coolbet averaged **1.96** where Pinnacle's 2.5 line averaged **1.60** and its 3.5 line averaged **2.44** — Coolbet's "over 2.5" is priced nearer a 3.0 total. `COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-GUARD-2026-08-22` cannot catch this: it rejects a *non-monotone* ladder, and a single uniformly shifted line stays internally monotone. Suspect Coolbet quoting quarter/Asian totals (2.75 / 3.0) that `coolbet_explorer.py` snaps to the nearest half line — cf. [[project_coolbet_limitations]] on AH quarter lines. **Why it matters:** Coolbet is the venue the operator actually places at, and this also inflates Coolbet's apparent miscalibration in the paired sharpness test (worst of 15 books, +0.003095 Brier vs Pinnacle, ~5× the next worst). **Root cause found 2026-08-26, and it is not a line shift.** Dumping Coolbet's live API for Grêmio Anápolis v Goianésia shows two market families carrying the SAME `line` values: `Total Goals Over / Under` (mtid **818**, in `_MTID_OU`, the real full-match ladder) and **`[Home] Total Goals` (mtid 1551, NOT in `_MTID_OU`)** — a single team's goals. The team-total market fell through to the `is_ou` name-fallback, matched `"total goals"`, and was written into the full-match OU slot at a colliding line. A single team going over 4.5 is far rarer than the match doing so, hence prices like **17.00 stored against Pinnacle's 4.19 (+306%)**. `_NON_GOALS_TOTAL_HINTS` already listed `"home team"`, `"away total"` etc. — but Coolbet's actual name is **bracketed**, matching none of them. Added `"[home]"`, `"[away]"`, `"[home "`, `"[away "`. **Verified live**: the parsed ladder for that match is now monotone and internally consistent — 0.5→1.12, 1.5→1.57, 2.5→2.63, 3.5→5.30, 4.5→11.20 (38% / 19% / 8.9% implied). **Third market-name collision in this family**, after COOLBET-COMBINED-MARKET-FILTER and COOLBET-HALF-MATCH-FILTER. **Correction to the original filing**: the "65% line match, 11 of 12 drifting to OU 3.5" figure came from a nearest-Pinnacle-line method that mis-attributes whenever Pinnacle does not quote that line at all — the fleet-level signal that held up is Coolbet sitting **+32% above Pinnacle on OU 4.5 and +12% on OU 3.5** while OU 1.5/2.5 were normal, which is exactly where a team total competes. Smoke `COOLBET-OU-LINE-SHIFT`. |
| **CONSENSUS-ANCHOR-BOT-2026-08-26** | ⬜ Filed 2026-08-26 (P2 — opens ~16% of matches + all of BTTS to line-shopping) | 4-6h | Pinnacle is treated as the required anchor everywhere, but that was an inherited assumption and it is **wrong**. Measured on 51,732 identical outcome rows (2026-05 → 08): a Shin-de-vigged **consensus** of the other books scores Brier −0.000047 vs Pinnacle alone, and the **leave-one-out** form — excluding the book being priced, which is the only version usable as a line-shop anchor since a consensus containing your own book is circular — still comes in at −0.000036. Every *individual* book is worse than Pinnacle (paired test, all 15), yet the *average* beats it: ordinary wisdom-of-crowds. **What this unlocks:** the 16.3% of matches with no Pinnacle price, and BTTS, which Pinnacle never quotes through API-Football (only 8 bet types; zero Pinnacle BTTS rows have ever existed). **It also reframes the two retired no-Pinnacle bots** — `bot_no_pin_home_v1` / `bot_no_pin_shadow_v1` anchored on the MODEL (average model edge **47.6%**, wildly overconfident), never on a market consensus, so "no-Pinnacle matches are unbettable" was never actually tested. Build a leave-one-out consensus-anchored shadow bot at the same 3% de-vigged floor and compare. Harness already exists: `scripts/anchor_comparison_backtest.py`, `scripts/lineshop_replay.py`. |
| **SHADOW-CLV-BOOKMAKER-FIX-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 (P0 — the only leading indicator we have is structurally fake) | 2-3h | `get_closing_odds()` (`settlement.py:437`) has **no bookmaker filter**: it takes the latest `is_closing` snapshot for the market/selection across all 13 books, and since they share a timestamp the winner is arbitrary (observed: 10Bet 77, Bet365 45, William Hill 28, no-closing-row 114). Meanwhile `odds_at_pick` on every line-shop shadow bot is the **max across accessible books** by construction. So `clv = max(13 books) / one_arbitrary_book − 1` is positive whether or not there is edge — which is exactly why the page showed +9 to +11% CLV next to negative ROI, a combination that should be impossible. Fix: bookmaker-filtered closing odds, de-vigged Pinnacle CLV as the primary validator. |
| **LINESHOP-SHIN-DEVIG-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 (P1 — manufactures edge on exactly the picks that lose) | 3-4h | `daily_pipeline_v2.py:4573` de-vigs Pinnacle proportionally (`true_prob = (1/odds) / overround`). Correct for 2-way OU; wrong in a known direction for 3-way 1X2, where the overround is concentrated on the longshot — proportional removal overstates longshot true probability and fabricates edge on draws and away dogs. Evidence: after PER-BOT-SWEEP-DEVIG-2026-08-24 `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` average odds jumped 3.06 → 4.32 and those picks returned −45%; pooled shadow picks at odds > 3.3 are n=98, ROI −11.2%, hit 22.4% vs 25.3% implied; the two draw bots are the two worst performers. Fix: Shin de-vig for 3-way, keep multiplicative for 2-way. Judge on **calibration**, not ROI — PER-BOT-SWEEP showed threshold-picking on backtest ROI is anti-predictive. |

At the observed per-bet return spread that is an SE of ±19%, so a genuinely break-even bot promotes ~4 times in 10. Every active bot currently sits at \|t\| < 0.7 (active-6 pooled: n=433, ROI +2.4% ± 6.4%). Replace the raw-ROI gate with a t-statistic gate and raise n. |
| **SHADOW-DISCRETION-BLEED-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 (P0 — real money, and a repeat finding) | 2h | `user_pick_marks` state=2 (bet placed) picks return **−18.2% (n=147)** against **+7.7% (n=415)** for picks the operator did not touch; reviewed-only sits between at −13.9% (n=97). Difference t ≈ 2.1. | **SHADOW-OU-EDGE-AUDIT-2026-08-26** | ✅ Done 2026-08-26 (P1 — OU mislabels have burned us ~10×) | 2-3h | The OU line-shop bots select on a de-vigged edge vs Pinnacle of 11-13% and the Pinnacle close **does not move toward them at all** (CLV vs Pinnacle close: 13.0% / 12.2%, ≈ equal to the selection edge). An edge that survives to kickoff is not a mispricing — it is a structural price difference, i.e. a different line, a stale feed, or a book that will limit. Given [[feedback_odds_quality_recurring]] and COOLBET-OU-LINE-MISLABEL-RCA, mislabelled lines are the prime suspect. Audit the winning offers before either OU bot is trusted. |

Audit of all shadow + simulated bets found **194 wrongly-voided rows across two independent causes**. **(1) Undocumented retroactive bulk void (143 rows).** An ad-hoc SQL UPDATE ran between **12:12:34 and 12:42:30 UTC on 2026-08-23** and voided every then-pending `bot_no_pin_home_v1` pick with `model_probability − 1/odds > 0.20` — exactly the `BOT-NO-PIN-MODEL-SANITY` rule committed at 12:02 UTC that morning (`0607c1f`). The criterion is exact: 143 voided rows span gap 0.205→0.410; zero pre-cutoff rows with gap > 0.20 escaped; zero voided rows with gap ≤ 0.20. Fløya's 12:42 pick survived only because it was written *after* the sweep — its own gap was 0.350, so it should have been blocked at source, but the guard was on GitHub only while the VPS ran 21 commits behind until `ENGINE-DEPLOY-2026-08-24`. Two tells it was ad-hoc, not code: it left `pnl` **NULL** (every void writer in the repo sets `pnl=0`), and there is no commit, queue entry or smoke test for it. 133 of the 143 sit on finished matches (8 games) and would have settled 20W/113L = **−57.8% ROI on €1,330** — the void *flattered* the bot (recorded −4.31%, true −11.52%), so the 2026-08-24 retirement is reinforced, not undermined. **(2) Postponement voids were a one-way door (57 shadow + 4 simulated).** `fix_stale_live_matches()` / `match_status_sweeper.py` void every pending bet when AF reports PST/CANC/SUSP/AWD/INT; when AF later reports FT the match flips to `finished` with a score and **nothing reopened the bets**. Piast Gliwice **1-1** Legia left 19 `double_chance 1X` picks each from `bot_dc_specialist`, `bot_dc_value`, `bot_dc_strong_fav` — 1X on a draw wins. Plus 4 `bot_v10_all` 1x2 rows in `simulated_bets` (2 winners), which touch bot bankroll. **Real-money exposure:** 10 voided picks were marked in `user_pick_marks`, **6 at `state=2` (bet placed)** — 5 of the 6 misreported (Västra Frölunda won; Lidingö, Galtabacks, Al Saqer, Grosseto lost; only FC Thun v Servette is a genuine void). **Shipped:** migration 282 adds `void_reason` to both bet tables and backfills `'quarantine'` for every `simulated_bets` void before 2026-07-01 (all documented May-June cleanups — INPLAY-O-QUARANTINE, OU pinnacle-cap, OU quality-fix; without this the first re-settler run would resurrect 300+ rows of deliberately-removed fake PnL). New `resettle_wrongly_voided_bets()` in `settlement.py` runs inside the 15-min `settle_ready` sweep: re-runs `settle_bet_result` over every void on a finished match and **writes only when the recomputed result stops being `void`**, so genuine AH/DNB pushes survive and the pass is idempotent (rehearsal: 194 repaired, 259 pushes untouched, PnL delta −272.53). `simulated_bets` repairs move `bots.current_bankroll` by the delta. Any repair Telegram-alerts — steady state is zero, so an alert is always actionable. Both deliberate-void writers now stamp `void_reason='postponed'`. `scripts/resettle_wrongly_voided_bets.py` gives CSV backup + `--dry-run`; pre-repair backup committed at `dev/active/void-integrity-backup-2026-08-24.csv` (816 rows). Migration 282 also adds the **`shadow_bets_unique`** view (one row per `bot_id,match_id,market,selection`, latest `pick_time`) — the 30-min refresh writes one row per `shadow_cohort` **by design** (BET-TIMING-MONITOR timing A/B), so the insert key is deliberately left alone, but raw row counts overstate n by ~16× against the n≥50 graduation gates (Esteghlal v Sepahan alone carries 31 identical rows). `/admin/shadow-bots` already dedupes client-side; the view gives engine-side analysis the same basis. Smoke `BET-VOID-INTEGRITY`. Full RCA: `dev/active/bet-void-integrity-2026-08-24-plan.md`. |

| **BOOKMAKER-LABEL-MISMATCH-2026-08-22** | ✅ Done 2026-09-02 — **root cause was neither hypothesis in the ticket, and the proposed fix would have left the bug live.** Not a cross-run cache (`best`/`best_bookmaker` are rebuilt per call) and not the outlier filter keeping an old best (Dafabet was never a candidate — it is not in `ACCESSIBLE_BOOKMAKERS`). The actual cause: the `odds_raw` query in `_load_today_from_db` had **no recency constraint**, so it returned every non-closing snapshot ever written for the fixture and `max()` picked the highest price *any* book showed at *any* time. Pruning does not bound it — `prune_odds_snapshots.py` only touches `status='finished'`, so scheduled fixtures keep ~15 days of history. Dandenong reproduced exactly: Betano 3.70 until 05:00, crashed to 2.82, and we stored "3.70 at Betano" at 05:08/05:14/05:44/06:15/06:44 while the best accessible price was 10Bet 3.10 — each 30-min refresh re-deriving the stale peak. **`bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` looked correct only because it uses a different query** with `DISTINCT ON … timestamp DESC`; the split is by pass age, not shadow-vs-prod, and `_run_no_pin_shadow_pass` + `_run_sweep_shadow_pass` carried the identical bug — those bots' signals are followed with real money ([[project_realmoney_shadow_signals]]). **It also defeated the outlier guard**: the anchor is `next(… if b == 'Pinnacle')` over an unordered scan, so it took a stale Pinnacle 2.81 (ceiling 3.79) and passed the 3.70 that live 2.73 would have capped at 3.69. **Fixed** all three queries to `DISTINCT ON (match_id, market, selection, bookmaker) … ORDER BY … timestamp DESC`, and documented the anchor's dependency on that recency at the anchor itself. **Scale:** 34.2% of 1X2 selections on scheduled fixtures had a stale higher price, mean +6.3%, worst 2.27×. Smoke `STALE-BEST-ODDS` locates the SELECT that actually reads `odds_snapshots` in each of the three functions (an earlier draft passed while the odds query was unguarded, because `_load_today_from_db` has three DISTINCT ONs) and pins `bookmaker` in the key plus `timestamp DESC`; each of the three verified to fail independently. Independently verified by a sub-agent before the fix ([[feedback_double_check_subagents]]). ANALYSIS_GOTCHAS #30. | 3-4h | Dandenong City vs Preston Lions (22 Aug): `bot_v10_all` + `bot_high_roi_global_v2` wrote Home @ 3.70 with `recommended_bookmaker=Betano` at 05:14, 05:44, 06:15, 06:44 UTC — but Betano crashed 3.70 → 2.82 at 05:00 UTC. Only Dafabet stayed at 3.70. Meanwhile `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` correctly refreshed to Home @ 3.10 book=10Bet after 05:00. So the two pipelines behave differently: v10/high_roi keep the pre-crash Betano label, pin_1x2_home refreshes. Hypotheses: (a) v10 pipeline caches `best_bookmaker` dict across runs, (b) ODDS-OUTLIER-FILTER-2026-08-18 rejects Dafabet@3.70 as outlier vs Pinnacle 2.73, but the pipeline keeps the OLD best (Betano when it was 3.70) rather than falling back to the next-best current price. **Diagnostic**: instrument `_load_today_from_db` to log `best[mid][key]` + `best_bookmaker[mid][key]` per key + timestamp; run against Dandenong odds_snapshots at 05:14 UTC. If Dafabet is filtered out but Betano isn't (because Betano's 2.82 doesn't trigger outlier), then the pipeline's "best" should be Betano@2.82 not the stale 3.70. Currently writing 3.70 suggests state leak. **Fix**: make sure `best_odds` and `best_bookmaker` are ALWAYS reset per pipeline run + both come from the SAME row's snapshot timestamp. Add smoke: pin that best_bookmaker is only written when best_odds is also updated (never one without the other). |

| **BOT-GRADUATION-GATES-TIERED-2026-08-22** | ⚠️ **RE-SCOPE OR CLOSE — the gate it describes no longer exists (verified 2026-09-02).** SHADOW-BOT-FIXES-2026-08-26 (7ea8623) replaced n≥50 + ROI≥3% with a **t-statistic gate**: `MIN_SETTLED_FOR_DECISION=200`, `PROMOTE_T/RETIRE_T=±1.65`, `MIN_DAYS_FOR_DECISION=14`, plus a CLV-anchored path on de-vigged Pinnacle CLV at `CLV_MIN_N=100` (`admin/shadow-bots/page.tsx:52-76,318-322`). The Monte-Carlo behind it (`scripts/promotion_gate_simulation.py`) shows the ticket's diagnosis was wrong: the problem was the *statistic*, not the sample size. What survives is narrower — volume tiering unimplemented, time floor still flat 14 days (ticket wanted 30/45), and Phase 2/3 (`check_bot_promotion_gates.py` cron, `promotion_gate_config` JSONB) do not exist. **Do not execute Phase 1 as written — its per-bot gate text contradicts the shipped gate.** | ⬜ Filed 2026-08-22 (P3 — superseded by PER-BOT-SWEEP-N500 for high-vol bots) | 1h to update PQ + 3-4h if we build a promotion-check cron | Current per-bot graduation gates in PRIORITY_QUEUE are uniform N-thresholds (e.g. `bot_no_pin_shadow_v1`: n≥50 settled AND ROI≥+3%). **Problem**: high-volume shadow bots blow past n=50 in 1-2 days. Concrete audit 2026-08-22 — daily unique-pick counts: `bot_sweep_ou25_v1` ~27, `bot_sweep_1x2_home_v1` ~34, `bot_no_pin_home_v1` ~30, `bot_sweep_ou35_v1` ~22, `bot_sweep_1x2_draw_v1` ~19, `bot_pin_1x2_home_v1` ~17. At those rates, n=50 settled ≈ 2-3 days of matches — no midweek variety, no European vs off-season rotation, single-weekend fixture-mix vulnerability. **Proposed tiered gate**: baseline (low-vol bots, <1 unique pick/day) = n≥50 AND ≥30 days in shadow AND avg CLV > 0% AND 95% bootstrap CI lower bound > −1%. High-vol bots (>1 unique pick/day) = n≥100 AND ≥45 days AND same CLV constraint. Rationale: (a) time floor forces 4-6 weekend cycles + midweek + league rotation, (b) higher N for high-vol compensates for lower per-pick diversity, (c) CLV CI (not point ROI) — OUT-OF-BETA-CUTOFF 2026-06-10 lesson was killing on ROI at low N with positive CLV is a systematic mistake. **Bots this immediately affects** (currently gated at n≥50 or n≥30): bot_no_pin_home_v1, bot_pin_1x2_home_v1, bot_sweep_1x2_home_v1, bot_sweep_1x2_draw_v1, bot_sweep_ou25_v1, bot_sweep_ou35_v1, bot_sweep_btts_yes_v1 (baseline OK — ~10/day), bot_no_pin_shadow_v1 (retired but referenced), bot_summer_specialist. **Scope of change**: (Phase 1, 1h) update each affected bot's PQ entry with the tiered gate; (Phase 2, 3-4h) `scripts/check_bot_promotion_gates.py` cron that queries settled counts + CLV CI per bot and Telegram-alerts on threshold crossings; (Phase 3, optional) migration to add `promotion_gate_config JSONB` column on `bots` so the gate is data-driven not prose. Phase 1 alone is enough to change agent behavior (next time an audit runs, it'll apply the new gate); Phase 2 automates. Depends on: nothing. **Do NOT bulk-apply until user confirms** — this is a policy call that affects 7+ bots' graduation timelines. |

That is textbook train/serve skew, and backfilling them raises the training-side coverage while inference stays at zero — **widening the skew, not closing it**. | **TELEGRAM-PAID-TIER-2026-08-18** | ⬜ Filed 2026-08-18, spec revised 2026-08-21 (P3 — three-stage trigger: build @50 subs, advertise @75, paid @100) | Build 6-10h · Advertise 30m · Paid launch 2h + Stripe test | Build a "premium picks" flow where paid subscribers get all Telegram picks + advanced signals via a private channel, and free subs get a rate-limited subset in @oddsintelpicks. **Three-stage triggers (2026-08-21):** (a) **@ 50 subs — start building.** Sub count is high enough that shipping this before growth stalls is worth the eng time. (b) **@ 75 subs — advertise it as coming soon.** Add "Pro tier launching soon — see /pricing" to the channel description + a badge on the /picks page. Builds anticipation, filters serious-buyer segment via mailing list interest. (c) **@ 100 subs — flip the paid tier live.** Enough denominator that a ~3-5% conv rate produces a real €30-50/mo signal, not noise. Current: **~29 subs** (2026-08-21). **Cheap intermediate now, no engineering:** update channel description to "Pro tier launching soon — all picks in real time + advanced signals" to plant the idea in the funnel. **Bulletproof build spec (fanciest version — for the 100-sub trigger):** **(1) Identity linking.** Use **Telegram Login Widget** on `/settings/telegram` for OAuth-quality identity handoff — user clicks button, we get their `telegram_id` + `first_name` + `username` via HMAC-signed payload from telegram.org, store `profiles.telegram_id` (already exists in schema). Fallback: one-time 8-char code DM'd to bot for users who don't have Telegram Login-linked account. **(2) Membership provisioning.** On `checkout.session.completed` + `customer.subscription.created` events: (a) call Telegram Bot API `createChatInviteLink` with `member_limit=1`, `expire_date=NOW()+3600`, store `invite_url` + `stripe_subscription_id` in new table `telegram_paid_memberships`. (b) Redirect success page shows the invite link + "click to join within 1 hour". (c) Bot listens on `chat_member` updates; when a user joins the paid channel, resolve their `telegram_id` → look up unclaimed invite → mark row `joined_at=NOW()` in `telegram_paid_memberships`. **(3) Membership revocation.** On `customer.subscription.deleted` OR `customer.subscription.updated` (where new tier is downgrade or `canceled`): (a) fetch `telegram_id` from `telegram_paid_memberships` for that subscription; (b) call `banChatMember(chat_id=paid_channel, user_id=telegram_id, until_date=NOW()+40)` (Telegram interprets `until_date < 30s in future` as a permanent ban but `+40s` gives a temporary kick that lets them re-join if they resub); (c) if they had one active invite that hadn't been used yet, call `revokeChatInviteLink` on it. Send them a bot DM "your subscription ended; your paid channel access has been paused. To resume, subscribe at /pricing". **(4) Chargeback + fraud handling.** On `charge.dispute.created`: immediately kick + revoke same as cancel. On `invoice.payment_failed` (3 retries fail): kick with a grace-period DM ("your payment failed 3× — access paused, update your card at /settings/billing"). **(5) Re-subscription flow.** If a previously-kicked user resubs, the same webhook triggers a fresh invite — no manual intervention. Track `resubscribed_at` in `telegram_paid_memberships` for analytics. **(6) Rate limits + safety.** Telegram Bot API allows ~30 messages/sec globally + 1 msg/sec per group. Queue paid-channel posts through the same queue as public (already ok at 30-sub volume, will need attention at 500+). Bot must be admin with "Invite Users via Link" + "Ban Users" permissions. **(7) Admin visibility.** New `/admin/paid-telegram` page: rows of {telegram_id, username, stripe_customer, subscription_status, joined_at, revoked_at, last_dm_sent}. Debug button "resend invite" per row. **(8) Metrics for tuning.** Track (a) invite → join rate (should be > 80%; if lower, invite window too short), (b) time-to-join, (c) invite-abandon rate (never joined + link expired). Log to PostHog. **(9) Copy + UX.** Success page after checkout: big invite button, secondary "or DM `/link ABC123` to @coolbet_t_bot" fallback. Bot DM template on subscribe: "Welcome! You now have Pro access to @oddsintelpicks_pro — all picks in real-time, no rate limit. Reply /help for commands." **(10) Migration + prep before build.** (i) manually create private channel `@oddsintelpicks_pro` in Telegram, add bot as admin with all 3 permissions; (ii) add `TELEGRAM_PAID_CHANNEL_ID` (numeric ID, not username — usernames can change) to `.env`; (iii) migration NNN: `CREATE TABLE telegram_paid_memberships (id, user_id, telegram_id, stripe_subscription_id, invite_url, invite_link_id, invite_expires_at, joined_at, revoked_at, resubscribed_at, revoke_reason)`. **Rollback**: env var `TELEGRAM_PAID_TIER_ENABLED=false` short-circuits the webhook Telegram calls (still processes Stripe) so a broken bot doesn't break Stripe. **Related**: /picks landing page CTA for the Telegram channel should be lifted higher (currently in footer) — organic pickup shows the offer resonates. **Attribution prereq**: before advertising (@75 subs), add UTM param `?utm_source=telegram&utm_campaign=paid_tier_launch` on the invite CTA so we can measure conversion from the free channel. |

| **CLV-AUTOVOID-2026-08-19** | ✅ Done 2026-08-19 | 45m | Post-settlement safety net: any settled bet where `odds_at_pick / closing_odds ≥ 1.65×` is auto-voided (result='void', pnl=0) with a CLV-AUTOVOID tag in reasoning. Catches data-error prices that slip past ODDS-OUTLIER-FILTER at placement (e.g. new bookmaker bug patterns, or filter regressions). Idempotent — the WHERE clause skips rows already tagged so re-running settlement doesn't double-tag. Uses `execute_write_returning` (not `execute_query` — per the 2026-06 COOLBET-INLINE-HEAL-BUTTONS lesson that `execute_query` doesn't commit). Wrapped in try/except in `run_settlement` — auto-void failure never blocks core settlement. **Historical retro-sweep note**: initial deploy caught 88 old bets (May-July, mostly pre-outlier-filter). Reversed those (`result → pending`, re-settled) because we couldn't confidently distinguish bad pick_odds from bad closing_odds capture on old rows (12 were on Pinnacle — sharp anchor — which strongly suggests closing_odds capture bug, not fantasy picks). Kept only the 4 recent manually-verified voids (Gremio-U20 Coolbet-swap, Sudtirol, Hapoel, Bognor). Auto-void is go-forward only from 2026-08-19. Smoke `CLV-AUTOVOID` pins threshold, execute_write_returning use, idempotency, won/lost-only filter, closing_odds guards, void+pnl=0 update. |
| **BOT-NO-PIN-SHADOW-2026-08-19 fallback-guard patch** | ✅ Done 2026-08-19 | 30m | Fix on top of Phase 1 (2026-08-18): the ensemble model returns a uniform (1/3, 1/3, 1/3) no-information fallback on leagues it has no coverage for — Slovak amateur cup, Japan Emperor Cup lower rounds, U-teams, Poland III Liga, Brazil Cearense U20, etc. Multiplying that 1/3 prior by long tail-book odds produced fake +100%+ "edges" on the 93 initial shadow picks. Added `_FALLBACK_UNIFORM_TOL=0.02` guard: if all three 1X2 probs are within 0.02 of 1/3, skip the match. Wiped the 93 garbage shadow rows so Phase 1 data collection restarts cleanly. Smoke `BOT-NO-PIN-SHADOW` updated to pin the fallback guard. Verdict on the "are these odds real?" audit user asked for: the **pick odds themselves are plausible** (Coolbet 5.25 on a Slovak amateur cup draw is a real thin-market price), but the **edge % was 100% fake** because the model has zero belief on those matches. Post-patch, only leagues with real ensemble coverage will trigger the shadow bot. |
| **BOT-NO-PIN-SHADOW-2026-08-18 Phase 1** | ✅ Done 2026-08-18 | 1.5h | New shadow-only bot `bot_no_pin_shadow_v1` for 1X2 markets Pinnacle doesn't quote. Fires when (a) ensemble model has 1X2 prob, (b) match has NO Pinnacle 1X2, (c) ≥3 accessible bookmakers quote the same selection (median = local anchor), (d) best-of-accessible odds ≤ 1.35× median (same outlier check as the ODDS-OUTLIER-FILTER), (e) edge ≥ 8% (higher than prod bots — compensates for unknown calibration on non-Pinnacle leagues). Writes to `shadow_bets` only — never touches `simulated_bets`, `real_bets`, or bankroll. New morning-cohort call in `run_morning` after acca, skipped when `shadow_mode=True`. Migration 271 registers bot with `maturity_label='experimental'`. Admin UI at `/admin/no-pin-shadow` (superadmin-only) — running ledger, hit rate, ROI, per-pick details. **Phase 2 trigger** (promote to paper beta writing simulated_bets): n≥50 settled AND ROI ≥ +3%. **Auto-kill**: ROI ≤ −8% at n≥50. Revisit target: **2026-10-01** (~6 weeks of data). Smoke `BOT-NO-PIN-SHADOW` pins function existence, hook, thresholds, migration. |
| **ODDS-OUTLIER-FILTER-2026-08-18** | ✅ Done 2026-08-18 | 1h | Recurring odds-quality issue: single soft/lazy books post stale or mislabelled 1X2/BTTS prices that get picked as "best odds" and inflate the reported edge %. Concrete example flagged 2026-08-18: `/picks` showed Hapoel Ramat Gan home win at Marathonbet 4.50 (+22% edge) while Coolbet/consensus was ~2.60 — 16 pp implied-prob gap on a low-tier Israeli Toto Cup fixture, not a real reachable price. Same class of problem as OU-PIN-REQUIRED (which capped OU rows at 2× Pinnacle) but for 1X2 / BTTS / DC. **Fix**: at the placement-layer aggregator (`_load_today_from_db`) a pre-pass now builds a consensus anchor per (match, market, selection) — Pinnacle odds if present, else median across ≥3 accessible-book offers — and the main aggregation loop rejects any non-Pinnacle offer whose odds exceed anchor × ceiling multiplier (1.35 for 1X2/DC, 1.30 for BTTS). Groups with no Pinnacle AND <3 accessible books are dropped entirely (no consensus to validate against). Reject count logged for operator visibility. Effect: obvious outliers no longer promoted to "best" odds; next-best accessible offer wins; reported ROI reflects prices a bettor could realistically reach. Smoke: `ODDS-OUTLIER-FILTER` (source-inspection — pins multipliers, min-books gate, Pinnacle exemption, reject counter). |

| **SCHEDULER-AF-429-DEADLOCK** | ✅ Done 2026-08-24 (filed 2026-07-16, P0) | 3-4h | **2nd multi-hour scheduler hang in 4 days.** Jul 12 07:00 UTC and Jul 15 07:00 UTC — both times, the process stayed alive but pipeline_runs froze for 24-33h. Root cause visible after 2026-07-16 restart: AF returns HTTP 429 `"Too many requests. You have exceeded the limit of requests per minute of your subscription"` on burst; some code paths raise (Stats/Events — see log lines 2026-07-16 20:28:10-11), others block **indefinitely** on retry-with-sleep loops. The Jul 15 hang caught 5 concurrent AF-touching jobs at 07:00 UTC (budget_sync, fetch_odds, odds_refresh, settle_ready, wc_bracket_slot_sync) — all deadlocked in `clock_nanosleep`. Daily quota is fine (0 / 150K after reset) — this is the **per-minute** limit on AF Ultra. **Fixes:** (a) hard `timeout=30` on every AF `requests.get` call — no exceptions; (b) max-retry cap (e.g. 3 attempts with exponential backoff, then RAISE not sleep); (c) APScheduler `misfire_grace_time` + `max_instances=1` per job so a hung instance doesn't queue clones; (d) install `py-spy` on VPS so the next hang gets a real stack trace not just "clock_nanosleep"; (e) rate-limiter on the AF client itself — Ultra is ~450 req/min, currently bursting way past that on startup. Smoke should pin: `AF_TIMEOUT_S` constant in af client, max-retry constant, an existence test for `misfire_grace_time` on the scheduler config. Related to but distinct from SCHEDULER-PORT-8080-HARDEN — that's the crash-loop case, this is the hang case. Both need to be fixed. |
| **SCHEDULER-PORT-8080-HARDEN** | ⬜ Filed 2026-07-13 (P1) | 1-2h | 2026-07-13 incident: orphan `python workers/scheduler.py` from Jul 09 cutover held port 8080 for ~4 days, then hung Jul 12 07:00 UTC without restarting → 33h of no pipeline_runs before the ops dashboard surfaced it. **Additional finding**: a duplicate systemd unit (`odds-scheduler.service`, older-name pre-rename) was crash-looping on the same port every 15s until it was masked. Two fixes: **(a)** `workers/scheduler.py:_start_health_server()` (around line 2403) should set `socket.SO_REUSEADDR` before bind + catch bind errors gracefully (log + skip health server, don't crash the whole scheduler); **(b)** add `ExecStartPre=/usr/bin/fuser -k 8080/tcp \|\| true` to `/etc/systemd/system/oddsintel-scheduler.service` so a stray process on the port gets nudged off on restart. Also delete/rename the old `odds-scheduler.service` file properly (currently renamed to `.disabled-20260713` — safe but ugly). Smoke should pin: (i) SO_REUSEADDR set in `_start_health_server`; (ii) no `odds-scheduler.service` file in `deploy/` if we track it; (iii) `ExecStartPre` line present in the systemd unit if we track it. Root cause of today's mess was the manual-run pattern from cutover + no protection against port conflict on restart. |

### Soccer Volume — Every-Day-Picks Push (filed 2026-07-08) — grow pick volume through summer

> Diagnosis 2026-07-08: 11 currently-active soccer bots, but 7 target leagues currently on summer break (top-5 Europe, UK, Greek+Turkish). Real-day production is ~1 pick from `bot_v10_all` + a handful from global-scope bots. European seasons restart mid-August → volume 2-3× naturally at that point. Meanwhile, we need pick density through July / early August. Shadow-bets audit today confirmed retired bots stay retired (all showed clean negative ROI on the full production pipeline with Pinnacle veto + calibration).

| **AH-BOT-POSTMORTEM** | ✅ Done 2026-06-25 | 30m | **Diagnosis at `dev/active/ah-bot-postmortem-2026-06-25.md`.** Loss isn't uniform — it's concentrated on the "favorite covers" lines. Per-selection breakdown of bot_ah_home_fav's 132 settled bets: home -0.5 (n=76, -15.81%), home -1 (n=20, -5.02%), home +0/DNB (n=11, -28.78%), home -1.5 (n=6, -35.19%), home -2 (n=4, -100%). The "+X" selections all WIN (n=24 total, all positive). **Root cause**: model passes raw `p(home moneyline win)` as the AH probability instead of computing per-line `P(cover X)` from the Poisson lambdas. P(home covers -1) ≠ P(home wins) — needs win-by-2+ plus half-refund on win-by-1. Same overconfidence flips POSITIVE on +X selections because the AH line forgives the model's error. **Fixes documented but not shipped (~1-2 weeks)**: per-line P(cover X) marginals; per-handicap-line Platt fits (not one aggregate AH key); AH CLV indexing bug (clv_pinnacle NULL on all 132 AH bets because odds_snapshots doesn't index Pinnacle AH by handicap_line). **Decision**: stay retired. OU 2.5 gap vs Forebet (-25pp) is a bigger next-priority issue. Filed as **AH-PER-LINE-CALIBRATION** (P3, future) and **AH-CLV-INDEXING-BUG** (orthogonal, next CLV audit). |

| **INPLAY-P-V2-BAYES-XG** | ✅ Done 2026-06-24 | 30m | **Bayesian Gamma posterior update applied to `_check_strategy_p_v2`.** Gate passed (INPLAY-P-V2-100-BET-CHECK): n=112 settled at +5.28% ROI / hit 29.5% / avg odds 3.41. Per-bucket: 2.00-2.99 -8.0% (n=15, small sample), **3.00-3.99 -14.7% (n=59, real weakness)**, 4.00-4.99 +41.6% (n=38, regressed from +81%/n=20 toward mean but still strongly positive). Bucket-3 weakness consistent with under-correcting the leading team's residual scoring rate when both teams have scored once by minute 30-75. Applied s=2 Gamma update (`P_PRIOR_STRENGTH = 2.0`) — same pattern as inplay_i / Strategy J. Posterior λ = (pm_xg × s + actual_goals) / (s + minute_frac), then scaled to remaining minutes for the bivariate Poisson. Now the 1-1 score state at minute 60 actually informs the posterior rather than the lambdas being treated as if the match hadn't started. Next gate: ≥100 settled bets AFTER 2026-06-24 → fit Platt via `scripts/fit_platt_inplay_p_v2.py` (clone of `fit_platt_inplay_e.py` with market_key='inplay_p_v2_1x2_home'/'_away'). ETA ≈ 2026-08-15 at current cadence. Smoke `INPLAY-P-V2-BAYES-XG` pins the function tag + prior strength + posterior assignments + goals_eq usage. |

The 30-day clock restarts from that fix — earliest clear date **2026-10-02**. **(3) 30-day P0 count = 0 — FAILS, badly.** The ticket's own test is "the bug-discovery rate has slowed enough". 2026-09-02 alone surfaced STALE-BEST-ODDS, SHADOW-BOTS-DETAIL-TRUNCATION (24 of 41 bot pages aggregating as little as 1.6% of their ledger), COMP-FALLBACK-DRIFT (a competitor published at the wrong sign for two months), plus EPICBET-403 and the shadow-bot truncation the day before. That is the opposite of a bending discovery curve. **Verdict: do not flip the beta flag.** Re-evaluate no earlier than 2026-10-02, and only if no new P0-class finding lands in the interim. The encouraging half is that the *performance* gate now passes on honest prices — the blocker is engineering confidence, not the model. | Active-bot maturity spread as of 2026-09-02: **beta 13 · experimental 6 · calibrated 4 · active 1**. Note the lone `active` bot — MOVE-ACTIVE-TO-BETA deprecated that label, so one bot was missed by that migration. | **Target cutoff: 2026-07-10 (optimistic) or 2026-09-01 (realistic).** When the bug-discovery rate has slowed enough that we can confidently present a "live cohort" ROI without hiding losses. **NEVER delete historical bets** — they're training data, evidence of due diligence, and the answer to "how do we know your bots aren't curve-fit?". Instead, **segment the display** with two cohorts always visible side-by-side.<br><br>**Three hard pre-conditions (ALL must be true before flipping the beta flag):**<br>1. Last known major fix active for **≥30 days** (today's blockers: PIN-CROSS-DRIFT-ACTIVATE + B-ML3-VALIDATE-ACTIVATION, both targeting 2026-06-10 → earliest pre-condition clear date is 2026-07-10).<br>2. Live cohort: **ROI ≥ +2% on N ≥ 500 settled bets** AND **CLV ≥ +1%** (CLV gate proves it's not luck — the model still beats closing odds in the new regime).<br>3. **30-day P0 count = 0** new P0 entries in PRIORITY_QUEUE. Pattern check: silent-failure traps have surfaced roughly once a month (InplayBot UUID 11-day bug, OU odds quality recurring, COOLBET-NO-MARKET-PRESENCE, SIM-BETS-COHORT-CONSTRAINT, NULL-BOOKMAKER-AH-FIX, PIN-CROSS-DRIFT). Two consecutive months of zero P0s = the discovery curve has bent enough.<br><br>**Why 2026-07-10 is the natural date** (if pre-conditions hold):<br>- 2026-06-07: Phase 4 self-use validation verdict (real-money viability of v20260524_market model) + Sunday weekly_retrain (Batch 1.5 mid-cycle training expansion check)<br>- 2026-06-10: B-ML3 activates + PIN-CROSS-DRIFT activates (both currently in shadow)<br>- 2026-06-15: Batch 2 per-bot timing — likely tightens 2-3 bot configs<br>- 2026-06-10 → 2026-07-10: 30-day window for new configs to bed in<br>The story is "we shipped v2 model + cross-market drift filter; here's the cohort from launch onward" — not arbitrary marketing.<br><br>**Implementation (frontend, no data wipe):**<br>1. Track-record + homepage hero: lead with `"Live performance — since v2 launch on 2026-07-XX"` (N bets, ROI, CLV, bots active). Compute by filtering `simulated_bets.created_at >= '2026-07-XX'` AND `model_version >= 'v20260524_market'`.<br>2. Secondary card / details tab: `"All-time including beta"` with full history since 2026-04-27. Always visible — never hidden — so we can't be accused of suppressing losses.<br>3. Add a `is_v2_cohort` derived column or a UI-side filter; do NOT drop or archive any rows.<br>4. Bot-list views: same pattern — "since v2" headline, "all-time" badge with the full history.<br><br>**What "out of beta" means concretely** (the flip changes user-facing behaviour, not the engine):<br>- Drop the "beta" label on the homepage<br>- Public marketing push (REDDIT_LAUNCH next phases, SEO content, paid ads — see LAUNCH_PLAN.md)<br>- Confidence to be more aggressive on free→Pro conversion CTAs (current copy is intentionally gentle because we're not sure of the numbers)<br>- `MODEL_WHITEPAPER.md` gets a "Published version 1.0" stamp instead of "Living document"<br>- Track-record page headline changes from "Performance to date (beta)" to "Live performance since 2026-07-XX"<br><br>**Mini-check on 2026-06-10** (alongside PIN-CROSS-DRIFT-ACTIVATE): compute "what would the headline ROI be if we started counting from 2026-05-21?" (post AH-HOME-BIAS + SIM-BETS-COHORT fixes). If that 21-day pre-window is already +2%+, the July date is realistic. If it's still noisy/negative, push to September and re-evaluate after Batch 2. <br>**Ran 2026-06-10 — verdict: PUSH to 2026-09-01.** 21-day window (2026-05-21 → 2026-06-09) yielded n=1,745 settled bets at **ROI -0.65%** (PnL -$62.32 on $9,526 staked). Both gates fail: Gate 1 (ROI ≥ +2%) fails at -0.65%; Gate 2 (CLV ≥ +1%) fails — only 33% of bets have CLV computed, beat rate is 25% (model behind closing on 3/4 bets). However, week-over-week trend is improving: week-of-05-18 -2.25% → 05-25 -0.90% → 06-01 +0.70% → 06-08 partial +9.80% (n=86, too thin to anchor on). Hypothesis: recent fixes (AH-HOME-BIAS 05-21, SIM-BETS-COHORT, NULL-BOOKMAKER-AH-FIX 06-03, v20260524_market model) are starting to bite but haven't accumulated enough weight in the rolling window. Re-run this mini-check **after Batch 2 (2026-06-15)** and again **after WC accumulates traffic (2026-06-25)**. If by 2026-07-01 the rolling 21-day window is consistently ≥ +1.5% with n ≥ 700, advance the cutoff target to 2026-08-01; otherwise hold 2026-09-01.<br><br>**Honest caveat**: you can't be "dead sure" no new bugs will surface — even Renaissance finds bugs. The pre-conditions above just establish that the discovery rate has slowed to the point where surprise losses are bounded. Have a rollback plan: if the live cohort goes ≥-3% ROI over a 14-day rolling window post-launch, revert the homepage to "beta" framing and investigate before re-flipping.<br><br>**Fallback gate — 2026-09-01**: if 2026-07-10 fails any pre-condition, set this as the next target. Repeats the same check loop monthly until all three conditions hold. |

Use `dedup_key=f"user-bet-{bet_id}"` with a 10-min window to protect against pipeline retries double-notifying.<br><br>**6 — User notification format**: `🔔 New value bet\n{home} vs {away}\n{market} {selection} @ {odds:.2f}\n{edge:+.1f}% edge · {league}`. Keep it clean — no alignment class, no bot name. Elite users get one extra line: `📊 {N} bots agree` (consensus count).<br><br>**Env vars needed**: `NEXT_PUBLIC_TELEGRAM_BOT_USERNAME` (for the deep link), `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (for webhook auth). | **PINNACLE-COHORT-FILTER** | ⬜ P3 · **blocker is misstated (verified 2026-09-02)** | research | The parked reason — "pending better in-play Pinnacle coverage" — is the wrong blocker for the design actually described, which is a **prematch** delayed-placement filter. Separately, in-play Pinnacle coverage is not "thin" but **exactly zero — 0 of 695,708 live rows, ever**, so it is never going to improve on its own. Signal re-measured at live prices (120d): survives but shrinks, **3 of 5 prematch bots not 6 of 8** — `bot_v10_all` +13.06% narrowed vs +2.89% widened (was +36.6 vs +9.1), `bot_aggressive` +6.00% vs −4.83%; inverts on `bot_lower_1x2` and `bot_opt_home_lower`. Rewrite the blocker line rather than leaving it parked. | **Tradeable signal pending better inplay Pinnacle coverage.** Per-bot Pinnacle cohort decomposition (2026-06-03, 60d) showed that for 6 of 8 bots, picks where Pinnacle's line moves *against* our pick by close (edge narrows) have markedly better ROI than picks where Pinnacle moves *toward* our pick (edge widens). bot_v10_all: narrowed +36.6% (n=59) vs widened +9.1% (n=17), a +27pp lift. bot_aggressive: +10.3% vs -9.8%, +20pp. The cleanest implementation would be a delayed-placement filter — pick at T-0, wait N minutes, check if Pinnacle has moved against us, only place if it has. But this requires reliable Pinnacle inplay snapshots which we currently lack. **Revisit** once Pinnacle live-odds ingestion is robust (or experiment with API-Football live Pinnacle if available). Per-bet data for replication: `dev/active/per-bot-clv-60d-pinnacle.csv`. |

| **INPLAY-E-ECE-RECHECK** | ✅ Done 2026-06-03 | 30m | **inplay_e is catastrophically miscalibrated despite being labelled `calibrated`.** Re-check on all 216 settled bets: **ECE = 0.2193 (21.93%) vs 5% gate — 4.4× over.** Avg predicted prob 0.797 vs actual hit rate 0.583 (+21.4pp over-confident).
