The miscalibration matters because downstream consumers (admin confidence displays, meta-model features, any future Kelly sizing) consume garbage. **Recommendation**: do NOT demote (it's +EV); DO file INPLAY-E-RECALIBRATE (P1) — fit Platt on historical bets, store calibration row, re-check ECE post-fit. **Critical for Sunday 2026-06-07 CHERRY-PICK-PLACER Phase 3**: confirm the placer doesn't gate on raw model_probability (currently uses pre-computed edge_percent, appears safe). Full writeup: `dev/active/inplay-e-ece-recheck-2026-06-03.md`. Re-runnable via `scripts/inplay_e_ece_recheck.py`. |
| **INPLAY-E-RECALIBRATE** | ✅ Done 2026-06-03 (shadow mode) | 2h | **Platt sigmoid fitted + wired in shadow mode.** Followup from INPLAY-E-ECE-RECHECK. Fit results: 216 settled bets, ECE 21.93% → **4.02% LOO out-of-sample (under 5% gate)**, in-sample post-fit 8.92%. Calibration params: a=2.909726, b=-1.976138 stored as `model_calibration.market='inplay_e_under_25'`. Mapping: raw 0.80 → calibrated 0.587, raw 0.90 → calibrated 0.655 (matches the empirical 65% hit rate at the high-conviction end). **Implementation:** (1) `scripts/fit_platt_inplay_e.py` — one-shot fit script reusing fit_platt's machinery + leave-one-out CV. (2) `workers/jobs/inplay_bot._check_strategy_e` — computes both `raw_model_prob` and `cal_model_prob = apply_platt(raw, 'inplay_e_under_25', odds)` on every fire. Edge gate uses `cal_model_prob` when `INPLAY_E_PLATT_ENABLED=true` else `raw_model_prob` (default → shadow). Both probs stored in `extras` regardless of env, so we can validate post-hoc which cohort would have been better. (3) 3 smoke tests guard the fit reuse, the wire-in pattern, and the apply_platt key resolution. **Followup gate**: PIN-CROSS-DRIFT-style — after ~7 days of shadow trail, query bets where `reasoning ->> 'extra' ->> 'cal_model_prob'` differs from raw, compare hit-rate of "would-have-fired with raw but not with cal" cohort. If that cohort loses money, flip `INPLAY_E_PLATT_ENABLED=true` on Railway. Filed as INPLAY-E-PLATT-ACTIVATE (P1, target ~2026-06-10). |

| COMBO-RESEARCH-PHASE-B | P2 | 1-2 days | **Joint probability model** for highly-correlated event pairs. Trivial for BTTS + O/U (Poisson already gives match-level goal distribution → directly compute P(both teams score AND >2.5 goals)). Medium for team-win + selection (needs joint scoreline distribution beyond independent Poisson — Dixon-Coles correction helps). Output: `compute_joint_prob(match_id, leg1, leg2)`. Gated on Phase A finding that Coolbet mispricies SGMs. |
| COMBO-RESEARCH-PHASE-C | P2 | 2-3 days | **SGM paper-trading bot.** Enumerates SGM offers from Coolbet for the day's matches, scores against the Phase B joint probability, generates paper bets where edge ≥ threshold. Run for 200+ settled bets to measure CLV vs single-bet CLV (the question: does the correlation edge survive execution?). Gated on Phase B. |
| COMBO-RESEARCH-PHASE-D | ✅ Done 2026-05-17 | 1 day | **Cross-match acca bot shipped.** Phase A confirmed Coolbet doesn't compound acca margin (3-fold combo / product ratio = 0.9999). Kelly stake at 1/3 the singles' Kelly fraction (combo variance protection). Settlement: new `settle_combo_bet` in settlement.py aggregates leg outcomes (all-win → won at product odds, any-lose → lost, mixed voids → won at reduced odds). Pipeline hook: `run_acca_pass()` runs at end of `run_morning()` for the morning cohort. Backtest in `scripts/backtest_acca_sizes.py` informs config. First 30-60 settled combos are the real validation — backtest sample (14 days) is too thin to trust the +151%/+193%/+504% ROI on 4-5-6 leg combos. Smoke: COMBO-ACCA-BOT-PRESENT, COMBO-SETTLE-COMBO-BET, COMBO-JOINT-PROB-MATH. |

| ODDSPORTAL-CUSTOM-SCRAPER | P3 | 2-3 days | **Write a custom OddsPortal scraper to replace OddsHarvester.** OddsHarvester is too slow for bulk historical collection: 50 matches × 12 markets takes 15+ min because it uses Playwright to visit every individual match page. A custom scraper should work at the listing-page level (OddsPortal results pages load ~50 matches in ~6s via internal API calls) and intercept the XHR/fetch calls the browser makes to populate odds. Target: 1x2 + BTTS + OU2.5 + AH closing odds for all 31 leagues × 6 seasons in ≤2-3 hours total. Approach: open browser devtools on OddsPortal results page → inspect Network tab → identify the JSON endpoints that serve odds data → replicate with requests + session cookies (no Playwright needed). Known risk: OddsPortal may rotate API keys or require auth tokens tied to the browser session. Fallback if API not viable: scrape only listing pages (gives 1x2 opening/closing for top 2-3 bookies per match, very fast) — enough for CLV analysis. Blocked on: nothing. Do when ready to invest 2-3 days. |

Edge filters at the end (≥3% G, ≥2% H, ≥3% J) unchanged — the relaxation only opens the candidate pool so we can validate the thesis with real bets. Tighten back if 50+ bets land at negative ROI. Smoke: INPLAY-LOOSEN-SILENT. I/L/Q left alone — I fires (1/14d) and is just inherently narrow; L has a goal-event-detection issue that needs deeper investigation; Q is rare by design (red-card matches). |

AF 1X2 percentages still win for home/draw/away (they blend form + H2H + standings — stronger than xG alone); OU 1.5/2.5/3.5/4.5, BTTS, and AH are now model-priced. `exp_home/exp_away` are set so AH bots (`bot_ah_home_fav`, `bot_ah_away_dog`) — previously stuck at zero candidates for Tier C — can fire. Old hardcoded prior is kept as a defensive fallback when AF gives 1X2 but no xG. Critically: `DATA_TIER_EDGE_BUMP["C"] = 0.08` kept unchanged — safety margin still applies. Quick check: `_poisson_probs(1.7, 1.2)` gives over_25_prob=0.554 (vs the previous flat 0.50). Smoke: TIER-C-AF-XG. |

| BOT-OU15-INVESTIGATE | ❌ Superseded 2026-05-25 by BOT-OU15-DIAGNOSE-CLOSE (migration 129 — bot retired with full silent-period evidence) | 1-2h | **Why did calibration kill OU 1.5 edge specifically?** The funnel diagnostic showed 97-98/104 candidates drop at `↓edge` — not at filters or odds range — meaning calibrated prob is tracking bookmaker implied very tightly for OU 1.5. Root cause: the May 7-8 calibration tightening (VIG-REMOVE shrank vig-adjusted implied probabilities, DRAW-PER-LEAGUE shifted probability mass from 1x2 toward draw, H2H-SPLITS adjusted team-strength priors) collectively compressed the model's OU 1.5 probability output toward true market pricing. OU 1.5 'over' is a high-baseline event (~75-80% implied); after vig removal the bookmaker implied for 'over 1.5' already sits near the model's pre-calibration estimate, leaving near-zero edge. Unlike OU 2.5/3.5 where the model's goal-distribution gives real information, OU 1.5 is nearly a certainty-level bet — the market is already efficient at 1.30-1.60 odds. **Viable re-enable paths**: (1) Wait for model retraining to establish a persistent edge ≥3% in shadow_bets over 2+ weeks — the retirement recovery criterion already captures this. (2) Segment by league: low-scoring defensive leagues (e.g. Serie A, Ligue 1) may still show OU 1.5 under edge even at tight thresholds — worth investigating when shadow data accumulates. (3) Focus on the 'under 1.5' side at odds 3.00-3.50 where the market is less efficient. **Conclusion: retirement is correct given current evidence.** The 3-year +89% backtest ROI has look-ahead bias and can't be trusted. The live shadow run with relaxed thresholds recovered 0/104 — a definitive signal that no threshold tuning rescues this bot at current calibration. Do not re-enable until shadow_bets show ≥30 bets at ≥3% ROI over a sustained week. |

Key considerations: (1) **Edge matching**: inplay bots compute edge from live Coolbet odds at fire time; the live Coolbet odds drift fast — placer must re-fetch live price and compare to `simulated_bets.edge_percent` before recording (same logic as pre-match `edge_eroded` check). (2) **Time window**: inplay bets are only valid for a narrow window (~2-3 minutes after fire); `placed_at` on `simulated_bets` is the fire time — skip if older than N minutes. (3) **Market resolution**: inplay 1x2 markets on Coolbet use the same `fo-match` / `sidebets` endpoints as pre-match but the market state changes (suspended during goals). Need `search_blocked` handling as today. (4) **Auto-trigger**: `inplay_bot.py` already calls `send_telegram()` on every bet — wire `_run_coolbet_record(inplay=True)` immediately after the inplay Telegram in `inplay_bot.py` (not in `betting_pipeline.py`).
